[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2018-108

P.C. 2018-602 2018-05-29
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Agriculture and Agri-Food and the Minister of Health
with respect to the provisions of the annexed Regulations other than
sections 391 and 408, and on the recommendation of the Minister of
Justice with respect to that section 391 and of the Minister of Finance
with respect to that section 408, makes the annexed [Safe Food for
Canadians Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-108) pursuant to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} section 12 of the [Canadian Dairy Commission
  Act](/eng/acts/C-15)[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-1756-E_hq_19018){#fn_81000-2-1756-E_hq_19018-ID0EBBIBA};

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-843679 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in these Regulations.

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Safe Food for Canadians
    Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the body of a dead
    animal. ([carcasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[catch-weight food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[catch-weight food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a food that because
    of its nature cannot normally be portioned to a predetermined fixed
    quantity and is, as a result, usually sold in containers of varying
    quantities. ([aliment à poids variable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[close proximity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[close proximity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an item of
    information that is shown on a label, means immediately adjacent to
    the item of information and without any intervening printed, written
    or graphic material. ([à proximité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commercially sterile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commercially sterile]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in section B.27.001 of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([stérilité
    commerciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[common name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a food, means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the food printed in boldface type,
      but not in italics, in a section of the Standards of Identity
      Document;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the food printed in boldface type,
      but not in italics, in a section of the Food Compositional
      Standards Document; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the name by which it is
      generally known or a name that is not generic and that describes
      the food. ([nom usuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Compendium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Compendium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the document entitled
    Canadian Grade Compendium, prepared by the Agency and published on
    its website, as amended from time to
    time. ([Recueil]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[consumer prepackaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[consumer prepackaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a food,
    means packaged in a container in the manner in which the food is
    ordinarily sold to or used or purchased by an individual --- or in
    which the food may reasonably be expected to be obtained by an
    individual --- without being repackaged, to be used for
    non-commercial purposes. ([de consommation
    préemballé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an outer receptacle or
    covering that is used or to be used in connection with a food. It
    includes a wrapper and a confining band but does not include a
    conveyance or any container that is an integral part of a
    conveyance. ([contenant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[contaminated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[contaminated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a food, means
    that the food contains any micro-organism, chemical substance,
    extraneous material or other substance or thing that may render the
    food injurious to human health or unsuitable for human consumption,
    including those that are not permitted under the [Food and Drugs
    Act](/eng/acts/F-27) or those that do not comply with any limits or
    levels provided under that Act. ([contaminé]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[dairy product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dairy product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means milk or a food that is
    derived from milk, alone or combined with another food, and that
    contains no oil and no fat other than that of milk. ([produit
    laitier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Descriptive Words, Expressions and Identification Names Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Descriptive Words, Expressions and Identification Names
    Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the document entitled
    Descriptive Words, Expressions and Identification Names for Specific
    Foods, prepared by the Agency and published on its website, as
    amended from time to time. ([document sur les mentions descriptives
    et noms d'identification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[dress]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[dress]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to dress a carcass in accordance
    with section 145. ([habiller]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[drug]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 2 of
    the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27). ([drogue]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[egg]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[egg]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an egg of a domestic chicken of
    the species [Gallus domesticus]{.otherLang lang="la"} or, in respect
    of a processed egg product, means that egg or an egg of a domestic
    turkey of the species [Meleagris gallopavo]{.otherLang lang="la"}.
    It does not include a balut. ([œuf]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[egg carton]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[egg carton]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a package that is capable
    of being closed and of containing not more than 30 eggs in separate
    compartments. ([boîte à oeufs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eviscerate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eviscerate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the carcass of a bird, other than
      an ostrich, rhea or emu, to remove the respiratory, digestive,
      reproductive and urinary systems, with or without the kidneys, and
      the other thoracic and abdominal organs; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any other carcass, to remove the
      respiratory, digestive, reproductive and urinary systems, except
      the kidneys, and the other thoracic and abdominal
      organs. ([éviscérer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes shellfish, crustaceans and
    other marine animals, and any of their parts, products and
    by-products. ([poisson]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 2 of
    the [Food and Drugs
    Act](/eng/acts/F-27). ([aliment]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[food additive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[food additive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([additif
    alimentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[food animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[food animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bird or mammal, other
    than a marine mammal, from which an edible meat product may be
    derived. ([animal pour alimentation humaine]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[Food Compositional Standards Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Food Compositional Standards Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
    the same meaning as in subsection B.01.001(1) of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870). ([Document sur les
    normes de composition des aliments]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[foreign state]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[foreign state]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a [[WTO
    Member]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of the
    [World Trade Organization Agreement Implementation
    Act](/eng/acts/W-11.8). ([État étranger]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[game animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[game animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a wild ruminant, pig or
    bird --- including a ruminant, pig or bird that lives in an enclosed
    territory under conditions of freedom similar to those of wild
    animals --- that is a food animal and that is hunted for commercial
    use under an authorization issued by a competent
    authority. ([gibier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hermetically sealed package]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hermetically sealed package]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a package
    that, due to its design, is secure against the entry of
    micro-organisms, including spores. ([emballage hermétiquement
    scellé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence that is issued under
    paragraph 20(1)(a) or (b) of the Act. ([licence]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[livestock carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[livestock carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a beef carcass,
    bison carcass, ovine carcass or veal carcass. ([carcasse de
    bétail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[maple product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[maple product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a food that is obtained
    exclusively by the concentration of sap from trees of the genus
    [Acer]{.otherLang lang="la"} or the concentration of maple
    syrup. ([produit de l'érable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[meat product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[meat product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the carcass of a food
    animal, the blood of a food animal or a product or by-product of its
    carcass or any food that contains the blood of a food animal or a
    product or by-product of its carcass. It does not include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} gelatin, bone meal, collagen casing, hydrolyzed
      animal protein, monoglycerides, diglycerides or fatty acids; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any food that contains a meat product in an
      insignificant quantity, having regard to the nature of the food
      and of the meat product. ([produit de viande]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[organic product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[organic product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a food commodity that
    has been certified as organic under subsection 345(1) or certified
    as organic by an entity accredited by a foreign state that is
    referred to in subparagraph 357(1)(a)(ii). ([produit
    biologique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ornamental container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ornamental container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a container that,
    except on the bottom, does not bear any advertising material, other
    than a trademark or common name, and that, because of any design
    appearing on its surface or because of its shape or texture, is sold
    both as a decorative item and as the container of a
    food. ([contenant décoratif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[poultry carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[poultry carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the carcass of a
    turkey, duck, goose, guinea fowl or bird of the species [Gallus
    domesticus]{.otherLang lang="la"}. ([carcasse de
    volaille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[prepackaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prepackaged]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a food, means
    packaged in a container in the manner in which the food is
    ordinarily sold to or used or purchased by a person, and includes
    consumer prepackaged. ([préemballé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[President]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[President]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the President of the
    Agency. ([président]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[principal display panel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[principal display panel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged food whose
      container is mounted on a display card, the part of the label that
      is applied to one or both of the following:

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all or part of the principal display surface,
        or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all or part of the surface of the display card
        that is displayed or visible under customary conditions of sale
        or use;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged food whose
      container is an ornamental container, the part of the label that
      is applied

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to all or part of the bottom of the container,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to all or part of the principal display
        surface, or

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to all or part of a tag that is attached to
        the container;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged food whose
      container is not described in paragraph (a) or (b), the part of
      the label that is applied to all or part of the principal display
      surface;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a prepackaged food other than a
      consumer prepackaged food, the part of the label

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is applied or attached to all or part of
        the surface of the container that is displayed or visible under
        customary conditions of sale or use, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the container does not have a surface
        described in subparagraph (i), that is applied to any part of
        the container except any part that is the bottom of the
        container; or

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food that is not a prepackaged
      food, the part of the label that is applied or attached to all or
      part of the surface of the food that is displayed or visible under
      customary conditions of sale or use. ([espace
      principal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[principal display surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[principal display surface]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of the
    container of a consumer prepackaged food, means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the container has a surface that is displayed
      or visible under customary conditions of sale or use, the total
      area of that surface, excluding any surface that is the top of the
      container;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the container has a lid that is the part of
      the container that is displayed or visible under customary
      conditions of sale or use, the total area of the top surface of
      the lid;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the container does not have a particular
      surface that is displayed or visible under customary conditions of
      sale or use, 40% of the total surface area of the container,
      excluding any surface area that is its top and bottom, if it is
      possible for that 40% to be displayed or visible under customary
      conditions of sale or use;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the container is a bag with surfaces of equal
      dimensions, the total area of one of the surfaces;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the container is a bag with surfaces of
      different dimensions, the total area of one of the largest
      surfaces;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} despite paragraphs (a) to (e), if the container
      does not have a surface that is displayed or visible under
      customary conditions of sale or use to which a label can be
      applied, the total area of one side of a tag that is attached to
      the container;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} despite paragraphs (a) to (e), if the container
      contains wine that is exposed for sale, any part of the surface of
      the container, excluding its top and bottom, that can be seen
      without having to turn the container; and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the container is a wrapper or confining band
      that is so narrow in relation to the size of the food that it
      cannot reasonably be considered to have any surface that is
      displayed or visible under customary conditions of sale or use,
      the total area of one side of a tag that is attached to the
      container. ([principale surface exposée]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[processed egg product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[processed egg product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a food for which
    a standard is set out in Volume 2 of the Standards of Identity
    Document. ([produit d'oeufs transformés]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[processed fruit or vegetable product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[processed fruit or vegetable product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    fruit or vegetable that is in a hermetically sealed package and is
    commercially sterile or that has been cooked, frozen, concentrated,
    pickled or otherwise prepared to assure its preservation and

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for which a standard is set out in Volume 4 of
      the Standards of Identity Document;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for which a grade is set out in Volume 3 of the
      Compendium; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is identified as a processed fruit or
      vegetable product in the Standard Container Sizes
      Document. ([produit de fruits ou de légumes
      transformés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ready-to-eat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ready-to-eat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an edible meat
    product, means that it has been subjected to a treatment or process
    that is sufficient to inactivate vegetative pathogenic
    micro-organisms or their toxins and control spores of food-borne
    pathogenic bacteria so that the meat product does not require
    further preparing before consumption except washing or thawing or
    exposing it to sufficient heat to warm it without cooking it. ([prêt
    à manger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[refrigerated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[refrigerated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a food, means
    that it is kept at a temperature of 4°C or less, without being
    frozen. ([réfrigéré]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sanitary condition]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sanitary condition]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a condition that
    does not present a risk of contamination of a food. ([conditions
    hygiéniques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[shellfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[shellfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bivalve mollusc of the
    class [Bivalvia]{.otherLang lang="la"} or a carnivorous marine
    mollusc of the class [Gastropoda]{.otherLang lang="la"}, or any
    product that is derived from one of those
    molluscs. ([mollusque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Standard Container Sizes Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Standard Container Sizes Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    document entitled Standard Container Sizes, prepared by the Agency
    and published on its website, as amended from time to
    time. ([document sur les tailles de contenants
    normalisées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Standards of Identity Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Standards of Identity Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    document entitled Canadian Standards of Identity, prepared by the
    Agency and published on its website, as amended from time to
    time. ([Document sur les normes d'identité]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[tray]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tray]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of eggs, means a package,
    other than an egg carton, that is capable of containing not more
    than 30 eggs in separate compartments. ([plateau]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[wine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an alcoholic beverage that
    complies with the standard set out in section 2.7.1 of Volume 2 of
    the Food Compositional Standards Document. ([vin]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation --- fresh fruits or
vegetables

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of any provision of these Regulations that refers to "fresh
  fruits or vegetables", other than section 122, any fresh plant or any
  fresh edible fungus, or any part of such a plant or fungus, that is a
  food is considered to be a fresh fruit or vegetable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- subparagraph 11(2)(c)(i)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of any provision of these
  Regulations that refers to "fresh fruits or vegetables", a food
  described in subparagraph 11(2)(c)(i) is not considered to be a fresh
  fruit or vegetable.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition
[[prepare]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in Act

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of the
definition [[prepare]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the Act,
the growing and harvesting of any fresh fruits or vegetables are
prescribed activities.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Words and expressions in documents
incorporated by reference

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
interpreting any document prepared by the Agency that is incorporated by
reference into these Regulations, words and expressions that are used
but not defined in that document have the same meaning as in these
Regulations.

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Trade]{.HTitleText1} {#h-843765 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 10(1) of Act

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of subsection 10(1) of the Act, any food commodity is a
  prescribed food commodity that a person is prohibited to send or
  convey from one province to another --- or to import or export ---
  unless the person does so in accordance with these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 10(2) of Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 10(2) of the Act, any
  food --- other than a food referred to in any of paragraphs 11(2)(a)
  to (c) of these Regulations --- is a prescribed food commodity that a
  person is prohibited to import unless the person is authorized to do
  so by a licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 10(3) of Act

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 10(3) of the Act, any
  food commodity is a prescribed food commodity that a person is
  prohibited to send or convey from one province to another --- or to
  import or export --- unless it meets the requirements of these
  Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Section 12 of Act

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of section
12 of the Act, any food commodity is a prescribed food commodity that a
person is prohibited to have in their possession for the purpose of
sending or conveying from one province to another --- or for the purpose
of exporting --- unless it meets the requirements of these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 13(1) of Act

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of subsection 13(1) of the Act, any food commodity is a
  prescribed food commodity that is to be exported or sent or conveyed
  from one province to another, and any of the following activities is a
  prescribed activity that a person is prohibited to conduct in respect
  of that prescribed food commodity except in accordance with these
  Regulations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} manufacturing, preparing, storing, packaging and
    labelling; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the food commodity is an organic product, in
    addition to the activities set out in paragraph (a), advertising and
    conveying.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 13(2) of Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 13(2) of the Act, any
  food --- other than a food referred to in paragraph 11(2)(a) or (b) of
  these Regulations --- and any food animal is a prescribed food
  commodity that is to be exported or sent or conveyed from one province
  to another, and any of the following activities is a prescribed
  activity that a person is prohibited to conduct in respect of that
  prescribed food commodity unless the person is authorized to conduct
  that activity by a licence:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} manufacturing, processing, treating, preserving,
      grading, packaging and labelling, other than

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the packaging and labelling of fresh fruits or
        vegetables in the field by the person who grows or harvests them
        if they are to be sent or conveyed from one province to another
        to be subsequently manufactured, processed, treated, preserved
        or graded by a licence holder,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the packaging and labelling of a food referred
        to in subparagraph 11(2)(c)(i) if, at the time that the food is
        exported or is sent or conveyed from one province to another, it
        is not a consumer prepackaged food and it has a label applied or
        attached to it, or accompanying it, that bears the expression
        "For Further Preparation Only" or "[pour conditionnement
        ultérieur seulement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}",

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the grading of a livestock carcass and a
        poultry carcass, and

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} any such activity conducted by a restaurant or
        other similar enterprise, where the food is to be sent or
        conveyed by the restaurant or other similar enterprise to
        consumers, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the food is an edible meat product, in
      addition to the activities set out in subparagraph (i), storing
      and handling in its imported condition for the purpose of the
      exercise of an inspector's powers under the Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food animal, slaughtering.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interprovincial trade, import and export

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any food
  that is sent or conveyed from one province to another or that is
  imported or exported

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must not be contaminated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} must be edible;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} must not consist in whole or in part of any filthy,
    putrid, disgusting, rotten, decomposed or diseased animal or
    vegetable substance; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} must have been manufactured, prepared, stored,
    packaged and labelled under sanitary conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- mixing of contaminated
  food

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for a person to mix a contaminated
  food with other food so that it meets the requirements of subsection
  (1), unless the person is authorized to do so by the Minister under
  subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ministerial authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize a person to mix a
  contaminated food with other food if the Minister is of the opinion
  that no risk of injury to human health will result.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with standard

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any food
  that is sent or conveyed from one province to another or that is
  imported or exported and for which a standard is set out in the
  Standards of Identity Document must comply with that standard.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Food likely to be mistaken

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any food that is sent or conveyed from one province
  to another or that is imported or exported and that is likely to be
  mistaken for a food for which a standard is set out in the Standards
  of Identity Document must comply with that standard.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of food additives and other
substances

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** Any person who
manufactures, processes, treats or preserves a food that has been
imported or that is to be exported or sent or conveyed from one province
to another may use, in or on the food, a food additive or other
substance unless the use of that food additive or other substance is not
permitted by these Regulations or under the [Food and Drugs
Act](/eng/acts/F-27) or the use does not comply with any limits or
levels provided by these Regulations or under that Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  food that is imported must have been manufactured, prepared, stored,
  packaged and labelled in a manner and under conditions that provide at
  least the same level of protection as that provided by sections 47 to
  81.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a food additive;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a beverage that contains more than 0.5% absolute
    ethyl alcohol by volume; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a food that is set out in Schedule 1 and that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is unprocessed and is intended to be
      manufactured, processed or treated for use as a grain, oil, pulse,
      sugar or beverage,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has a label applied or attached to it, or
      accompanying it, that bears the expression "For Further
      Preparation Only" or "[pour conditionnement ultérieur
      seulement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is not a consumer prepackaged food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import --- fixed place of business

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who imports a food, other than a food referred to in any of
  paragraphs 11(2)(a) to (c), and who does not have, in Canada, a fixed
  place of business from which they carry on business related to the
  food must,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have a fixed place of business from which they
    carry on business related to the food in a foreign state that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has an inspection system that has been recognized
      under Part 7, if the imported food is a meat product or live or
      raw shellfish, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has a food safety system that has been
      determined by the Minister under subsection (2) to provide at
      least the same level of protection in relation to that food as
      that provided by the provisions of the Act and these Regulations,
      if the imported food is not a meat product or live or raw
      shellfish; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} send or convey the food directly to Canada from a
    foreign state that has an inspection system described in
    subparagraph (a)(i) or a food safety system described in
    subparagraph (a)(ii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Food safety system --- Minister's
  determination

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must determine whether a foreign state's
  food safety system provides at least the same level of protection in
  relation to an imported food as that provided by the provisions of the
  Act and these Regulations by taking into account the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any applicable legislative framework, controls and
    procedures;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the organizational structure of the authority that
    is responsible for the system;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the implementation of the system;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the resources that support the objectives of the
    system; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other relevant information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- certain shellfish

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(a), the reference
  to "shellfish" does not include the adductor muscles of scallops or
  the meat of geoducks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}In transit

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(b), if the food
  passes only in transit through a foreign state, the person is not
  considered to have sent or conveyed the food directly to Canada from
  that foreign state.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import information

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who imports a food must provide to the Minister, in a form
  approved by the President, the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their name and address and, if they hold a licence
    to import, the number of that licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person from whom the
    food is received;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the foreign state of origin;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the address of the first destination of the food in
    Canada;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a description of the food, including its common
    name and quantity;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any information relating to the safety of the food
    that the Minister believes is required in order to respond to a risk
    of injury to human health; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of live or raw shellfish other than the
    adductor muscles of scallops or the meat of geoducks, with respect
    to the establishment at which the shellfish was last manufactured,
    prepared, stored, packaged or labelled prior to its importation, the
    establishment's registration number, or another identification
    number for the establishment, that is provided by the foreign state.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of import information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information referred to in subsection (1), and
  any documents required by sections 96 and 104 and paragraph 167(d),
  must be provided before or at the time of the import.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), in the case of a food other
  than a meat product, the Minister may authorize the person who imports
  the food, at their written request, to provide the information after
  the time of import, at the time specified by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meat products

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3), the foods set out
  in paragraphs 25(a) and (b) are not considered to be meat products.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import --- further inspection

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If,
  during an inspection that is conducted at the time of the import, the
  inspector determines that a further inspection is required,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an edible meat product,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the meat product must be immediately delivered,
      by the licence holder who imports it, to an establishment where it
      must be stored and handled in its imported condition by a licence
      holder and must be kept in that establishment until the further
      inspection is completed, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder who imports the meat product
      must provide the address of the establishment referred to in
      subparagraph (i) to the inspector if it is different from the
      address referred to in paragraph 13(1)(d); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food other than an edible meat
    product, the food must be kept, by the person who imports it, at the
    address referred to in paragraph 13(1)(d) until the further
    inspection is completed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meat products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the foods set out
  in paragraphs 25(a) and (b) are not considered to be meat products.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interprovincial trade and export

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  food that is sent or conveyed from one province to another or that is
  exported must meet the following requirements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the food is manufactured, processed, treated,
    preserved, graded, packaged or labelled in Canada, that activity
    must be conducted by a licence holder in accordance with the
    provisions of the Act and these Regulations, unless that activity is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the packaging and labelling of fresh fruits or
      vegetables in the field by the person who grows or harvests them
      if they are to be sent or conveyed from one province to another to
      be subsequently manufactured, processed, treated, preserved or
      graded by a licence holder,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the packaging and labelling of a food that is
      set out in Schedule 1 and that is unprocessed and intended to be
      manufactured, processed or treated for use as a grain, oil, pulse,
      sugar or beverage, if, at the time that the food is sent or
      conveyed from one province to another or exported, it is not a
      consumer prepackaged food and it has a label applied or attached
      to it, or accompanying it, that bears the expression "For Further
      Preparation Only" or "[pour conditionnement ultérieur
      seulement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the grading of a livestock carcass or a poultry
      carcass;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the food, other than a food referred to in
    paragraph 11(2)(c), has been imported, it must have been imported by
    a licence holder in accordance with the provisions of the Act and
    these Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the food is a meat product and if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any meat product that it contains was
      manufactured, processed, treated, preserved, packaged or labelled
      in Canada, that activity must have been conducted by a licence
      holder in accordance with the provisions of the Act and these
      Regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any meat product that it contains has been
      derived from a livestock carcass or a poultry carcass that has
      been graded in Canada, it must have been graded by a grader in
      accordance with these Regulations,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any meat product that it contains has been
      imported, that meat product must have been imported by a licence
      holder in accordance with the provisions of the Act and these
      Regulations, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any meat product that it contains is derived
      from food animals that were slaughtered in Canada, the food
      animals must have been slaughtered by a licence holder in
      accordance with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of the foods
  referred to in paragraphs 11(2)(a) and (b) and foods that are
  manufactured, processed, treated, preserved, graded, packaged or
  labelled by a restaurant or other similar enterprise, when the food is
  to be sent or conveyed by the restaurant or other similar enterprise
  to consumers.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- export of non-compliant
food

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may export a food that does not meet the requirements of these
  Regulations, other than a requirement of paragraph 8(1)(c) or (d) or
  subsection 15(1), if a label applied or attached to the food bears the
  word "Export" or "[exportation]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign state to which the food is exported
    has a different requirement on the same matter as the unmet
    requirement, the person prepares a document that substantiates that
    the foreign state's requirement has been met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the foreign state to which the food is exported
    has no requirement on the same matter as the unmet requirement,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the unmet requirement must be a requirement set
      out in any of subsection 9(1) and sections 10, 195, 197, 201, 210,
      244 to 249, 262, 268, 272, 273, 292, 293, 306 to 308, 312, 313,
      316, 322, 324 to 327, 329 and 331, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person prepares a document that sets out the
      specifications for the unmet requirement as stipulated by the
      person in the foreign state for whom the exported food is
      intended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period --- documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The documents referred to in paragraph (1)(a) and
  subparagraph (1)(b)(ii) must be kept for two years after the day on
  which the food is exported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional requirements --- meat
  products

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of the export of a meat product under
  subsection (1), the requirements of paragraphs 168(1)(a) and (b) must
  also be met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for export certificate

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for the issuance of a certificate or other document
  referred to in section 48 of the Act must be made to the Minister in a
  form approved by the President.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for issuance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may issue a certificate or other
  document referred to in section 48 of the Act in respect of a food
  commodity that is intended for commercial use if the applicant holds a
  licence to export the food commodity and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food, the manufacturing,
    preparing, storing, packaging and labelling of the food meets the
    applicable requirements of Part 4;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity referred to in
    paragraph (b) of the definition [[food
    commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the Act, other
    than an animal, for which a competent authority of a foreign state
    requires a certificate or other document referred to in section 48
    of the Act for its import into that foreign state for the purpose of
    human consumption, the manufacturing, preparing, storing, packaging
    and labelling of the food commodity meets the applicable
    requirements of Part 4 --- other than Division 3 --- as if it were a
    food; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity referred to in
    subsection (3), the manufacturing, preparing, storing, packaging and
    labelling of the food commodity meets the applicable requirements of
    Part 4 --- other than Division 3 --- as if it were a food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[food
  commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in Act

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (c) of the definition
  [[food commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the Act, any
  commodity that is derived from an animal or plant, or any of its
  parts, is a prescribed food commodity if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that commodity is not included in paragraph (a)
    or (b) of that definition; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a competent authority of a foreign state requires a
    certificate or other document referred to in section 48 of the Act
    in order for that commodity to be imported into that foreign state
    for the purpose of human consumption.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The prescribed food commodity referred to in
  subsection (3) is exempted from the application of any provision of
  the Act and these Regulations that is not necessary to give effect to
  this section. For greater certainty, the exemption does not include
  section 6 of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection before export

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may require that an inspection be
  conducted of any food commodity in respect of which a person has
  applied for a certificate or other document referred to in section 48
  of the Act, for the purpose of deciding whether to issue the
  certificate or other document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection --- accessibility

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If an inspection of a food commodity is required, the
  applicant must make the food commodity readily accessible to an
  inspector at the time of inspection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-compliant food

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may send or convey from one province to another or import a
  food that does not meet the requirements of the Act or these
  Regulations --- other than section 188 as that section relates to
  fresh fruits or vegetables, processed fruit or vegetable products or
  honey, section 306 as that section relates to fresh fruits or
  vegetables or processed fruit or vegetable products and Volume 4 of
  the Standards of Identity Document --- if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a label that bears the expression "For Further
    Preparation Only" or "[pour conditionnement ultérieur
    seulement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" is applied or attached to the food
    or accompanies it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 8(2), the food is
    manufactured, processed, treated, preserved, graded, packaged or
    labelled so that it meets the requirements that are set out in the
    Act and these Regulations within

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} three months after the day on which the food is
      sent or conveyed from one province to another or is imported, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any longer period that is specified by the
      Minister at the person's written request; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of import, the food is not a meat
    product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a beef carcass, or a carcass side, hind quarter,
    front quarter, primal cut or sub-primal cut of a beef carcass, that
    meets the requirements that are set out in paragraph 306(2)(f);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a processed fruit or vegetable product that is
    labelled under paragraph 306(3)(a); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} honey that is labelled under paragraph 306(3)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence holder

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The activities referred to in paragraph (1)(b), other
  than the grading of a livestock carcass or a poultry carcass, must be
  conducted by a licence holder.

Version](section-18-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- import for export

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may import a food that does not meet the requirements of the
  Act or these Regulations --- other than section 188 as that section
  relates to fresh fruits or vegetables, processed fruit or vegetable
  products or honey, section 306 as that section relates to fresh fruits
  or vegetables or processed fruit or vegetable products, Part 13 and
  Volume 4 of the Standards of Identity Document --- if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a label that bears the expression "Imported for
    Export" or "[importé pour l'exportation]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" is
    applied or attached to the food or accompanies it; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the food is intended to be manufactured, processed,
    treated, preserved, graded, packaged or labelled for the purpose of
    exporting it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence holder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The activities referred to in paragraph (1)(b), other
  than the grading of a livestock carcass or a poultry carcass, must be
  conducted by a licence holder.

  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- person who conveys

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  provisions of the Act and these Regulations do not apply to any person
  who conveys a food commodity if their sole concern, in respect of the
  food commodity, is its conveyance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 122 and 123 and subsection 359(3), and any
  provisions of the Act and these Regulations that are necessary to give
  effect to them, apply to the person referred to in subsection (1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Personal use

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 19 of the Act, the sending or conveying from one province to
another, or the import or export, of a food is considered to be an
activity carried out solely for personal use if the food is not intended
for commercial use and if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is sent or conveyed, imported or exported by an
  individual, otherwise than in the course of business, and is part of a
  shipment of food in a quantity that is not more than the quantity set
  out in the document entitled Maximum Quantity Limits for Personal Use
  Exemption, prepared by the Agency and published on its website, as
  amended from time to time; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is imported or exported and is part of the
  personal effects of an immigrant or emigrant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- return to Canada of
exported food

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  requirements of the Act and these Regulations in relation to import do
  not apply in respect of a food that is imported after having been
  exported from Canada if the food is in its exported condition and if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food other than an edible meat
    product, the food is sent back to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person who exported it from Canada, if that
      person holds a licence to export, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person who was last in possession of it
      before its export, from among the persons who manufactured,
      processed, treated, preserved, graded, packaged or labelled it;
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an edible meat product, the import
    is authorized by an inspector and the meat product is immediately
    delivered to an establishment where it is stored and handled in its
    imported condition by a licence holder and kept in that
    establishment until an inspector has completed an inspection of the
    product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meat products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the foods set out
  in paragraphs 25(a) and (b) are not considered to be meat products.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- interprovincial trade,
import and export

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  provisions of the Act and these Regulations do not apply in respect of
  a food that is sent or conveyed from one province to another or that
  is imported or exported if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the food is carried on a conveyance for use by the
    crew or passengers of that conveyance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the food is intended and used for analysis,
    evaluation, research or a Canadian or international food exhibition
    and is part of a shipment that weighs 100 kg or less or, in the case
    of eggs, is part of a shipment of five or fewer cases that are each
    intended to contain 30 dozen eggs;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the food is not intended or sold for use as food
    and a label that indicates its intended use and bears the expression
    "Not for Use as Human Food" or "[ne peut servir à l'alimentation
    humaine]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" is applied or attached to the food
    or accompanies it;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food that is imported, the food

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is imported from the United States into the
      Akwesasne Reserve for use by an individual who has established
      permanent residence on that Reserve, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is part of an in bond shipment that is sent or
      conveyed from a foreign state to a cruise ship or military ship in
      Canada for use by the crew or passengers; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food that is sent or conveyed from
    one province to another, the food is sent or conveyed from one
    federal penitentiary to another.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}In transit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(d)(i), if the
  food is part of an in bond shipment that passes only in transit
  through the United States, the food is not considered to have been
  imported from the United States.
