[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- city of Lloydminster

- **[[23.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The prohibitions under sections 10
  to 13 of the Act and the requirements of these Regulations that apply
  to a food commodity that is sent or conveyed from one province to
  another or to a person conducting a prescribed activity in respect of
  a food commodity that is to be sent or conveyed from one province to
  another do not apply if that food commodity is sent or conveyed from
  Alberta to the part of the city of Lloydminster that is in
  Saskatchewan or from Saskatchewan to the part of the city of
  Lloydminster that is in Alberta.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[city of
  Lloydminster]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1), the [[city of
  Lloydminster]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} has the meaning assigned by the
  definition "City" in section 2 of the The City of Lloydminster Act,
  S.S. 2004, c. C-11.2, as amended from time to time, or the definition
  "City" in section 1 of the City of Lloydminster Act, S.A. 2005, c.
  C-13.5, as amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-216, s. 1](#1494486-1494803){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1494486-1494803.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- in bond shipment

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** The provisions of the Act
and these Regulations do not apply in respect of a food that is part of
an in bond shipment that is sent or conveyed from one foreign state to
another if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the food is manufactured or prepared in a foreign
  state; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the food passes only in transit through Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- meat products

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Subparagraph 7(2)(a)(ii),
subsection 28(2), paragraph 29(1)(d), subsection 31(2), section 42,
paragraph 46(1)(b) and sections 69, 167, 168 and 296 do not apply in
respect of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} meat products --- other than those set out in column
  1 of Part A of Table 2 to Volume 7 of the Standards of Identity
  Document --- that are a mixture of a ready-to-eat meat product and a
  food other than a meat product, if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the ready-to-eat meat product that is contained in
    the mixture was manufactured, processed, treated, preserved,
    packaged or labelled in Canada by a licence holder in accordance
    with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the ready-to-eat meat product that is contained in
    the mixture is derived from a livestock carcass or poultry carcass
    that has been graded in Canada by a grader in accordance with these
    Regulations,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the ready-to-eat meat product that is contained
    in the mixture has been imported by a licence holder in accordance
    with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the mixture is imported and

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the foreign state in which the mixture is
      manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged or labelled, as the case
      may be, has, at the time the activity is conducted, an inspection
      system for meat products that is recognized under Part 7,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the foreign state from which the mixture is
      imported has, at the time of the import, an inspection system for
      meat products that is recognized under Part 7,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the establishment where the food animal from
      which the ready-to-eat meat product that is contained in the
      mixture is derived was slaughtered, and any establishment where
      that meat product was manufactured, processed, treated, preserved,
      handled, tested, graded, coded, stored, packaged or labelled,
      have, at the time that the activity is conducted and at the time
      of the import, a system for manufacturing, processing, treating,
      preserving, handling, testing, grading, coding, slaughtering,
      storing, packaging or labelling, as the case may be, that is
      recognized under Part 7, and

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the licence to import provides an
      inspector with an official document issued by the foreign state,
      in a form approved by the President, that states that the
      ready-to-eat meat product that is contained in the mixture meets
      the requirements that are set out in the Act and these
      Regulations; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} broth, lard, leaf lard, tallow or other rendered fat,
  suet, shortening, flavour or extract if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the meat product from which the broth, lard, leaf
    lard, tallow or other rendered fat, suet, shortening, flavour or
    extract is derived was manufactured, processed, treated, preserved,
    packaged or labelled in Canada by a licence holder in accordance
    with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the meat product from which the broth, lard, leaf
    lard, tallow or other rendered fat, suet, shortening, flavour or
    extract is derived is a livestock carcass or a poultry carcass that
    has been graded in Canada by a grader in accordance with these
    Regulations,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the meat product from which the broth, lard, leaf
    lard, tallow or other rendered fat, suet, shortening, flavour or
    extract is derived has been imported by a licence holder in
    accordance with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the broth, lard, leaf lard, tallow or other
    rendered fat, suet, shortening, flavour or extract is imported and

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the foreign state in which the broth, lard, leaf
      lard, tallow or other rendered fat, suet, shortening, flavour or
      extract is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged or labelled,
      as the case may be, has, at the time the activity is conducted, an
      inspection system for meat products that is recognized under Part
      7,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the foreign state from which the broth, lard,
      leaf lard, tallow or other rendered fat, suet, shortening, flavour
      or extract is imported has, at the time of the import, an
      inspection system for meat products that is recognized under Part
      7,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the establishment where the food animal from
      which the meat product from which the broth, lard, leaf lard,
      tallow or other rendered fat, suet, shortening, flavour or extract
      is derived was slaughtered, and any establishment where the meat
      product was manufactured, processed, treated, preserved, handled,
      tested, graded, coded, stored, packaged or labelled, have, at the
      time that the activity is conducted and at the time of the import,
      a system for manufacturing, processing, treating, preserving,
      handling, testing, grading, coding, slaughtering, storing,
      packaging or labelling, as the case may be, that is recognized
      under Part 7, and

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the licence to import keeps a
      document that substantiates that the conditions set out in
      clauses (A) to (C) are met.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Licences]{.HTitleText1} {#h-843981 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-843982 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 20(1)(a) of Act --- import

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of issuing a licence to import under paragraph 20(1)(a) of
  the Act, any food is a prescribed food commodity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 20(1)(a) of Act --- export

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of issuing a licence to export under
  paragraph 20(1)(a) of the Act, any of the following is a prescribed
  food commodity:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any food;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any food commodity referred to in paragraph
    17(2)(b); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any food commodity referred to in subsection 17(3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 20(1)(b) of Act --- import

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purpose of issuing a licence under paragraph 20(1)(b) of the Act,
  any food is a prescribed food commodity that has been imported, and
  the storing and handling of a food in its imported condition for the
  purpose of the exercise of an inspector's powers under the Act are
  prescribed activities in respect of that prescribed food commodity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 20(1)(b) of Act ---
  interprovincial trade

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of issuing a licence under paragraph
  20(1)(b) of the Act, any food and any food animal are the prescribed
  food commodities that are to be sent or conveyed from one province to
  another, and the following activities are prescribed activities in
  respect of those prescribed food commodities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food, manufacturing, processing,
    treating, preserving, grading, storing, packaging and labelling; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food animal, slaughtering.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Paragraph 20(1)(b) of Act --- export

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of issuing a licence under paragraph
  20(1)(b) of the Act, any food, any food animal, any food commodity
  referred to in paragraph 17(2)(b) and any food commodity referred to
  in subsection 17(3) are prescribed food commodities that are to be
  exported, and the following activities are prescribed activities in
  respect of those prescribed food commodities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food, a food commodity referred to
    in paragraph 17(2)(b) and a food commodity referred to in subsection
    17(3), manufacturing, processing, treating, preserving, grading,
    storing, packaging and labelling; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food animal, slaughtering.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for issuance, renewal or
amendment

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for the issuance, renewal or amendment of a licence must
  be made to the Minister in a form approved by the President.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- food animals and meat
  products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application for the issuance, renewal or amendment
  of a licence to slaughter a food animal, to manufacture, process,
  treat, preserve, grade, package or label a meat product or to store
  and handle an edible meat product in its imported condition must
  include at least one proposed work shift for each establishment where
  the activity is conducted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Work shift

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A work shift must be

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of slaughtering, a work shift during
    which no inspection station referred to in subsection 41(1) is
    operated for more than 7.5 hours in one day and 37.5 hours in one
    work week, excluding meal times; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of manufacturing, processing, treating,
    preserving, grading, packaging or labelling a meat product or
    storing and handling an edible meat product in its imported
    condition, a work shift during which those activities are conducted

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for not more than 7.5 hours in one day and 37.5
      hours in one work week, excluding meal times, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} between 6:00 a.m. and 6:00 p.m.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions --- issuance, renewal or
amendment

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may issue, renew or amend a licence if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application for the issuance of a
    licence, the applicant is not in default of payment of any fee
    related to the Act that is fixed under the [Canadian Food Inspection
    Agency Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant, whether or not they are a licence
    holder conducting the activity in respect of which the application
    for the issuance, renewal or amendment of the licence is made,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence in respect of a food,
      meets the applicable requirements of Part 4, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a licence in respect of a food
      commodity referred to in paragraph 17(2)(b) or subsection 17(3),
      meets the applicable requirements of Part 4 --- other than
      Division 3 --- as if the food commodity were a food;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application for the issuance,
    renewal or amendment of a licence to import, the applicant carries
    on business related to the food in respect of which the application
    is made from a fixed place of business that is in

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Canada, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a foreign state that has an inspection system
      described in subparagraph 12(1)(a)(i) or a food safety system
      described in subparagraph 12(1)(a)(ii);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application for the issuance,
    renewal or amendment of a licence to slaughter a food animal, to
    manufacture, process, treat, preserve, grade, package or label a
    meat product or to store and handle an edible meat product in its
    imported condition, a work shift has been approved by the President
    for each establishment where the activity is conducted;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the information submitted in the application is
    complete, truthful and not misleading; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is of the opinion, on the basis of the
    information that was made available to him or her, that the conduct
    of the activity in respect of which the application for the
    issuance, renewal or amendment of the licence is made does not
    present a risk of injury to human health.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal of suspended licence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b), the Minister may renew a
  suspended licence if the requirements of subsection (1), other than
  any requirements whose contravention forms the grounds of the
  suspension, are met. However, the suspension of the licence remains in
  effect.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal --- issuance, renewal or
amendment

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may refuse
to issue, renew or amend a licence if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the five years before the day on which the
  application is made, the applicant or any of their directors or
  officers

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} have had a licence suspended or cancelled, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} have been convicted of an offence under the Act or
    the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application for the renewal or
  amendment of a licence, the applicant is in default of payment of any
  fee related to the licence that is fixed under the [Canadian Food
  Inspection Agency Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence holder must conduct the activities that are identified in
  their licence, other than importing and exporting, in the
  establishment that is identified in the licence for the activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Work shift

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3) and unless otherwise
  authorized by an inspector, the activities that are conducted in
  respect of a food animal or meat product must be conducted during a
  work shift approved by the President and during which inspection
  services are provided in accordance with Division 2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- ante-mortem examination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of the slaughtering of a food animal, the
  ante-mortem examination may be conducted outside a work shift.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment of licence --- inability to
conduct activity

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  licence holder is unable to conduct an activity that is identified in
  their licence in one of the establishments that are identified in the
  licence, the Minister may amend the licence to remove the
  authorization to conduct that activity in that establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the licence holder in
  writing of the amendment and the date on which it takes effect.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiry

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence expires two years after the date of issuance or renewal that
  is specified in it, unless the licence is cancelled before that date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiry --- amendment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister amends a licence, its expiry date
  remains unchanged.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Invalidity

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** A licence becomes invalid
if the licence holder surrenders the licence to the Minister and it is
not subject to a cancellation procedure.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds for suspension

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may suspend
a licence if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder does not comply with any provision
  of the Act, other than section 15, or with any provision of these
  Regulations, the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27) or the [Food and
  Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder is in default of payment of any
  fee related to the licence that is fixed under the [Canadian Food
  Inspection Agency Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5); or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is of the opinion that a risk of injury
  to human health may result if the licence holder continues to conduct
  an activity that is identified in the licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must not suspend a licence unless the licence holder

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was provided with a written report that sets out
    the grounds for the suspension and the period within which
    corrective action must be taken in order to avoid the suspension;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} failed to take corrective action within that
    period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the licence holder in
  writing of the suspension and the date on which it takes effect.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk of injury to human health

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 36, if the Minister
  is of the opinion that a risk of injury to human health may result if
  the licence holder continues to conduct an activity that is identified
  in the licence, the Minister may suspend the licence immediately after
  the licence holder is provided with a written report that sets out the
  grounds for the suspension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the licence holder in
  writing that their licence is suspended and that the suspension takes
  effect immediately.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of suspension

**[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** The suspension of a
licence must be lifted if the Minister determines that corrective action
has been taken.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds for cancellation

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may cancel a
licence if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder fails to take corrective action
  within 90 days after the day on which the licence was suspended,
  unless a longer period is granted by the Minister at the written
  request of the licence holder;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder continues to conduct an activity
  that is identified in their licence while the licence is suspended;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder or any of their directors or
  officers has been convicted of an offence under the Act or the [Food
  and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder does not comply with any provision
  of the Act, other than section 15, or with any provision of these
  Regulations, the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27) or the [Food and
  Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) and, since its
  issuance or renewal, the licence

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has already been suspended for non-compliance with
    that provision, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has already been suspended twice; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder was not in compliance with section
  15 of the Act in respect of their application for the issuance,
  renewal or amendment of the licence or at any time during the period
  of validity of the licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must not cancel a licence unless the licence holder was
  notified of the grounds for cancellation and was provided with an
  opportunity to be heard in respect of the cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the licence holder in
  writing of the cancellation and the date on which it takes effect.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Inspection Services --- Food Animals and Meat Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844086 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection stations --- slaughtering

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must determine the number of inspection stations that are
  required annually during each work shift that has been approved by the
  President, for each establishment where food animals are slaughtered
  by a licence holder, taking into account the following factors:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the animal species that are slaughtered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the method of carcass examination or inspection
    that is used;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the speed of the slaughter line; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the volume of production.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fixed or unfixed locations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must determine whether inspection
  services at an inspection station are to be provided at a fixed or
  unfixed location in the establishment and, in the case of a fixed
  location, must specify the location in the establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional inspection stations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize one or more additional
  inspection stations for a work shift, on an annual or hourly basis,
  taking into account the factors set out in subsection (1), if the
  licence holder submits a written request to the Minister and an
  inspector is available.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 8(E)](#1362152-1362743){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362152-1362743.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum hours of inspection --- meat
products

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must
determine the minimum number of hours of inspection that are required
per year during each work shift that has been approved by the President,
for each establishment where a meat product is manufactured, processed,
treated, preserved, graded, packaged or labelled, or where an edible
meat product is stored and handled in its imported condition, by a
licence holder, taking into account the following factors:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nature and complexity of the activities that are
  conducted by the licence holder in the establishment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the size of the establishment, the layout of
  equipment and the type of equipment and technology that are used;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the range of meat products and the volume of
  production;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} work scheduling practices; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the inspection records in respect of the
  establishment and the activities that are conducted by the licence
  holder in the establishment and, if available, any inspection records
  in respect of comparable establishments where the same activities are
  conducted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection services outside work shifts

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** Inspection services may
be provided during a period other than a work shift if a licence holder
submits a written request to the Minister and an inspector is available.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence holder must notify the Minister in writing of any change that
  affects any of the factors set out in subsection 41(1) or section 42
  or if an additional inspection station that is authorized under
  subsection 41(3) on an annual basis is no longer required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister becomes aware of a change referred to
  in subsection (1), the Minister must reconsider and, as appropriate,
  adjust the number of inspection stations or minimum number of hours of
  inspection that are required.

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Preventive Controls]{.HTitleText1} {#h-844112 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844113 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in this Part.

[[acceptable level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[acceptable level]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a level of a
    biological, chemical or physical hazard that does not present a risk
    of contamination of the food. ([niveau acceptable]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[agronomic input]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[agronomic input]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an input that is used
    in growing fresh fruits or vegetables, and includes agricultural
    chemicals, biological controls, pollinators, commercial fertilizers,
    compost, compost tea, green manure, manure, mulch, row covers, soil
    amendments and pulp sludge. ([intrant agronomique]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[control measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[control measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a measure that can be
    applied to prevent or eliminate any biological, chemical or physical
    hazard that presents a risk of contamination of a food or to reduce
    the hazard to an acceptable level. ([mesure de
    contrôle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[critical control point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[critical control point]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a step at which
    the application of a control measure is essential to prevent or
    eliminate any biological, chemical or physical hazard that presents
    a risk of contamination of a food or to reduce the hazard to an
    acceptable level. ([point de contrôle critique]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[low-acid food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low-acid food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a food of which any
    component has a pH that is greater than 4.6 and a water activity, as
    determined by the ratio of the water vapour pressure of the
    component to the vapour pressure of pure water at the same
    temperature and pressure, that is greater than 0.85. ([aliment peu
    acide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of a licence to manufacture, process,
      treat, preserve, grade, store, package or label a food, to store
      and handle an edible meat product in its imported condition or to
      slaughter a food animal;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any person who grows or harvests fresh fruits or
      vegetables; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any person who handles fish in a
      conveyance. ([exploitant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[scheduled process]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[scheduled process]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a process in which a
    treatment is applied to a food to render the food commercially
    sterile, taking into account the critical physical and chemical
    factors that affect the treatment's effectiveness. ([traitement
    programmé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  otherwise specified, the requirements of this Part apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any foods that are to be exported or sent or
    conveyed from one province to another;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any edible meat products that are imported, during
    their storing and handling in their imported condition, for the
    purpose of the exercise of an inspector's powers under the Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any food animals from which meat products that are
    to be exported or sent or conveyed from one province to another may
    be derived.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), in the case of the holder of
  a licence to import, section 86 applies in respect of any food that is
  imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- establishment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The requirements of this Part that refer to an
  establishment apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the holder of a licence referred to
    in paragraph (a) of the definition
    [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 45, an establishment
    that is identified in their licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any person referred to in
    paragraph (b) of the definition [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
    in section 45, an establishment where that person grows or harvests
    fresh fruits or vegetables; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any person referred to in
    paragraph (c) of the definition [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
    in section 45, the conveyance where that person handles fish.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment --- slaughtering game
  animals

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 50, subsection 56(2) and
  sections 66, 67 and 71, in the case of an establishment that is
  identified in a licence to slaughter a game animal, the facility in
  the establishment is deemed to be the establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- game animals

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Section 55, subsection 56(1) and sections 58 and 69
  do not apply in respect of an establishment where game animals are
  slaughtered.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Biological, Chemical and Physical Hazards]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844143 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification and analysis of hazards

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must identify and analyze the biological, chemical and
  physical hazards that present a risk of contamination of a food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prevention, elimination and reduction
  of hazards

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator must prevent, eliminate or reduce to an
  acceptable level the hazards referred to in subsection (1) by using
  control measures that are shown by evidence to be effective, including
  any treatment or process and including, in the case of a meat product,
  the control measures that are set out in the document entitled
  Preventive Control Requirements for Biological Hazards in Meat
  Products, prepared by the Agency and published on its website, as
  amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported food

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The holder of a licence to import must comply with
  subsections (1) and (2) in respect of a food that is imported.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Treatments and Processes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844151 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of scheduled process to
low-acid food

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  low-acid food is in a hermetically sealed package, an operator must
  apply the scheduled process referred to in subparagraph (3)(a)(i) and,
  if batch thermal treatment is applied, must use a
  temperature-sensitive indicator that visually indicates that the
  package has been thermally treated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- refrigerated or frozen
  food

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the low-acid food is
  kept refrigerated or frozen and the expressions "Keep Refrigerated"
  and "[garder réfrigéré]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", or "Keep Frozen" and
  "[garder congelé]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", as the case may be, are
  shown on the principal display panel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator must prepare documents that set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of each low-acid food,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the scheduled process that will
      be applied to it, together with the name of the person who is
      responsible for developing the process, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the formulation of the food; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of each application of the scheduled
    process to a low-acid food,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the food and its production volume,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the equipment that is used for the treatment,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any parameters of the treatment, such as start
      and end times, temperatures of the treatment and the pressure used
      in the treatment,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a description of any maintenance of, and of any
      modifications to, the equipment that is used for the treatment,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} any deviations from the scheduled process and any
      corrective action taken,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the incubation results, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a description of any treatment of the cooling
      water.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period of documents

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The documents that set out the information referred
  to in paragraph (3)(a) must be kept for three years after the day of
  the most recent application of the scheduled process to the low-acid
  food, and the documents that set out the information referred to in
  paragraph (3)(b) must be kept for three years after the day of the
  application of the scheduled process.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Maintenance and Operation of Establishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844172 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Responsibility of Operator]{.HTitleText3} {#h-844173 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintenance and operation

**[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** An operator must maintain
and operate an establishment so that the requirements of sections 50 to
81 are met.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Sanitation, Pest Control and Non-food Agents]{.HTitleText3} {#h-844176 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clean and sanitary condition

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  establishment, and any conveyance or equipment in it that is used in
  connection with an activity that is regulated under the Act, must be
  clean and in a sanitary condition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cleaning and sanitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The cleaning and sanitation of the establishment and
  of any conveyance or equipment in it that is used in connection with
  an activity that is regulated under the Act must be conducted in a
  manner that does not present a risk of contamination of a food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Animals --- establishment

- **[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  establishment must be protected against the entry of any animal that
  presents a risk of contamination of a food, except if, in the case of
  any land that forms part of an establishment, there are no reasonably
  practicable measures that may be taken to prevent the entry of such
  animals onto the land.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Animals --- facility or conveyance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An animal must not be in a facility or conveyance
  where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged or labelled
  or where a food animal is slaughtered, unless the animal is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a food that is intended to be manufactured,
    prepared, stored, packaged or labelled in the facility or
    conveyance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a food animal that is intended to be slaughtered in
    the facility or conveyance, whether or not the meat products that
    may be derived from it are intended to be exported or to be sent or
    conveyed from one province to another; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an animal that is intended to be used in the
    manufacturing or preparing of a food in the facility or conveyance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk of contamination

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any measures that are taken for the purposes of
  complying with subsections (1) and (2) must not present a risk of
  contamination of a food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sanitizers, agronomic inputs and non-food
chemical agents

**[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** Any sanitizer, agronomic
input or non-food chemical agent that is in an establishment must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be properly and clearly identified;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be suitable for its intended use and not present a
  risk of contamination of a food; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} be handled and used in a manner that does not present
  a risk of contamination of a food and that is in accordance with any
  manufacturer's instructions.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Conveyances and Equipment]{.HTitleText3} {#h-844197 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conveyances and equipment --- food

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** Any conveyance or
equipment that is used in the manufacturing, preparing, storing,
packaging or labelling of a food or in the slaughtering of a food animal
must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be appropriate for the food or the food animal, as
  the case may be, and for the activity being conducted;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be designed, constructed and maintained to prevent
  contamination of the food;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} be constructed of, and maintained using, materials
  that are suitable for their intended use and, if those materials
  present a risk of contamination of the food, that are

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} corrosion-resistant,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} durable,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} capable of withstanding repeated cleaning and, if
    necessary to prevent contamination of the food, repeated sanitizing,
    unless the equipment is intended for a single use, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} free of any noxious constituent;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} be equipped with instruments to control, indicate and
  record any parameters that are necessary to prevent contamination of
  the food;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} function as intended;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} be accessible and, if necessary for its cleaning,
  sanitizing, maintenance or inspection, able to be easily disassembled;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} be used, maintained and, if necessary, calibrated in
  accordance with the manufacturer's instructions and in a manner that
  does not present a risk of contamination of the food; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} have surfaces that, if they come into contact with a
  food, are smooth, free from pitting, cracks and flakes and
  non-absorbent, except when the surface does not present a risk of
  contamination of the food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other conveyances and equipment

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** Any conveyance or
equipment in an establishment that is used to handle any contaminated
materials, any waste or any other thing that is inedible must, unless
that conveyance or equipment does not come into contact with those
materials, waste or things,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be used only for that purpose;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} be identified as being reserved for that purpose; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} meet the applicable requirements of section 53.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equipment --- restraining

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** An establishment where
food animals are slaughtered must have equipment for restraining food
animals during their handling, their assessment, their ante-mortem
examination and their inspection.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[Conditions Respecting Establishments]{.HTitleText3} {#h-844219 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Land

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If any
  land that forms part of an establishment presents a risk of
  contamination of a food, measures must be taken to eliminate the risk.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an establishment is located near any place or
  thing that presents a risk of contamination of a food, measures must
  be taken to eliminate the risk.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interior of facility or conveyance

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** The interior of any
facility or conveyance where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored,
packaged or labelled or where a food animal is slaughtered must be

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} designed to prevent the accumulation of substances
  that present a risk of contamination of the food, including dust,
  dirt, micro-organisms and food particles, and to permit effective
  maintenance, cleaning and sanitizing;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} designed, constructed and maintained in such a manner
  that

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the size and layout is adequate to accommodate the
    activity being conducted and the equipment used in the activity,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the entry of insects, rodents and other vermin is
    prevented,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any floors, walls, ceilings, windows and doors
    are smooth, non-absorbent and impervious to moisture, except if
    those floors, walls, ceilings, windows or doors do not present a
    risk of the contamination of the food, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any floors provide or permit good drainage, except
    if there is no risk of liquid accumulation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} constructed of, and maintained using, materials that
  are

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} suitable for their intended use,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} appropriate for the food or the food animal, as
    the case may be, and for the activity being conducted,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} durable,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} capable of withstanding repeated cleaning and, if
    necessary to prevent contamination of the food, repeated sanitizing,
    and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} free of any noxious constituent; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} of sound construction and in good repair.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Slaughtering --- separate areas

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  establishment where food animals are slaughtered must have separate
  areas for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} keeping, examining and inspecting food animals;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} segregating and isolating food animals under
    section 132 or paragraph 140(b);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} holding food animals that show a deviation from
    normal behaviour, physiology or appearance; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} humanely killing food animals under paragraph
    140(c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inedible meat products area

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The establishment must also have an enclosed area for
  the handling of inedible meat products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Movement of food animals

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Floors, ramps, gangways and chutes that are used by
  food animals in the establishment must provide secure footing and must
  not present a risk of injury to the food animals during movement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stations for inspections, examinations
  and screenings

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The establishment must be equipped with inspection
  stations at the fixed locations specified by the President under
  subsection 41(2) and in the numbers determined by the President under
  subsection 41(1), and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a licence holder is authorized to conduct a
    post-mortem examination program, stations for post-mortem
    examinations; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a licence holder is authorized to conduct a
    post-mortem defect management program, stations for post-mortem
    screenings.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Design, construction and maintenance ---
movement

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  facility or conveyance where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored,
  packaged or labelled or where a food animal is slaughtered must be
  designed, constructed and maintained in such a manner that the
  movement of persons and things within, into and out of it is
  controlled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Movement --- no risk of contamination

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The movement must not present a risk of contamination
  of the food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incompatible activities

**[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** Physical or other
effective means must be used to separate incompatible activities in
order to prevent contamination of a food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separation of food

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** Physical or other
effective means must be used to separate a food from

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} anything that presents a risk of contamination of the
  food;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any food that does not meet the requirements of the
  Act or these Regulations; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} anything that is manufactured, prepared, stored,
  packaged or labelled in an establishment and not intended or sold for
  use as food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arrival of certain food at establishment

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  food that presents a risk of injury to human health, that is exempted
  under section 22 from the application of the import requirements that
  are set out in the Act and these Regulations or that does not meet the
  requirements that are set out in the Act or these Regulations must be
  identified as such and placed in a designated area when it arrives at
  an establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measures to prevent contamination

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any measures that are necessary to prevent the food
  described in subsection (1) from contaminating any other food that is
  in the establishment must be taken.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lighting

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  establishment must be equipped with natural or artificial lighting
  that is appropriate for the food or the food animal that is intended
  to be slaughtered, as the case may be, and for the activity being
  conducted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Light fixtures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any light fixtures in the establishment must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be capable of withstanding repeated cleaning and,
    if necessary to prevent contamination of a food, repeated
    sanitizing; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} not present a risk of contamination of the food in
    the event of breakage.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ventilation system

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** A facility or conveyance
where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged or labelled or
where a food animal is slaughtered must be equipped with a ventilation
system that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} provides natural or mechanical ventilation with
  sufficient air exchange to provide clean air and to remove unclean air
  and odours that might affect the food;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is accessible and, if necessary for its cleaning,
  maintenance or inspection, is able to be disassembled;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} is capable of withstanding repeated cleaning; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} functions as intended.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temperature and humidity

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  temperature and humidity level in a facility or conveyance where a
  food is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged or labelled or where
  a food animal is slaughtered must be maintained at levels that are
  appropriate for the food or the food animal, as the case may be, and
  for the activity being conducted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Heating, cooling or humidity-control
  system

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the facility or conveyance is equipped with a
  heating, cooling or humidity-control system, the system must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if necessary to prevent contamination of a food, be
    equipped with instruments to control, indicate and record the
    temperature and humidity levels;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be accessible and, if necessary for its cleaning,
    maintenance or inspection, is able to be disassembled;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be capable of withstanding repeated cleaning; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} function as intended.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal and disposal of contaminated
materials and waste

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  establishment must have means for the removal and disposal of
  contaminated materials and waste and, if necessary to prevent
  contamination of a food, be equipped with a drainage, sewage and
  plumbing system that functions as intended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Frequency and manner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Contaminated materials and waste must be removed and
  disposed of at a frequency that is sufficient to prevent contamination
  of a food and in a manner that does not present a risk of
  contamination of a food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cleaning stations, lavatories, etc.

- **[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  necessary to prevent the contamination of a food, an establishment
  must be equipped with hand cleaning and sanitizing stations,
  lavatories, showers, drinking water stations, break rooms or change
  rooms that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are appropriately equipped and adequate in number
    and size for the number of persons using them;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are located so that they are readily accessible to
    the persons using them; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} are capable of withstanding repeated cleaning and,
    if necessary to prevent contamination of a food, repeated
    sanitizing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hand cleaning and sanitizing stations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The hand cleaning and sanitizing stations must permit
  the effective cleaning of hands.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lavatories

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The lavatories must be located and maintained so that
  they do not present any risk of contamination of a food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Area for inspector's use

**[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** At the request of an
inspector, the inspector must be provided with an area that is readily
accessible, appropriately equipped and appropriate in size for the
exercise of their powers, and the performance of their duties and
functions, under the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Office, lockers, etc., for inspector

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  establishment in which a meat product or processed egg product is
  manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged or labelled or in which a
  food animal is slaughtered must provide an inspector with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a furnished office that is readily accessible,
    appropriately equipped and appropriate in size for the exercise of
    their powers and the performance of their duties and functions under
    the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} lockers and cabinets that are readily accessible
    and appropriate for the protection and storing of their equipment
    and documents; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} access to a lockable storage facility or equipment
    that is appropriate for the protection, preservation and storing of
    samples.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Private office, change rooms, etc., for
  inspector

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an establishment in which a food
  animal is slaughtered, the office referred to in paragraph (1)(a) must
  be private and the inspector must also be provided with access to a
  lavatory, a shower and a change room in the establishment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Water --- contact with food

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  water that might come into contact with a food must be potable, unless
  it does not present a risk of contamination of the food, and must be
  protected against contamination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Steam and ice --- contact with food

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any steam or ice that might come into contact with a
  food must be made from water that meets the requirements of subsection
  (1), unless the steam or ice does not present a risk of contamination
  of the food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Water --- cross-connections

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any system that supplies water that meets the
  requirements of subsection (1) must not be cross-connected with any
  other system, unless measures are taken to eliminate any risk of
  contamination of a food as a result of the cross-connection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Water given to food animals

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any water or other source of hydration that is
  provided to food animals that are intended to be slaughtered in an
  establishment must not present a risk of injury to the health of those
  animals and must not present a risk of contamination of the meat
  products that may be derived from those animals.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supply of water, steam and ice

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  establishment must be supplied, as appropriate for the food or the
  food animal that is intended to be slaughtered, as the case may be,
  and for the activity being conducted, with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} water that is adequate in quantity, temperature, pH
    and pressure to meet the needs of the establishment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} steam that is adequate in quantity and pressure to
    meet those needs; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} ice that is adequate in quantity to meet those
    needs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Treatment of water, steam or ice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any treatment of water, steam or ice must be applied
  in a manner that does not present a risk of contamination of a food.

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[Unloading, Loading and Storing]{.HTitleText3} {#h-844336 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conveyances

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** Any conveyance that is
used to convey a food to or from an establishment and that is unloaded
or loaded at the establishment

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} must be designed, constructed and maintained to
  prevent contamination of the food;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} must be constructed of, and maintained using,
  materials that are suitable for their intended use and, if the
  materials present a risk of contamination of the food, that are

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} durable,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} capable of withstanding repeated cleaning and, if
    necessary to prevent contamination of a food, repeated sanitizing,
    and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} free of any noxious constituent;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} must be capable of maintaining the temperature and
  humidity at levels that are appropriate for the food and, if necessary
  to prevent contamination of the food, be equipped with instruments
  that control, indicate and record those levels;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} must not contain any animal, other than an animal
  referred to in paragraph 51(2)(a), any [[pest control
  product]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of the
  [Pest Control Products Act](/eng/acts/P-9.01) or any other material or
  substance that presents a risk of contamination of the food; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} must be clean and in a sanitary condition at the time
  of unloading or loading.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unloading and loading

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** Any unloading and loading
of a food or of a food animal that is intended to be slaughtered, from
or onto a conveyance at an establishment, must be conducted in a manner
that does not present a risk of contamination of a food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storing --- food

- **[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  storing of a food must be conducted in a manner that does not present
  a risk of contamination of the food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storing --- other

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any storing of conveyances, equipment, sanitizers,
  agronomic inputs, chemical agents, starter products, packaging
  material, labels or any other thing that is used in the manufacturing,
  preparing, storing, packaging or labelling of a food must be conducted
  in a manner that does not present a risk of contamination of the food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[starter
  products]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (2), [[starter
  products]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the materials that are used to
  start growing fresh fruits or vegetables and includes seeds,
  seedlings, plants, cuttings, canes, seed potatoes and nursery stock.

#### [SUBDIVISION F]{.HLabel3}[Competency]{.HTitleText3} {#h-844356 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Competencies and qualifications

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** Any person who is
involved in the manufacturing, preparing, storing, packaging or
labelling of a food or in the slaughtering of a food animal must have
the competencies and qualifications that are necessary to carry out
their duties.

#### [SUBDIVISION G]{.HLabel3}[Hygiene]{.HTitleText3} {#h-844359 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clothing, footwear and protective
coverings

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** Any person who enters or
is in an area where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged
or labelled or where a food animal is slaughtered must wear clothing,
footwear and protective coverings, including gloves, a hairnet, a beard
net and a smock, that are in good condition, clean and in sanitary
condition and that are appropriate for the food and for the activity
being conducted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Personal cleanliness

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** Any person who enters or
is in an area where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged
or labelled or where a food animal is slaughtered must maintain personal
cleanliness to prevent contamination of the food, including by cleaning
and, if necessary, by sanitizing their hands

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} immediately on entering the area;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} immediately after using a lavatory;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} immediately before beginning to conduct the activity;
  and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} at a frequency appropriate for the food and for the
  activity being conducted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Spitting, chewing gum and other acts

**[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** Any person who enters or
is in an area where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged
or labelled or where a food animal is slaughtered must refrain from
spitting, chewing gum, using tobacco products, eating, having
unnecessary contact with the food and doing any other act that presents
a risk of contamination of the food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objects and substances --- risk of
contamination

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** Any person who enters or
is in an area where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged
or labelled or where a food animal is slaughtered must refrain from
wearing or using any object or substance that presents a risk of
contamination of the food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting of disease, illness, symptoms
and lesions

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** Any person who works in
an area where a food is manufactured, prepared, stored, packaged or
labelled or where a food animal is slaughtered and who has a disease or
illness, symptoms of a disease or illness or an open or infected lesion
must report them to the operator.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communicable disease and lesions --- risk
of contamination

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** The operator must prevent
any person who is suffering from, or is a known carrier of, a
communicable disease or who has an open or infected lesion from entering
or being in an area of an establishment where a food is manufactured,
prepared, stored, packaged or labelled or where a food animal is
slaughtered if the person's condition presents a risk of contamination
of the food.

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Investigation, Notification, Complaints and Recall]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844376 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investigation

- **[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator who suspects on reasonable grounds that a food presents a
  risk of injury to human health or does not meet the requirements of
  the Act or these Regulations must immediately investigate the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification and mitigation of risk

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the investigation establishes that the food
  presents a risk of injury to human health, the operator must
  immediately notify the Minister and immediately take action to
  mitigate the risk.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaints procedure

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must prepare, keep and maintain a document that sets out a
  procedure for receiving, investigating and responding to complaints
  that are received in relation to a food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complaints

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a complaint is received, the operator must
  implement the procedure and prepare a document that sets out the
  details of the complaint, the results of the investigation and the
  actions taken based on those results and keep it for two years after
  the day on which the actions are completed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recall procedure

- **[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  operator must prepare, keep and maintain a document that sets out a
  recall procedure that enables the effective recall of a food, the name
  of a contact person who is responsible for the procedure and the name
  of a contact person who is responsible for conducting recalls.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recall simulation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator must, at least once every 12 months,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} conduct a recall simulation, based on the recall
    procedure; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prepare a document that sets out the details of how
    the recall simulation was conducted and the results of the
    simulation, and keep that document for two years after the day on
    which the recall simulation is completed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recall --- notice to Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an operator determines that a food should be
  recalled because it presents a risk of injury to human health, the
  operator must immediately notify the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recall --- implementation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a food is the subject of a recall because it
  presents a risk of injury to human health, the operator must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} immediately implement the recall procedure; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prepare a document that sets out the details of the
    recall, including any information that substantiates its
    effectiveness, and keep the document for two years after the day on
    which the recall is initiated.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported food

**[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a licence
to import must comply with sections 82 to 84 in respect of a food that
is imported.

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Preventive Control Plan]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844402 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licence holders

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence holder must prepare, keep and maintain a written preventive
  control plan that meets the requirements of section 89 for any
  activity identified in their licence that they conduct in respect of a
  food or food animal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- food to be exported

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a preventive control plan is
  not required to be prepared, kept or maintained for any activity that
  the licence holder conducts in respect of a food, other than fish or a
  meat product, that is exported, unless a certificate or other document
  referred to in section 48 of the Act is sought in respect of the food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- sales of \$100,000 or
  less

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if a licence holder's gross
  sales that are derived from food are \$100,000 or less for the 12
  months before the day on which they most recently made an application
  for the issuance, renewal or amendment of a licence, a preventive
  control plan must be prepared, kept and maintained only for any
  activity that they conduct in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a food animal, meat product, fish, dairy product,
    egg, processed egg product or processed fruit or vegetable product
    that is identified in their licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a food in respect of which a certificate or other
    document referred to in section 48 of the Act is sought.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Growers or harvesters of fresh fruits or
vegetables

**[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** Any person who grows or
harvests fresh fruits or vegetables must prepare, keep and maintain a
written preventive control plan that meets the requirements of section
89 for any activity that they conduct in respect of those fresh fruits
or vegetables if they are

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to be exported and a certificate or other document
  referred to in section 48 of the Act is sought in respect of the fresh
  fruits or vegetables; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to be sent or conveyed from one province to another
  and the person's gross sales that are derived from food are more than
  \$100,000 for the previous 12 months.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Implementation

**[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** Any person who is
required to prepare, keep and maintain a preventive control plan must
implement that plan.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of preventive control plan

- **[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  preventive control plan must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures for ensuring that the
    applicable requirements of sections 201 and 205, subsection 206(1),
    sections 208, 218, 221, 296, 306, 307, 316, 317, 321, 322, 324 to
    326 and 328 are met;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures for ensuring that the
    food is packaged and labelled in a manner that does not contravene
    subsection 6(1) of the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in relation to the applicable requirements of these
    Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the biological, chemical and
      physical hazards that are identified under subsection 47(1) as
      presenting a risk of contamination of a food, of the control
      measures for preventing or eliminating those hazards or reducing
      them to an acceptable level and of the evidence that the control
      measures are effective,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the critical control points, of
      the related control measures and of the evidence that the control
      measures are effective,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the critical limits for each
      critical control point,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for monitoring the critical
      control points in relation to their critical limits,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the corrective action procedures for each
      critical control point,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for verifying that the
      implementation of the preventive control plan results in
      compliance with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations,
      and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} documents that substantiate that the preventive
      control plan has been implemented with respect to
      subparagraphs (i) to (vi); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in relation to the applicable requirements of
    sections 128 to 136, paragraphs 140(b) and (c) and sections 141 to
    144,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures for preventing or
      eliminating a risk of avoidable suffering, injury or death to the
      food animals during their handling, and of the evidence that those
      measures are effective,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures for preventing or
      eliminating a risk of avoidable suffering or injury to the food
      animals during their slaughtering, and of the evidence that those
      measures are effective,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the performance criteria for
      evaluating the effectiveness of each of those measures,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for monitoring each of those
      measures,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the corrective action procedures for each of
      those measures,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for verifying that the
      implementation of the preventive control plan results in
      compliance with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for auditing, on a regular
      basis, the outcome of the implementation of the preventive control
      plan, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} documents that substantiate that the
      preventive control plan has been implemented with respect to
      subparagraphs (i) to (vii); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} supporting documents that show evidence of the
    information recorded under paragraphs (a) and (b), subparagraphs
    (c)(i) to (vi) and (d)(i) to (vii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period of documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Each document referred to in subparagraphs
  (1)(c)(vii) and (d)(viii) must be kept for two years after the day on
  which it is prepared.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- game animals

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The preventive control plan of the holder of a
  licence to slaughter a game animal is not required to include any
  information specified in subsection (1) other than the information
  specified in subparagraphs (1)(c)(i) and (d)(i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional content --- import

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The preventive control plan of the holder of a
  licence to import must also include the information specified in
  subparagraphs (1)(c)(i) to (vii) in relation to the requirements of
  section 11.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional content --- export

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The preventive control plan of the holder of a
  licence to export must also include the information specified in
  subparagraphs (1)(c)(i) to (vii) in relation to the requirements of
  subsection 15(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional content --- post-mortem
  programs

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The preventive control plan of a licence holder who
  is authorized under subsection 160(3) to conduct a post-mortem
  examination program or a post-mortem defect management program must
  also include the information specified in subparagraphs (1)(c)(i)
  to (vii) and paragraph (1)(e) in relation to that program and must
  meet the requirements that are set out in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a post-mortem examination, the
    document entitled Fundamentals of the Post-mortem Examination
    Program, prepared by the Agency and published on its website, as
    amended from time to time; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a post-mortem defect management
    program, the document entitled Fundamentals of the Post-mortem
    Defect Management Program, prepared by the Agency and published on
    its website, as amended from time to time.

## [PART 5]{.HLabel1}[Traceability]{.HTitleText1} {#h-844453 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents

- **[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who sends or conveys a food from one province to another, or
  who imports or exports it, any holder of a licence to slaughter a food
  animal, to manufacture, process, treat, preserve, grade, store,
  package or label a food or to store and handle an edible meat product
  in its imported condition and any person who grows or harvests fresh
  fruits or vegetables that are to be sent or conveyed from one province
  to another or exported must, if they provide the food to another
  person, prepare and keep documents that set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the common name of the food, a lot code or other
    unique identifier that enables the food to be traced and the name
    and principal place of business of the person by or for whom the
    food was manufactured, prepared, produced, stored, packaged or
    labelled;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} except if they provide the food to another person
    as a sale at retail, the date on which it was provided and the name
    and address of the person to whom it was provided;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if they were provided the food by another person,
    the name and address of that person and the date on which it was
    provided; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name of any food commodity that they
    incorporated into the food or from which they derived the food and,
    if they were provided the food commodity by another person, the name
    and address of that person and the date on which it was provided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents --- retail sale

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person who sells a food at retail, other than a
  restaurant or other similar enterprise that sells the food as a meal
  or snack, must prepare and keep documents that include the information
  specified in paragraphs (1)(a), (c) and (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period of documents

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The documents referred to in subsections (1) and (2)
  must be kept for two years after the day on which the food was
  provided to another person or sold at retail, and must be accessible
  in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production of documents

- **[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who has received a request from the Minister for a document
  referred to in section 90, or any part of such a document, must
  provide it to the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within 24 hours after receipt of the request, or
    within

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any shorter period that is specified by the
      Minister, if the Minister believes that it is necessary in order
      to identify or respond to a risk of injury to human health
      associated with a food commodity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any longer period that is specified by the
      Minister, if the Minister believes that the document is not
      necessary for a recall that is or may be ordered under subsection
      19(1) of the [Canadian Food Inspection Agency
      Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if provided electronically, in a single file and in
    plain text that is capable of being imported into and manipulated by
    standard commercial software.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[plain
  text]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In paragraph (1)(b), [[plain
  text]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means data that is not encrypted and whose
  semantic content is available.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Labelling

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person referred to in subsection 90(1) or (2) must ensure that a label
  that bears the information specified in paragraph 90(1)(a) is applied
  or attached to any food, or accompanies any food, that is provided to
  another person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged food

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of consumer prepackaged food that is not
  packaged at retail, the unique identifier referred to in paragraph
  90(1)(a) must be a lot code.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a food at the time of its export;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a food, other than a consumer prepackaged food, at
    the time of its sale at retail; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prepackaged food described in paragraphs 213(a)
    to (c) at the time of its sale at retail.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- foods described in
  paragraphs 219(1)(a) and (b)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), any food described in
  paragraph 219(1)(a) or (b) is not required to be labelled with the
  common name of the food at the time of its sale at retail.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- foods described in
  section 220

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), any food described in section
  220 is not required to be labelled with the name and principal place
  of business of the person by or for whom the food was manufactured,
  prepared, produced, stored, packaged or labelled.

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Commodity-specific Requirements]{.HTitleText1} {#h-844488 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844489 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- import, interprovincial
trade and export

- **[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  requirements of this Part apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any foods that have been imported or any foods that
    are to be sent or conveyed from one province to another or exported;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any food animals from which meat products that are
    to be sent or conveyed from one province to another or exported may
    be derived.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- food animals

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The requirements of sections 128 to 136 apply in
  respect of food animals that are in an establishment that is
  identified in a licence to slaughter.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Dairy Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844497 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preparation

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** Any milk or cream that is
used in preparing a dairy product that is to be sent or conveyed from
one province to another or exported must meet the applicable
requirements of the legislation of the province in which the dairy
product is prepared.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Eggs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-844500 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pasteurization

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence holder may pasteurize eggs in the shell only if they are
  graded Canada A or Grade A.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import --- eggs pasteurized in shell

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Eggs that are pasteurized in the shell and that are
  imported must have been graded Grade A before pasteurization.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import --- foreign official document

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a licence
to import may import eggs only if the licence holder provides an
inspector with an official document issued by the foreign state, in a
form approved by the President, that states that the eggs meet the
requirements that are set out in the Act and these Regulations.
