[Previous
Version](section-170-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 170"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for recognition of system

- **[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  foreign state's inspection system for meat products is recognized, the
  foreign state may make an application to the Minister in writing for
  the recognition of the system of manufacturing, processing, treating,
  preserving, handling, testing, grading, coding, slaughtering, storing,
  packaging or labelling that is used in an establishment and that is
  subject to that inspection system.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must include the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person who conducts any relevant
    activities and the establishment's address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the establishment's registration number, or another
    identification number for the establishment, that is provided by the
    foreign state;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement that identifies the system in respect
    of which the application is made;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a declaration by the authorized representative of
    the foreign state who makes the application that states that the
    system in respect of which the application is made is subject to the
    foreign state's recognized inspection system and meets the
    requirements of that inspection system that apply to the conduct of
    those activities in respect of meat products that are intended to be
    exported to Canada; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name, title and signature of the authorized
    representative of the foreign state who makes the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recognition by Minister

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must recognize a system of
  manufacturing, processing, treating, preserving, handling, testing,
  grading, coding, slaughtering, storing, packaging or labelling in
  respect of which an application is made if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign state's inspection system for the
    relevant meat products is recognized under subsection 170(3); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the system of manufacturing, processing, treating,
    preserving, handling, testing, grading, coding, slaughtering,
    storing, packaging or labelling is subject to the inspection system
    referred to in paragraph (a) and meets the requirements of that
    inspection system that apply to those activities in respect of meat
    products that are intended to be exported to Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of recognition --- inspection
system

- **[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must suspend the recognition of a foreign state's inspection
  system that is referred to in section 170 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the foreign state fails to notify the Minister in
    writing, as soon as feasible, of any changes that it has made to the
    system or to the legislation governing the system; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the system no longer provides at least the same
    level of protection as that provided by the provisions of the Act
    and these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of recognition --- system
  used in establishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must suspend the recognition of a system
  of manufacturing, processing, treating, preserving, handling, testing,
  grading, coding, slaughtering, storing, packaging or labelling that is
  referred to in section 171 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the system no longer meets the foreign state's
    requirements that apply to those activities;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a shipment of meat products that have been
    manufactured, processed, treated, preserved, handled, tested,
    graded, coded, stored, packaged or labelled using the system is
    determined to contravene a provision of the Act, these Regulations,
    the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27) or the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870), and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} during the six-month period immediately prior to
      that shipment, there have been two other shipments of meat
      products that have been manufactured, processed, treated,
      preserved, handled, tested, graded, coded, stored, packaged or
      labelled using the system in respect of which such a determination
      of non-compliance has been made, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} among the four most recent shipments, prior to
      that shipment, of meat products that have been manufactured,
      processed, treated, preserved, handled, tested, graded, coded,
      stored, packaged or labelled using the system, two shipments have
      been determined to be non-compliant;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the system is no longer subject to the foreign
    state's inspection system; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the recognition of the foreign state's inspection
    system to which the system is subject has been suspended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the foreign state in writing
  of the suspension under subsection (1) or (2), the grounds for the
  suspension and the date on which it takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The suspension takes effect on the day on which the
  notice is issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of recognition

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the foreign state in writing
  that the recognition is no longer suspended if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a suspension under subsection (1) or
    under paragraph (2)(a), (c) or (d), the circumstances that gave rise
    to a suspension have been remedied; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a suspension under paragraph (2)(b),
    the establishment has taken corrective action.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of recognition ---
inspection system

- **[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must cancel the recognition of a foreign state's inspection
  system referred to in section 170 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no meat products or live or raw shellfish to which
    the system applies have been exported to Canada from the foreign
    state in the previous five years;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances that gave rise to a suspension
    have not been remedied within 12 months after the day on which the
    recognition was suspended; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the foreign state requests the cancellation in
    writing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of recognition --- system
  used in establishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must cancel the recognition of a system
  of manufacturing, processing, treating, preserving, handling, testing,
  grading, coding, slaughtering, storing, packaging or labelling
  referred to in section 171 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the recognition of the inspection system to which
    it is subject has been cancelled;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no meat products that were manufactured, processed,
    treated, preserved, handled, tested, graded, coded, stored, packaged
    or labelled using the system have been exported to Canada from the
    foreign state in the previous five years;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} circumstances that gave rise to a suspension have
    not been remedied within 12 months after the day on which the
    recognition was suspended; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the foreign state in which the establishment is
    located requests the cancellation in writing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the foreign state in writing
  of the cancellation under subsection (1) or (2), the grounds for the
  cancellation and the date on which it takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The cancellation takes effect on the day on which the
  notice is issued.

## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Ministerial Exemptions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-845093 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[test market
food]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[173.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 174, [[test market food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a food that,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prior to being subject to an exemption referred to in
  subsection 174(1), was not sold in Canada in its current form; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} differs substantially from any other food sold in
  Canada with respect to its composition, function, condition or
  packaging form.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 12](#1362159-1362758){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362159-1362758.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for exemption --- test market
food or shortage in supply

- **[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person may apply, in a form approved by the President, for an
  exemption from the application of a provision of the Act or these
  Regulations for the purpose of selling a test market food or for
  alleviating a shortage in Canada in the available supply of a food
  that is manufactured, processed or produced in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, in writing, grant the exemption if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information submitted in the application for
    the exemption is complete, truthful and not misleading;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the food in respect of which the application for
    the exemption is made meets the requirements that are set out in
    paragraphs 8(1)(a) to (d);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is of the opinion that no risk of
    injury to human health will result;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application for an exemption for
    the purpose of selling a test market food, the Minister is of the
    opinion that the exemption will not

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} confuse or mislead the public, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} disrupt the normal trading patterns of the
      industry or the normal food pricing patterns; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an application for an exemption for
    the purpose of alleviating a shortage in Canada in the available
    supply of a food that is manufactured, processed or produced in
    Canada, the exemption is necessary to alleviate that shortage.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 13](#1362161-1362763){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362161-1362763.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-174-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 174"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for exemption --- inspection
legend

- **[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  holder of a licence to slaughter food animals and to process meat
  products may apply, in a form approved by the President, for an
  exemption from the application of section 151 in respect of a carcass
  or carcass side.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, in writing, grant the exemption if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information submitted in the application for
    the exemption is complete, truthful and not misleading;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder has established procedures

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for processing the carcass or carcass side within
      the establishment where the food animal was slaughtered, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for identifying the carcass or carcass side to
      allow it to be linked with any edible meat products derived from
      it and for the keeping of documents to allow for this linkage;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is of the opinion that no risk of
    injury to human health will result; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is of the opinion that no risk of harm
    to interprovincial trade or export will result.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The exemption granted under subsection (2) is subject
  to the following conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carcass or carcass side must be processed
    within the establishment where the food animal was slaughtered; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the carcass or carcass side must be identified to
    allow it to be linked with any edible meat products derived from it
    and documents must be kept to allow for this linkage.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional conditions

**[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, at
any time, make any exemption referred to in subsection 174(2) or 175(2)
subject to conditions.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

**[[177]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-177 .sectionLabel}** An exemption referred
to in subsection 174(2) or 175(2) is valid until the expiry date that is
specified in the exemption or, if no date is specified, until the end of
the period that is two years after the day on which the exemption is
granted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

**[[178]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-178 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may cancel
an exemption if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is of the opinion that to not cancel the
  exemption may result in a risk of injury to human health;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an exemption referred to in subsection
  175(2), the Minister is of the opinion that to not cancel the
  exemption may result in a risk of harm to interprovincial trade or
  export; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person who has been granted the exemption does
  not comply with any condition to which the exemption is subject or any
  provision of the Act or these Regulations other than a provision in
  respect of which the exemption is granted.

## [PART 9]{.HLabel1}[Inspection Legends]{.HTitleText1} {#h-845130 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[inspection
mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in Act

**[[179]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-179 .sectionLabel}** The inspection legends
that are set out in Figures 1 and 2 of Schedule 2 are prescribed for the
purposes of the definition [[inspection mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
section 2 of the Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Edible meat products --- Figure 1 of
Schedule 2

- **[[180]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence holder or an inspector is authorized to apply the inspection
  legend that is set out in Figure 1 of Schedule 2 to, and use it in
  connection with, an edible meat product, whether prepackaged or not,
  if the following conditions are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the meat product was manufactured, processed,
    treated, preserved, packaged or labelled by the licence holder in
    accordance with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where any meat product that it contains
    was manufactured, processed, treated, preserved, packaged or
    labelled in Canada, that activity was conducted by a licence holder
    in accordance with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the meat product is a livestock
    carcass or poultry carcass that was graded in Canada, or is derived
    from such a carcass, that activity was conducted by a grader in
    accordance with these Regulations;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case where any meat product that it contains
    was imported, it was imported by a licence holder in accordance with
    the provisions of the Act and these Regulations;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the meat product, or any meat
    product that it contains, is derived from food animals that were
    slaughtered in Canada, the food animals were slaughtered by a
    licence holder in accordance with the provisions of the Act and
    these Regulations;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the meat product complies with the standards that
    are set out in Volume 7 of the Standards of Identity Document and
    meets the requirements that are set out in paragraphs 8(1)(a) to (d)
    and in Division 7 of Part 6; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the inspection legend is applied or used in an
    establishment that is identified in the licence holder's licence,
    unless the meat product is intended to be exported and the
    inspection legend is applied to the conveyance or used in connection
    with the conveyance in which the meat product is exported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Edible meat products --- Figure 2 of
  Schedule 2

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licence holder or an inspector is authorized to
  apply the inspection legend that is set out in Figure 2 of Schedule 2
  to, and use it in connection with, an edible prepackaged meat product
  if the conditions set out in subsection (1) are met and the container
  of the meat product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a hermetically sealed package that is labelled
    in a legible and permanent manner so as to make it possible to
    identify the establishment that is identified in the licence
    holder's licence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a casing or bag that is closed by a clip, if the
    number identifying the licence holder's establishment is legibly
    engraved on the clip and is visible when the clip is closed; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} bears the number identifying the licence holder's
    establishment on the label except if it is shown on any part of the
    label that is applied or attached to the bottom of the container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- export under section 16

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of the export of a meat product under
  subsection 16(1), the requirements of subsections (1) and (2) must be
  met except any requirement that is set out in paragraph (1)(f) in the
  case where it is the unmet requirement referred to in subsection
  16(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- dressed beef carcass side

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(f), a licence holder is
  authorized to apply, after the post-mortem inspection or examination
  and before refrigeration, in the manner set out in paragraph 151(a) or
  (b), the inspection legend set out in Figure 1 of Schedule 2 to a
  dressed beef carcass side containing dorsal root ganglia if the
  carcass side has been clearly marked to identify it as containing
  dorsal root ganglia. The dorsal root ganglia must be removed from the
  carcass side before any meat product derived from it is identified as
  edible.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Processed egg products

**[[181]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181 .sectionLabel}** A licence holder or an
inspector is authorized to apply the inspection legend that is set out
in Figure 1 of Schedule 2 to, and use it in connection with, a
prepackaged processed egg product if the following conditions are met:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the processed egg product was manufactured,
  processed, treated, preserved, packaged or labelled by the licence
  holder in accordance with the provisions of the Act and these
  Regulations;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the processed egg product or any processed egg
  product that it contains was manufactured, processed, treated,
  preserved, packaged or labelled in Canada, that activity was conducted
  by a licence holder in accordance with the provisions of the Act and
  these Regulations;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the eggs from which the processed egg product is
  derived were processed, treated, preserved, graded, packaged or
  labelled in Canada, that activity was conducted by a licence holder in
  accordance with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the processed egg product complies with the standards
  that are set out in Volume 2 of the Standards of Identity Document and
  meets the requirements that are set out in paragraphs 8(1)(a) to (d)
  and in Division 4 of Part 6; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the inspection legend is applied or used in an
  establishment that is identified in the licence holder's licence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fish

**[[182]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-182 .sectionLabel}** A licence holder or an
inspector is authorized to apply the inspection legend that is set out
in Figure 1 or 2 of Schedule 2 to, and use it in connection with,
prepackaged fish if the following conditions are met:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the fish was manufactured, processed, treated or
  preserved by the licence holder in accordance with the provisions of
  the Act and these Regulations;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the fish or any fish that it contains was
  manufactured, processed, treated, preserved, graded, packaged or
  labelled in Canada, that activity was conducted by a licence holder in
  accordance with the provisions of the Act and these Regulations; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the fish complies with the standards that are set out
  in Volume 3 of the Standards of Identity Document and meets the
  requirements that are set out in paragraphs 8(1)(a) to (d) and in
  Division 5 of Part 6.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 14(E)](#1362166-1362766){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362166-1362766.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-182-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 182"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number identifying establishment

**[[183]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-183 .sectionLabel}** A licence holder or an
inspector who applies or uses the inspection legend that is set out in
Figure 1 of Schedule 2 must replace the numbers "00" with the number
identifying the licence holder's establishment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized use

- **[[184]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-184 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following persons are authorized to use the inspection legends that
  are set out in Figures 1 and 2 of Schedule 2:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} printers of labels, other than printers of official
    export labels, and manufacturers of packages, if the labels and
    packages that bear the inspection legend are provided to any person
    who is authorized under any of sections 180 to 182 to apply and use
    the inspection legend;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} printers of official export labels, if the labels
    that bear the inspection legend are provided to an inspector;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} publishers of documents on the subject of the
    inspection of meat products, processed egg products or fish;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} publishers of documents that advertise meat
    products, processed egg products or fish; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} manufacturers of stamps, if the stamps that bear
    the inspection legend are provided to any person who is authorized
    under any of sections 180 to 182 to apply and use the inspection
    legend.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advertising and sale

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person who is authorized under subsection (1) to
  use an inspection legend is also authorized to advertise the labels,
  packages, documents and stamps, as the case may be, which bear the
  inspection legend and to sell them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advertising and sale of certain foods

**[[185]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-185 .sectionLabel}** Any person is
authorized to advertise and sell a meat product, prepackaged processed
egg product or prepackaged fish to which an inspection legend has been
applied or in connection with which an inspection legend is used, if the
inspection legend was applied or is used in accordance with these
Regulations. This includes using an inspection legend set out in Figures
1 and 2 of Schedule 2 for advertising the meat product, prepackaged
processed egg product or prepackaged fish.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection legend used as label

**[[185.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-185.1 .sectionLabel}** In the case of the
inspection legend set out in Figure 1 of Schedule 2, if it is used or is
to be used as a label, the number identifying the licence holder's
establishment that replaces the numbers "00" must be in characters of at
least 1.6 mm in height.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 15](#1362168-1362770){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362168-1362770.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART 10]{.HLabel1}[Packaging]{.HTitleText1} {#h-845178 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845179 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for packages

**[[186]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186 .sectionLabel}** A prepackaged food that
is sent or conveyed from one province to another or that is imported or
exported must meet the following requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} its package

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} must be suitable for its intended use and
    appropriate for the food,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} must be capable of protecting the food against
    moisture, loss, damage, contamination and deterioration during
    normal handling, storing and conveying,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} must be clean and in a sanitary condition,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} must be of sound construction,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} must be free from odours that might affect the
    food,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} must not impart any undesirable substance to the
    food,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} must not have a design or mark, or be of a
    colour, that enhances the appearance of the food with respect to its
    quality or composition, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} must be new, in the case of

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} a liner that is used in connection with a
      processed egg product,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} a package of a processed egg product, if the
      package is made of corrugated fibreboard,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} an egg carton of eggs that are graded in
      accordance with these Regulations, and

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} a tray of eggs that are graded Canada A or Canada
      B that is made of molded pulp;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a processed egg product, its package
  must, if it has previously been used and is not constructed of
  corrosion-resistant material, be lined with a sanitary plastic or
  equivalent liner;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of eggs that are graded in accordance
  with these Regulations, its package must, if it is a plastic tray that
  has previously been used, be sanitized and dry before reuse; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of eggs that are graded Canada A or
  Canada B, its package must not have previously been used to package
  ungraded eggs or eggs that are graded Canada Nest Run.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Standard Container Sizes]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845198 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[187]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187 .sectionLabel}** The requirements of
this Division apply in respect of any food that is sent or conveyed from
one province to another or imported.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 16](#1362170-1362774){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362170-1362774.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-187-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 187"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Size corresponding to net quantity

- **[[188]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  container of a prepackaged food, including a hermetically sealed food
  container listed in the Standard Container Sizes Document, must be of
  a size that corresponds to a net quantity by weight or volume or of a
  maximum capacity by net weight, as the case may be, that is set out in
  that document and, if the hermetically sealed container is made of
  metal, it must be of the dimensions that correspond to the net
  quantity by volume that is set out in that document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- catch-weight foods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a
  consumer prepackaged catch-weight food if a label that bears the net
  weight for retail sale is applied or attached to the food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- consumer prepackaged
  foods

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a
  consumer prepackaged food that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} manufactured, prepared, produced, packaged or
    labelled for use by commercial or industrial enterprises or
    institutions without being sold by them as a consumer prepackaged
    food;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} manufactured, prepared, produced, packaged or
    labelled only for sale to or by a duty-free shop; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} distributed to one or more persons for no
    consideration.
