### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Standards of Fill for Processed Fruit or Vegetable Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845243 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[194]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-194 .sectionLabel}** The requirements of
this Division apply in respect of any foods that are sent or conveyed
from one province to another, imported or exported.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Frozen processed fruit or vegetable
products

**[[195]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-195 .sectionLabel}** At least 90% of the
volume capacity of the package of a frozen processed fruit or vegetable
product must be filled with the product.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-frozen processed fruit or vegetable
products

**[[196]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196 .sectionLabel}** The package of a
processed fruit or vegetable product that is not frozen

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} must be filled with as much of the product as its
  preparation allows; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} must not contain more syrup, brine, water or other
  liquid packaging medium than is necessary for the processing of the
  product.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hermetically sealed packages

**[[197]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-197 .sectionLabel}** Despite section 196, a
processed fruit or vegetable product that is in a hermetically sealed
package must meet the requirements for minimum drained weight and
average drained weight that are set out in the document entitled Minimum
Drained Weights and Average Drained Weights for Processed Fruit or
Vegetable Products in a Hermetically Sealed Package, prepared by the
Agency and published on its website, as amended from time to time.

## [PART 11]{.HLabel1}[Labelling]{.HTitleText1} {#h-845254 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845255 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845256 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[198]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-198 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[Canadian unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Canadian unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a unit of measurement
    that is set out in Schedule II to the [Weights and Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/W-6). ([unité canadienne]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[metric unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[metric unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a unit of measurement that
    is set out in Schedule I to the [Weights and Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/W-6). ([unité métrique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Subsection 6(1) of Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845261 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}False, misleading or deceptive labelling

- **[[199]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-199 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 6(1) of the Act, labelling a food in a
  manner that is false, misleading or deceptive or is likely to create
  an erroneous impression includes labelling a food with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any representation in which expressions, words,
    figures, depictions or symbols are used, arranged or shown in a
    manner that may reasonably be considered to qualify the declared net
    quantity of a consumer prepackaged food or that is likely to deceive
    with respect to the net quantity of a consumer prepackaged food; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any expression, word, number, depiction or symbol
    that may reasonably be considered to imply that a prepackaged food
    contains any matter that it does not in fact contain or that it does
    not contain any matter that it does in fact contain.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}False, misleading or deceptive selling,
  importing and advertising

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 6(1) of the Act,
  selling, importing or advertising a food in a manner that is false,
  misleading or deceptive or is likely to create an erroneous impression
  includes selling, importing or advertising

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a consumer prepackaged food that is labelled in the
    manner set out in paragraph (1)(a); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prepackaged food that is labelled in the manner
    set out in paragraph (1)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration of net quantity ---
  labelling

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 6(1) of the Act,
  labelling a consumer prepackaged food with a declaration of net
  quantity does not constitute labelling a food in a manner that is
  false, misleading or deceptive if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the declaration meets any requirements under the
    Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the actual net quantity of the food is, subject to
    the tolerance provided under subsection (5), not less than its
    declared net quantity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration of net quantity ---
  selling, importing or advertising

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 6(1) of the Act,
  selling, importing or advertising a consumer prepackaged food that is
  labelled with a declaration of net quantity does not constitute
  selling, importing or advertising a food in a manner that is false,
  misleading or deceptive if the conditions set out in paragraphs (3)(a)
  and (b) are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tolerance --- paragraph (3)(b)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (3)(b), the tolerance
  for a declared net quantity that is set out in column 1 of the
  applicable table in Schedule 4 is the tolerance that is set out in
  column 2 or 3 for that net quantity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 17](#1362172-1362798){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362172-1362798.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-199-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 199"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection --- net quantity

- **[[200]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-200 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspection of a lot of food, each unit of which purports to contain
  the same net quantity, by an inspector for the purpose of determining
  whether the lot meets the conditions of paragraphs 199(3)(a) and (b)
  must be conducted by collecting and examining a sample from the lot.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of units collected --- Schedule
  5

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), if a lot contains the
  number of units set out in column 1 of Part 1 of Schedule 5, the
  inspector must collect from the lot at least the number of units set
  out in column 2 and the units collected constitute the sample referred
  to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Destruction of units

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, for the purpose of determining the net quantity,
  other than for establishing the weight of the container, it is
  necessary to destroy a certain number of units in the lot, an
  inspector must not collect a number of units intended to be destroyed
  that is more than 10% of the total number of units in the lot or less
  than one unit, and the units collected constitute the sample referred
  to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of inspector

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The lot from which a sample was collected and
  examined by an inspector does not meet the conditions of paragraphs
  199(3)(a) and (b) if the inspector determines that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the weighted average quantity of the units in the
    sample, as determined by the formula set out in Part 2 of Schedule
    5, is less than the declared net quantity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of units in the sample that contain less
    than the declared net quantity by more than the prescribed tolerance
    set out in Schedule 4 for that quantity is equal to or greater than
    the number set out in column 2 of Part 4 of Schedule 5 for the
    sample size set out in column 1 of that Part; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} two or more units in the sample contain less than
    the declared net quantity by more than twice the prescribed
    tolerance set out in Schedule 4 for that quantity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liquid consumer prepackaged food

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an inspection of a consumer
  prepackaged food that consists of a liquid, the net quantity of the
  food must be determined on the basis of the assumption that the liquid
  is at a temperature of 20°C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Frozen liquid consumer prepackaged food

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an inspection of a consumer
  prepackaged food that consists of a frozen liquid food and that is
  normally sold and consumed in a frozen state, the net quantity of the
  food must be determined when the food is in a frozen state.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Standards Prescribed for Food]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845293 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common names

**[[201]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-201 .sectionLabel}** A food, whether
prepackaged or not, that is sent or conveyed from one province to
another or that is imported or exported, and whose label bears a common
name printed in boldface type, but not in italics, in the Standards of
Identity Document must meet any standard that applies in respect of that
common name.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Icewine

**[[202]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited for a
person to sell a consumer prepackaged food whose label bears the word or
expression "icewine" or "ice wine" or "ice-wine" or "[vin de
glace]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or any similar word or expression, or any
abbreviation of, symbol for or phonetic rendering of any of those words
or expressions, unless the food meets the standard set out in Volume 8
of the Standards of Identity Document.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845298 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance with requirements of this Part

- **[[203]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-203 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  item of information that is shown on the label of a prepackaged food
  must meet any requirement of this Part that applies to that item of
  information, even if these Regulations do not require the food to be
  labelled with that item of information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an item of information referred to
    in Division 2, any prepackaged food that is sold in Canada and any
    prepackaged food that is sent or conveyed from one province to
    another or that is imported; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an item of information referred to
    in these Regulations, other than in Division 2, any food that is
    sent or conveyed from one province to another or that is imported or
    exported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application --- paragraphs 218(1)(a)
  and (b)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an item of information referred to in
  paragraphs 218(1)(a) and (b), subsection (1) also applies in respect
  of any dairy products, eggs, processed egg products, fish, fresh
  fruits or vegetables, processed fruit or vegetable products, honey,
  maple products or meat products that are prepackaged and exported.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of word "[classifié]{.otherLang
lang="fr"}"

**[[204]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-204 .sectionLabel}** If a provision of these
Regulations requires the word "[classifié]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" to be
shown on a label, the word "[classé]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" may be used
in its place.

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[Official Languages]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845310 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged food

- **[[205]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  information that is required by these Regulations to be shown on the
  label of a prepackaged food, other than a consumer prepackaged food,
  and that is not information referred to in subsection (3) must be
  shown on the label in at least one official language.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- words or expressions in
  quotation marks

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 48(2), section 254 and
  subsections 256(1) and 333(2), both an English and a French word or
  expression that appears in quotation marks must be shown on the label
  of a prepackaged food except if the label of that food is authorized
  to show information in only one official language under subsections
  B.01.012(2) to (10) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- both official languages

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The label of a prepackaged food must bear, in both
  official languages,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the size designation of eggs that are graded Canada
    A referred to in section 316; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the colour class of maple syrup that is graded
    Canada Grade A or graded Grade A referred to in section 325.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 18](#1362177-1362802){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362177-1362802.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 205"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged food

- **[[206]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-206 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  information that is required by these Regulations to be shown on the
  label of a consumer prepackaged food must be shown in both official
  languages, except in the case where the label of that food is
  authorized to show information in only one official language under
  subsections B.01.012(2) to (10) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Words or expressions in quotation marks

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A provision of these Regulations --- other than
  paragraphs 110(a) and 111(a) --- that requires a consumer prepackaged
  food to be labelled with words or expressions that appear in quotation
  marks must be read to require both an English word or expression and a
  French word or expression to be shown on the label of the food, except
  in the case where the label of that food is authorized to show
  information in only one official language under subsections
  B.01.012(2) to (10) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- only one official
  language

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the grade name may be shown
  on the label of consumer prepackaged fish in only one official
  language.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Modifications

**[[207]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-207 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsections 205(2) and 206(1) and (2),

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the expression "principal display panel" in
  subsections B.01.012(8) and (10) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) has the same meaning as
  in section 1 of these Regulations;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reference to "these Regulations" in subsections
  B.01.012(2), (3), (7) and (8) of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) must be read as a
  reference to "Part 11 of the [Safe Food for Canadians
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-108)"; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a reference to "manufactured, processed, produced or
  packaged", "manufactured, processed or packaged" or "manufactured,
  processed, produced or packaged for resale" in the definitions [[local
  food]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[specialty
  food]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection B.01.012(1) of the [Food
  and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) and in
  subsection B.01.012(9) of those Regulations must be read as a
  reference to "manufactured, processed, treated, preserved, produced or
  packaged".

#### [SUBDIVISION F]{.HLabel3}[Legibility and Type Size]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845332 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Legibility

**[[208]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-208 .sectionLabel}** Any information that a
label is required by these Regulations to bear must be clearly and
prominently shown and readily discernible to the purchaser under the
customary conditions of purchase and use.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 19](#1362179-1362806){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362179-1362806.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-208-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 208"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Upper or lower case

**[[209]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209 .sectionLabel}** If a word or expression
that appears in quotation marks in these Regulations is required to be
shown on a label, it may, unless otherwise provided, be shown in upper
or lower case, or both, so long as it meets the legibility and character
height requirements of these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

- **[[210]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-210 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section applies unless another provision of this Part specifies a
  character height for a particular item of information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged food

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information that a label of a consumer
  prepackaged food is required by this Part to bear must be shown in
  characters that are at least 1.6 mm in height.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} That information, other than the declaration of net
  quantity, may be shown in characters that are at least 0.8 mm in
  height if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information that a label is required by
    Division 2 to bear is shown on the principal display panel; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the area of the principal display surface is 10
    cm^2^ or less.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measurement of type size

**[[211]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-211 .sectionLabel}** The height of the
characters in words shown on a label must be determined by measuring

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the height of an upper case letter, if the words are
  shown in upper case only; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the height of the lower case letter "o", if the words
  are shown in lower case or in both upper and lower case.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Requirements Applicable to Prepackaged Food]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845350 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Application of this Division]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845351 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale, interprovincial trade or import

- **[[212]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-212 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  requirements of this Division apply in respect of any prepackaged food
  that is sold in Canada, sent or conveyed from one province to another
  or imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Export

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 217, paragraphs 218(1)(a) and (b) and section
  225 also apply in respect of any dairy products, eggs, processed egg
  products, fish, fresh fruits or vegetables, processed fruit or
  vegetable products, honey, maple products or meat products that are
  prepackaged and exported.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- sections 214 and 217

**[[213]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-213 .sectionLabel}** Sections 214 and 217 do
not apply in respect of any prepackaged foods that are

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} confections that are sold individually, commonly
  known as one-bite confections;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fresh fruits or vegetables that are packaged in a
  wrapper, or a confining band, that is less than 13 mm in width; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fresh fruits or vegetables that are packaged in a
  protective wrapper, or a protective bag, that is transparent and on
  which no information is shown other than a price, bar code, number
  code, environmental statement or product treatment symbol.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Sale and Advertisement]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845362 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale --- prepackaged food

**[[214]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-214 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited for a
person to sell a prepackaged food unless a label that meets the
requirements of Divisions 1 and 2 is applied or attached to the
prepackaged food in the manner set out in these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advertising --- consumer prepackaged food

**[[215]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-215 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited for a
person to advertise a consumer prepackaged food unless a label is
applied or attached to the food in the manner set out in Divisions 1 and
2.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations relating to net quantity

**[[216]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-216 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited for a
person, in advertising a consumer prepackaged food, to make any
representation with respect to the net quantity of the food except in
the manner set out in Divisions 1 and 2.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale of prepackaged food after expiration
date

- **[[216.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-216.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for a person to
  sell a prepackaged food after the expiration date that is required to
  be shown on the prepackaged food's label under Divisions 24 and 25 of
  Part B of the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- unpackaged food or used
  as ingredient

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for a person to sell the food
  referred to in subsection (1) as an unpackaged food or use it as an
  ingredient in another food.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 20](#1362181-1362813){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362181-1362813.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Label Required]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845369 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged food

**[[217]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-217 .sectionLabel}** A prepackaged food must
have a label that meets the requirements of these Regulations applied or
attached to it in the manner set out in these Regulations.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845372 .Subheading}

##### [Prepackaged Foods]{.HTitleText4} {#h-845373 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged foods --- label

- **[[218]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-218
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise provided by this
  Part, a label that is applied or attached to a prepackaged food must
  bear

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the common name of the food, shown on the principal
    display panel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and principal place of business of the
    person by or for whom the food was manufactured, prepared, produced,
    stored, packaged or labelled, on any part of the label other than
    any part that is applied or attached to the bottom of the container
    of the food; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other information that is required to be shown
    on the label of the prepackaged food in accordance with the
    requirements of the [Food and Drug
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) for any [[prepackaged
    product]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection B.01.001(1)
    of those Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- name and principal place
  of business

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information referred to in paragraph (1)(b) may
  be shown on any part of the label that is applied or attached to the
  bottom of the container if that information is also shown on a part of
  the label that is not applied or attached to the bottom of the
  container.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- common name

- **[[219]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-219 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following foods are not required to be labelled with the common name:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables that are
    packaged in such a manner that the fresh fruits or vegetables are
    visible and identifiable in the container; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} consumer prepackaged fresh apples that are packaged
    in such a manner that the name of their variety is shown on any part
    of the label, except if that name is shown on any part that is
    applied to the bottom of the container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[apple]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In paragraph (1)(b), [[apple]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means a fresh apple for which a grade is prescribed by these
  Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- name and principal place of
business

**[[220]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-220 .sectionLabel}** Consumer prepackaged
fresh fruits or vegetables that are packaged at retail in such a manner
that the fresh fruits or vegetables are visible and identifiable in the
container are not required to be labelled with the name and principal
place of business referred to in paragraph 218(1)(b).

##### [Consumer Prepackaged Foods]{.HTitleText4} {#h-845391 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged food --- declaration
of net quantity

**[[221]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-221 .sectionLabel}** A label that is applied
or attached to a consumer prepackaged food must bear on the principal
display panel a declaration of net quantity of the food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of manufacture --- label or
container

**[[222]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-222 .sectionLabel}** If the label that is
applied or attached to a consumer prepackaged food bears any reference,
direct or indirect, to the place of manufacture of the label or
container, the reference to that place must be accompanied by an
additional statement that indicates that the reference relates only to
the place of manufacture of the label or container.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Name of importer

- **[[223]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-223 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  consumer prepackaged food was wholly manufactured, processed or
  produced in a foreign state and the name and principal place of
  business of the person in Canada for whom it was manufactured,
  processed or produced or the person by whom it was stored, packaged or
  labelled in Canada is shown on its label, that information must be
  preceded by the expressions "Imported by" and "[importé
  par]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or "Imported for" and "[importé
  pour]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", as the case may be, unless the
  geographic origin of the consumer prepackaged food is shown on the
  label in accordance with subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Food packaged in Canada

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a food that was wholly manufactured, processed,
  produced in a foreign state is packaged in Canada, other than at
  retail, and the name and principal place of business of the person in
  Canada for whom the food was manufactured, processed, produced or
  packaged is shown on the label that is applied or attached to the
  resulting consumer prepackaged food, that information must be preceded
  by the expressions "Imported by" and "[importé par]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}" or "Imported for" and "[importé pour]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}", as the case may be, unless the geographic origin of the
  food is shown on the label in accordance with subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Geographic origin

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The geographic origin of a food must, subject to the
  requirements of any other federal or provincial law, be shown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in close proximity to the name and principal place
    of business of the person by or for whom the food was manufactured,
    processed or produced; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in characters of at least the same height as those
    in which the information referred to in paragraph (a) is shown.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Flavouring ingredient

- **[[224]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-224 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  flavouring ingredient is added to a consumer prepackaged food, the
  label that is applied or attached to the consumer prepackaged food
  must bear a statement that indicates that the flavouring ingredient is
  imitation, artificial or simulated if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ingredient is not derived from a natural
    substance such as meat, fish, poultry, fruits, vegetables, edible
    yeast, herbs, spices, bark, buds, roots, leaves or other plant
    material; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the label bears a pictorial representation that
    suggests the natural food flavour that corresponds to the added
    flavouring ingredient.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The statement must be shown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on or in close proximity to the pictorial
    representation, if the representation is shown on the principal
    display panel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the principal display panel, in close proximity
    to the common name, if the pictorial representation is shown on a
    part of the label other than the principal display panel; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} on or in close proximity to the portion of the
    pictorial representation shown on the principal display panel, if
    the representation is shown on the principal display panel and on
    another part of the label.

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[Requirement to Apply or Attach Label]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845415 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged food

**[[225]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-225 .sectionLabel}** The label of a
prepackaged food must be applied or attached in such a manner that the
label is still applied or attached at the time it is sold.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged food --- container

**[[226]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-226 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 228,
the label of a consumer prepackaged food that is for sale must be
applied or attached to the container in accordance with section 227.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 21](#1362183-1362817){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362183-1362817.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-226-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 226"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Principal display surface

- **[[227]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-227 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  or part of the label of a consumer prepackaged food must be applied to
  the principal display surface.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ornamental container

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), in the case of a consumer
  prepackaged food whose container is an ornamental container, the label
  may be applied to the bottom of the container or attached to the
  container.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Display card

**[[228]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-228 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
consumer prepackaged food whose container is mounted on a display card,
the label may be applied to the surface of the display card that is
displayed or visible under customary conditions of sale or use.

#### [SUBDIVISION F]{.HLabel3}[Type Size --- Specific Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845427 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged food

- **[[229]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-229 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  the case of the label of a consumer prepackaged food, the following
  must be shown in characters of at least the minimum character height
  that is set out in column 2 of Schedule 6 for the area of a principal
  display surface that is set out in column 1:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the numerical quantity in the declaration of net
    quantity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the statement referred to in section 224 that
    indicates that a flavouring ingredient is imitation, artificial or
    simulated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Container mounted on display card ---
  specific case

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), in the case of a
  container that is mounted on a display card, the reference to "Area of
  Principal Display Surface" in Schedule 6 must be read as "Total Area
  of the Surface of the Display Card that is Displayed or Visible under
  Customary Conditions of Sale or Use", if the information is shown on a
  label that is applied to all or part of that surface.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged wine --- specific
  case

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(a), in the case of consumer
  prepackaged wine, the numerical quantity in the declaration of net
  quantity may, if the net quantity is 750 mL, the container is no
  taller than 360 mm and the area of the principal display surface is
  greater than 258 cm^2^, be shown in characters of a height less than
  the minimum character height that is set out in column 2 of Schedule
  6, but must be shown in characters that are at least 3.3 mm in height.

#### [SUBDIVISION G]{.HLabel3}[Manner of Showing Declaration of Net Quantity]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845437 .Subheading}

##### [Legibility]{.HTitleText4} {#h-845438 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged food

**[[230]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-230 .sectionLabel}** The declaration of net
quantity that is shown on the label of a consumer prepackaged food must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be in distinct contrast to any other information or
  pictorial representation on the label; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} show the numerical quantity in boldface type.

##### [Declaration by Volume, Weight or Numerical Count]{.HTitleText4} {#h-845443 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General requirements

**[[231]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-231 .sectionLabel}** The declaration of net
quantity of a consumer prepackaged food must be shown

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged food that is
  listed in the document entitled Units of Measurement for the Net
  Quantity Declaration of Certain Foods, prepared by the Agency and
  published on its website, as amended from time to time, by volume,
  weight or numerical count in accordance with that document; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged food that is
  not listed in the document referred to in paragraph (a),

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the food is liquid, gas or viscous, by food
    volume, or, if the food is solid, by weight, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the established trade practice in respect of
    the food is to show its net quantity in a manner that is different
    than what is required by subparagraph (i), in accordance with that
    established trade practice.

##### [Metric Units]{.HTitleText4} {#h-845450 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted units of measurement

**[[232]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232 .sectionLabel}** The declaration of net
quantity of a consumer prepackaged food must be shown in metric units,
unless otherwise provided by these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Millilitres, litres, grams and kilograms

- **[[233]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  metric units that must be shown in a declaration of net quantity of a
  consumer prepackaged food must be in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} millilitres, if the net volume of the food is less
    than 1 000 mL;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} litres, if the net volume of the food is 1 000 mL
    or more;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} grams, if the net weight of the food is less than
    1 000 g; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} kilograms, if the net weight of the food is 1 000 g
    or more.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Half-litre or half-kilogram

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraphs (1)(a) and (c), 500 mL may be
  shown as 0.5 L and 500 g may be shown as 0.5 kg.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decimal fraction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case referred to in paragraph (1)(c), the net
  weight may be shown as a decimal fraction of a kilogram if the food is
  packaged from bulk at retail or is a catch-weight food that is sold by
  a retailer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Number of digits

- **[[234]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-234 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the declaration of net quantity of a consumer prepackaged food is
  shown in metric units, it must be shown in the decimal system to three
  figures.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net quantity below 100 g or mL

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if the net quantity is below
  100 g or 100 mL, it may be shown to two figures.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Zero as final decimal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), any final zero
  appearing to the right of the decimal point is not required to be
  shown.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity less than one

**[[235]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-235 .sectionLabel}** If the declaration of
net quantity of a consumer prepackaged food is shown in metric units and
the quantity is less than one metric unit, the quantity must be shown

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in words; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the decimal system, with a zero preceding the
  decimal point.

##### [Metric Units and Canadian Units]{.HTitleText4} {#h-845475 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grouping

**[[236]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-236 .sectionLabel}** If the declaration of
net quantity of a consumer prepackaged food is shown in metric units and
Canadian units, those units must be grouped together, except that any
symbol or pictogram that is shown in accordance with the [Canada
Consumer Product Safety Act](/eng/acts/C-1.68) or any regulations made
under that Act may be shown between those units.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canadian units of volume

- **[[237]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the declaration of net quantity of a consumer prepackaged food whose
  volume is less than one gallon includes Canadian units, those units
  must be in fluid ounces, except that 20 fluid ounces may be shown as
  one pint, 40 fluid ounces as one quart, 60 fluid ounces as three
  pints, 80 fluid ounces as two quarts or as one-half gallon and 120
  fluid ounces as three quarts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Oysters

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), in the case of oysters that
  are sold in the shell, other than those in a hermetically sealed
  package, the declaration of net quantity must, if shown by volume, be
  shown in bushels or pecks.

**[[238]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-238 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 22\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-238-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 238"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Individually Packaged Food Sold as One Unit and Servings]{.HTitleText4} {#h-845485 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individually packaged food sold as one
unit

- **[[239]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-239 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  consumer prepackaged food is sold as one unit but consists of two or
  more individually packaged foods that are labelled with the
  information required for a consumer prepackaged food, the declaration
  of net quantity of the consumer prepackaged food being sold as one
  unit must show

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of individually packaged foods in each
    class of food, as well as the common name of the food in each class;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total net quantity of the individually packaged
    foods in each class, or the net quantity of each identical
    individually packaged food in each class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dairy products sold as one unit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in the case of a dairy
  product that is sold as one unit but consists of two or more
  individual packages of butter patties, butter reddies or other related
  dairy products if the total net quantity of the individual packages is
  20 g or less.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged honey

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of prepackaged honey that is graded and
  that is sold as one unit but that consists of two or more individual
  packages, the label must bear the number of those packages and the net
  quantity of each, in metric units.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 23](#1362186-1362831){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362186-1362831.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-239-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 239"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- representation respecting
number of servings

**[[240]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-240 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited for a
person to apply or attach to any consumer prepackaged food a label that
bears any representation with respect to the number of servings
contained in the consumer prepackaged food unless the label bears a
declaration of net quantity of each serving in accordance with section
241.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Servings

- **[[241]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  declaration of net quantity of a serving of a consumer prepackaged
  food must be shown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in close proximity to the representation with
    respect to the number of servings contained in the consumer
    prepackaged food; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in characters of the same height as those in which
    that representation is shown.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Units

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The declaration of net quantity of a serving must be
  shown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with the requirements of sections 231
    and 233 to 237 respecting the declaration of net quantity of the
    food; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in metric units, unless otherwise provided by these
    Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation --- cups or tablespoons

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the representation with respect to the number of
  servings is made in terms of cups or tablespoons,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one cup is equivalent to 250 mL and one tablespoon
    is equivalent to 15 mL; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the declaration of net quantity is not required to
    meet the requirements of paragraph (2)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- number of portions
  indicated

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in the case of a
  prepackaged food that bears a nutrition facts table that contains the
  serving size of the food in accordance with the [Food and Drug
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 24](#1362188-1362835){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362188-1362835.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-241-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 241"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net quantity in advertisement

**[[241.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241.1 .sectionLabel}** If the declaration
of net quantity of a consumer prepackaged food or of a serving of the
food is shown in metric units and Canadian units, the net quantity in an
advertisement may be shown in either a metric unit or a Canadian unit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 25](#1362190-1362839){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362190-1362839.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions to declaration of net quantity

**[[241.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241.2 .sectionLabel}** In addition to the
exceptions in section 299, the following consumer prepackaged foods are
not required to be labelled with the declaration of net quantity
referred to in section 221:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual portion of a food that is prepared by a
  commissary and sold by an automatic vending machine or a mobile
  canteen;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a catch-weight food that is sold to a retailer;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an individual portion of a food that is sold by a
  restaurant or other commercial enterprise if it is served with meals
  or snacks; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} consumer prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables sold
  in a retail store that are packaged in an unsealed transparent
  protective bag, if sold by weight.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 25](#1362190-1362847){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362190-1362847.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged raspberries or
strawberries

**[[241.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241.3 .sectionLabel}** The requirements of
sections 216, 221 and 240 do not apply in respect of consumer
prepackaged raspberries or strawberries that are packaged in the field
in a container that has a capacity of 1.14 L or less.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 25](#1362190-1362851){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362190-1362851.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individually measured food

- **[[241.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A declaration of net quantity of a
  consumer prepackaged food that is an individually measured food is not
  required to meet the legibility and character height requirements of
  subsection 210(2), paragraph 229(1)(a), subsections 229(2) and (3) and
  paragraph 230(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Food packaged from bulk

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If it is clearly shown on the principal display panel
  in Canadian units, the declaration of net quantity of a consumer
  prepackaged food, other than an individually measured food, that is
  packaged from bulk at retail, is not required to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} meet the legibility and character height
    requirements of subsection 210(2), paragraph 229(1)(a), subsections
    229(2) and (3) and paragraph 230(b); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be shown in metric units.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[individually
  measured]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[individually
  measured]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with respect to a food, that the
  food is measured and packaged in a manner other than in accordance
  with a predetermined fixed quantity and, as a result, is sold in
  varying quantities.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 25](#1362190-1362862){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362190-1362862.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individually packaged food sold as one
unit

**[[241.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241.5 .sectionLabel}** Sections 221, 239
and 240 do not apply to the label of a consumer prepackaged food that
consists of six or less identical individually packaged foods but is
sold as one unit if each of the individually packaged foods is labelled
with the information required by this Part and that information is
clearly visible at the time of sale.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 25](#1362190-1362866){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362190-1362866.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Specific Requirements for Certain Foods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845505 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Application of Division]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845506 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interprovincial trade, import and export

**[[242]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-242 .sectionLabel}** The requirements of
this Division apply in respect of any food that is sent or conveyed from
one province to another, imported or exported.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Declaration of Net Quantity]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845509 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- consumer prepackaged food

**[[243]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-243 .sectionLabel}** The requirements
relating to the declaration of net quantity that are set out in this
Division do not apply in respect of a consumer prepackaged food.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration of net quantity

**[[244]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244 .sectionLabel}** Any declaration of net
quantity that is required by this Division must be shown by volume,
weight or numerical count in accordance with the document entitled Units
of Measurement for the Net Quantity Declaration of Certain Foods,
prepared by the Agency and published on its website, as amended from
time to time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration of net quantity ---
prepackaged foods

**[[244.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244.1 .sectionLabel}** The label of the
following prepackaged foods must bear a declaration of net quantity:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} dairy products;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} eggs graded in accordance with these Regulations;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fish;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} fresh fruits or vegetables;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} processed fruit or vegetable products;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} honey graded in accordance with these Regulations;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} maple products, except maple syrup that is not graded
  in accordance with these Regulations; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} edible meat products.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 26](#1362192-1362878){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362192-1362878.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Metric units

**[[244.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244.2 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
provided by these Regulations, the declaration of net quantity must be
shown in metric units.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 26](#1362192-1362882){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362192-1362882.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

**[[244.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244.3 .sectionLabel}** Despite section
244.2, the declaration of net quantity must be,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a prepackaged dairy product,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in metric units or Canadian units, or both (in
    which case the units must be grouped together), if a standard is set
    out in Volume 1 of the Standards of Identity Document for the dairy
    product, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in metric units, if no standard is set out in
    Volume 1 of the Standards of Identity Document for the dairy
    product; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of fresh fruits or vegetables, in metric
  units or Canadian units, or both (in which case the units must be
  grouped together), unless the declaration of net quantity is shown by
  numerical count.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 26](#1362192-1362890){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362192-1362890.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

**[[244.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244.4 .sectionLabel}** The declaration of
net quantity must be shown on the principal display panel of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prepackaged dairy products;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fish that is in a hermetically sealed package and
  commercially sterile;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prepackaged processed fruit or vegetable products;
  and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} prepackaged edible meat products.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 26](#1362192-1362898){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362192-1362898.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged edible meat products

**[[244.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244.5 .sectionLabel}** In the case of
prepackaged edible meat products, the declaration of net quantity must
be shown in the manner required by sections 233 to 236 and 239 as if it
were a consumer prepackaged food.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 26](#1362192-1362902){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362192-1362902.html"
  role="button"}
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Location of Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845514 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Food or container

- **[[245]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-245 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  label that bears the information required by this Division in respect
  of a food must be applied or attached

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a prepackaged food, to its
    container; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of food that is not prepackaged, to the
    food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Any part of label

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information may be shown on any part of the
  label, unless otherwise provided by this Division in respect of the
  food.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bottom of food or container

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), if the information is shown
  on any part of the label that is applied or attached to the bottom of
  the prepackaged food or container, that information must also be shown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on any part of the label where it is required to be
    shown under another provision of this Division in respect of the
    food; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (a) does not apply, on any part of the
    label that is not applied or attached to the bottom of the food or
    container.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[Dairy Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845526 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged dairy products

**[[246]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-246 .sectionLabel}** The principal display
panel of a prepackaged dairy product must bear

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of butter, calorie-reduced butter, light
  butter or lite butter, dairy spread and whey butter, the following
  words or expressions:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} "Cultured" or "[de culture]{.otherLang lang="fr"}",
    preceding the common name in English or following the common name in
    French, if the dairy product has been prepared from cream to which a
    bacterial culture has been added,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} "Whipped" or "[fouetté]{.otherLang lang="fr"}",
    preceding the common name in English or following the common name in
    French, if the dairy product has had air or inert gas uniformly
    incorporated into it as a result of whipping,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} "Unsalted" or "[non salé]{.otherLang lang="fr"}",
    in close proximity to the common name, if the dairy product is
    unsalted and has not been cultured, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} "Salted" or "[salé]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", in
    close proximity to the common name, if the dairy product is salted
    and has been cultured;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a combination of skim milk powder and
  whey powder, the percentage of each powder; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of partly skimmed milk powder, dairy
  spread and calorie-reduced butter, the percentage of milk fat.

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-144, s. 27\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 27](#1362194-1362907){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362194-1362907.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-246-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 246"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged dairy products --- not
consumer prepackaged

**[[247]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247 .sectionLabel}** The principal display
panel of a prepackaged dairy product, other than a consumer prepackaged
dairy product, must bear

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cheese in its original shape, made
  from pasteurized milk, the word "Pasteurized" or
  "[pasteurisé]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", unless the list of ingredients
  indicates that the cheese is made from pasteurized milk;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of buttermilk powder, the percentage of
  milk fat;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of skim milk powder that has a whey
  protein nitrogen content of not less than 6.0 mg/g, the expression
  "Low Heat" or "Low Temperature" or "[basse température]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}" or the abbreviation "Low Temp." or "[basse
  temp.]{.otherLang lang="fr"}"; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of skim milk powder that has a whey
  protein nitrogen content of not more than 1.5 mg/g, the expression
  "High Heat" or "High Temperature" or "[haute température]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}" or the abbreviation "High Temp." or "[haute
  temp.]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged dairy products

**[[248]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248 .sectionLabel}** The principal display
panel of a consumer prepackaged dairy product must bear

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cheese, except cottage cheese and
  creamed cottage cheese, and in the case of cheese curd, the percentage
  of moisture;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of cheese, cheese curd and evaporated
  partly skimmed milk or concentrated partly skimmed milk, the
  percentage of milk fat; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a dairy product that consists of or
  was manufactured or prepared wholly or partly from milk that is
  obtained from an animal other than a cow, the source of the milk,
  unless the source is indicated in the common name.

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-144, s. 28\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 28](#1362195-1362912){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362195-1362912.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-248-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 248"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged cheese

- **[[249]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-249 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  principal display panel of a consumer prepackaged cheese must bear

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the relative firmness of the cheese;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of a soft white cheese, the
    principal ripening characteristic of the cheese; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a mixture of grated or shredded
    cheeses, the varieties of the cheeses, in descending order of their
    proportion in the mixture.

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-144, s. 29\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cheddar cheese;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cream cheese with or without added ingredients;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} cream cheese spread with or without added
    ingredients;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} whey cheese;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} processed cheese with or without added ingredients;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} processed cheese food with or without added
    ingredients;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} processed cheese spread with or without added
    ingredients;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} cold-pack cheese with or without added ingredients;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} cold-pack cheese food with or without added
    ingredients;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} cottage cheese;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} creamed cottage cheese; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} any cheese that is listed in Part I or II of the
    table to section 7.4.1 of Volume 7 of the Food Compositional
    Standards Document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Relative firmness

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The relative firmness of the cheese must be
  identified by the expressions that correspond to the applicable
  moisture on fat-free basis content set out in the Descriptive Words,
  Expressions and Identification Names Document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Principal ripening characteristic

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The principal ripening characteristic of the cheese
  must be identified by the words or expressions that correspond to the
  applicable ripening process set out in the Descriptive Words,
  Expressions and Identification Names Document.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 29](#1362196-1362921){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362196-1362921.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2024-244, s. 165](#1497507-1505148){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1497507-1505148.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-249-20220621.html "Link to previous version of section 249"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported dairy products

- **[[250]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-250 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of the following dairy products must bear the expression
  "Product of" or "[produit de]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", followed by the
  name of the foreign state of origin:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an imported prepackaged dairy product; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a consumer prepackaged cheese that is packaged in
    Canada from imported bulk cheese for which a standard is set out in
    Volume 1 of the Standards of Identity Document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Principal display panel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of the cheese referred to in paragraph
  (1)(b), the information must be shown on the principal display panel.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[251]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251 .sectionLabel}** Subsection 239(2) and
sections 244.3, 246, 248 and 250 do not apply in respect of an
individual portion of a consumer prepackaged dairy product that is sold

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by means of an automatic vending machine or mobile
  canteen; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} by a restaurant or other commercial enterprise if it
  is served with meals or snacks.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 30](#1362201-1362924){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362201-1362924.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-251-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 251"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[252]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 31\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-252-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 252"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

**[[253]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-253 .sectionLabel}** The information that is
required by section 250 must be shown in boldface type in characters
that are at least 16 mm in height, in the case of a prepackaged dairy
product other than a consumer prepackaged dairy product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 31](#1362203-1362928){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362203-1362928.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-253-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 253"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[Eggs]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845600 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Graded eggs

**[[254]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-254 .sectionLabel}** The label of
prepackaged eggs that are graded in accordance with these Regulations
and that are pasteurized in the shell must bear the words "Pasteurized"
and "[pasteurisé]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", as well as the expressions
"Graded Canada A Before Pasteurization" and "[classifié Canada A avant
pasteurisation]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or the expressions "Graded Grade
A Before Pasteurization" and "[classifié catégorie A avant
pasteurisation]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 32](#1362205-1362934){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362205-1362934.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-254-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 254"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[255]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-255 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 32\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-255-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 255"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported eggs

- **[[256]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-256 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of imported prepackaged eggs must bear the expressions "Product
  of" and "[produit de]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", followed by the name of
  the foreign state of origin.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Location and type size

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} That information must be shown in characters that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a tray with an overwrap or an egg
    carton, on the top or side of the tray or egg carton, at least 1.5
    mm in height; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a container other than a tray with
    an overwrap or an egg carton, at least 6 mm in height.

**[[257]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-257 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 33\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-257-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 257"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION F]{.HLabel3}[Processed Egg Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845621 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged processed egg products

**[[258]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-258 .sectionLabel}** The label of a
prepackaged processed egg product must bear

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inspection legend set out in Figure 1 of Schedule
  2, if the prepackaged processed egg product is sent or conveyed from
  one province to another or exported; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the official inspection mark of the foreign state of
  origin, if the prepackaged processed egg product is imported.

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-144, s. 34\]]{.Repealed}

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-144, s. 34\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 34](#1362208-1362941){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362208-1362941.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-258-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 258"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported prepackaged processed egg
products

**[[259]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-259 .sectionLabel}** The label of an
imported prepackaged processed egg product must also bear the expression
"Product of" or "[produit de]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", followed by the
name of the foreign state of origin.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged dried egg blends

**[[260]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-260 .sectionLabel}** The label of the
following prepackaged processed egg products must bear the expression
"Product of Canada and" or "[produit du Canada et]{.otherLang
lang="fr"}", followed by the name of the foreign state of origin:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} dried whole egg that is a blend of imported and
  Canadian dried whole egg;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} dried yolk that is a blend of imported and Canadian
  dried yolk; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} dried egg white or dried albumen that is a blend of
  imported and Canadian dried egg white or dried albumen.

#### [SUBDIVISION G]{.HLabel3}[Fish]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845635 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[salted
fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[261]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-261 .sectionLabel}** In this Subdivision,
[[salted fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fish of the
[Gadidae]{.otherLang lang="la"} family that has been preserved by salt
and that has a salt content of 12% or more by wet weight and a moisture
content of not more than 65%.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 35](#1362209-1362945){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362209-1362945.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-261-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 261"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged fish

- **[[262]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of prepackaged fish must bear

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of bivalve molluscs in the shell that
    are not in a hermetically sealed package, the date of processing and
    an expression, code or identifier that indicates the area from which
    they were harvested; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of tuna that is in a hermetically
    sealed package, one of the expressions to describe the applicable
    colour of the fish flesh based on the indicated condition as set out
    in the Descriptive Words, Expressions and Identification Names
    Document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged salted fish

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of prepackaged salted fish, if the label
  bears one of the expressions set out in the Descriptive Words,
  Expressions and Identification Names Document to describe the salted
  fish, the applicable salt or moisture content after salting is
  complete as indicated for that expression must be met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 35](#1362209-1362954){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362209-1362954.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-262-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 262"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged fish placed in a second
container

**[[263]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-263 .sectionLabel}** If prepackaged fish
that is labelled in accordance with this Part is placed inside of a
second container and the resulting product is prepackaged fish, other
than consumer prepackaged fish, the second container is not required to
be labelled with the declaration of net quantity referred to in
paragraph 244.1(c).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 35](#1362209-1362958){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362209-1362958.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-263-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 263"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[264]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-264 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 35\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-264-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 264"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[265]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-265 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 35\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-265-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 265"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported prepackaged fish

**[[266]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-266 .sectionLabel}** The label of imported
prepackaged fish must bear the name of the foreign state of origin.

**[[267]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-267 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 36\]]{.Repealed}
