#### [SUBDIVISION H]{.HLabel3}[Fresh Fruits or Vegetables]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845689 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged fresh apples

- **[[268]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-268 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of prepackaged fresh apples must bear the name of the variety.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged apples placed in second
  container

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If prepackaged fresh apples that are labelled in
  accordance with this Part are placed inside of a second container and
  the resulting product is prepackaged fresh apples, other than consumer
  prepackaged fresh apples, the second container is not required to be
  labelled with the name of the variety.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[apple]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[apple]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
  a fresh apple for which a grade is prescribed by these Regulations.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-144, s. 37\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 37](#1362212-1362968){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362212-1362968.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-268-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 268"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported prepackaged fresh fruits or
vegetables

- **[[269]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-269 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  expression "Product of" or "Produce of" or "[produit de]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}", "Grown in" or "[cultivé dans]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or
  "Country of Origin" or "[pays d'origine]{.otherLang lang="fr"}",
  followed by the name of the foreign state in which the fresh fruits or
  vegetables were grown, or other words that clearly indicate that
  foreign state, must be shown on the principal display panel of
  imported prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables in close proximity to
  the declaration of net quantity or the grade name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged fruits or vegetables ---
  second container

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables that are
  labelled in accordance with this Part are placed inside of a second
  container and the resulting product is prepackaged fresh fruits or
  vegetables, other than consumer prepackaged fresh fruits or
  vegetables, the second container is not required to be labelled with
  the information referred to in subsection (1) if that information is
  easily visible and legible without having to open the second container
  and that information is not obscured by the container.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsequent repackaging

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section applies whether or not the imported
  prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables are subsequently repackaged in
  Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 38(E)](#1362217-1362972){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362217-1362972.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-269-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 269"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

- **[[270]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-270 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  information that is required by section 269 must be shown in boldface
  type in characters of at least the minimum character height that is
  set out in column 2 of Schedule 6 for the area of a principal display
  surface that is set out in column 1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of consumer
  prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables that are packaged from bulk at
  retail or that are catch-weight foods sold by a retailer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reusable plastic container

**[[271]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-271 .sectionLabel}** Despite subsection
270(1), in the case of prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables, other
than consumer prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables, whose container is
a reusable plastic container, the characters must be at least 1.6 mm in
height.

#### [SUBDIVISION I]{.HLabel3}[Processed Fruit or Vegetable Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845715 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged processed fruit or vegetable
products

**[[272]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-272 .sectionLabel}** The label of a
prepackaged processed fruit or vegetable product must bear

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the expression "Contents \... Per Cent Slack Filled"
  or "[\... pour cent du contenant non rempli]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or
  "Contents \... Per Cent Short Weight" or "[contient \... pour cent de
  moins que le poids indiqué]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", if the package is
  not filled to capacity or contains less than the minimum drained
  weight and average drained weight as set out in the document entitled
  Minimum Drained Weights and Average Drained Weights for Processed
  Fruit or Vegetable Products in a Hermetically Sealed Package, prepared
  by the Agency and published on its website, as amended from time to
  time; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the expression "Keep Refrigerated" or "[garder
  réfrigéré]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", in the case of sauerkraut with
  preservative, or a fruit juice that is in a non-hermetically sealed
  package.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 39](#1362219-1362978){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362219-1362978.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-272-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 272"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification name --- food in syrup or
juice

**[[273]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-273 .sectionLabel}** A food that is set out
in the Descriptive Words, Expressions and Identification Names Document
that is frozen or in a hermetically sealed package and that is packaged
in syrup, fruit juice, or in fruit juice to which sugar has been added,
must be labelled with the identification name that is set out for the
corresponding percentage of soluble solids contained in the applicable
food.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 39](#1362219-1362982){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362219-1362982.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-273-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 273"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Name of foreign state

- **[[274]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-274 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of an imported prepackaged processed fruit or vegetable product
  must bear the name of the foreign state where the processed fruit or
  vegetable product was packaged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The name must be shown in characters that are at
  least 1.6 mm in height.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Product packaged for Canadian importer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), if the product was packaged
  for a Canadian importer under the importer's private label, the name
  must be shown in characters that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 6.4 mm in height, if the declaration of
    net quantity is more than 283.5 g; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at least 3.2 mm in height, if the declaration of
    net quantity is 283.5 g or less.

#### [SUBDIVISION J]{.HLabel3}[Honey]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845756 .Subheading}

**[[275]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-275 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 40\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-275-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 275"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Graded Canadian honey

**[[276]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-276 .sectionLabel}** The label of
prepackaged honey that is produced in Canada and graded in accordance
with these Regulations must bear the expression "Product of Canada" or
"[produit du Canada]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or "Canadian Honey" or
"[miel canadien]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported prepackaged honey

- **[[277]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-277 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of imported prepackaged honey must bear the expression "Product
  of" or "[produit de]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" followed by the name of
  the foreign state of origin.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of imported prepackaged honey, other than
  consumer prepackaged honey, that information must be shown in
  characters that are at least 9.5 mm in height.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Honey packaged from imported honey

**[[278]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-278 .sectionLabel}** The label of consumer
prepackaged honey that was packaged from imported honey and graded in
accordance with these Regulations must bear the expressions "Product of"
and "[produit de]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" followed by the name of the
foreign state of origin.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Blend of Canadian and imported honey

- **[[279]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-279 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of prepackaged honey that is a blend of imported honey and
  Canadian honey and that is graded in accordance with these Regulations
  must bear the expression "A Blend of Canadian and (naming the foreign
  state or states of origin) Honey" or "[mélange de miel canadien et de
  miel (indication de l'État étranger ou des États étrangers
  d'origine)]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or "A Blend of (naming the foreign
  state or states of origin) Honey and Canadian Honey" or "[mélange de
  miel (indication de l'État étranger ou des États étrangers d'origine)
  et de miel canadien]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sources of honey

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The states of origin, Canadian or foreign, must be
  shown in descending order of the proportion of honey from each state.

#### [SUBDIVISION K]{.HLabel3}[Maple Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845778 .Subheading}

**[[280]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-280 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 41\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-280-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 280"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported maple products

**[[281]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-281 .sectionLabel}** The label of the
following maple products must bear the name of the foreign state of
origin:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any imported prepackaged maple syrup whose net
  quantity is 5 L or less; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other imported prepackaged maple product whose
  net quantity is 5 kg or less.

#### [SUBDIVISION L]{.HLabel3}[Edible Meat Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-845788 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection legend --- non-prepackaged
edible meat products

- **[[282]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-282 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  edible meat product that is not prepackaged must bear

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inspection legend set out in Figure 1 of
    Schedule 2, if the edible meat product is sent or conveyed from one
    province to another or exported; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the official inspection mark of the foreign state
    of origin, if the edible meat product is imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Size of inspection legend

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an inspection legend or official inspection mark
  of a foreign state is applied directly on an edible meat product, the
  transverse axis passing through the centre of the legend or mark must
  be at least 25 mm in length.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Label --- non-prepackaged edible meat
products

- **[[283]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-283 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  label must be applied or attached to an edible meat product that is
  not prepackaged, and must bear

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information required by paragraphs 218(1)(a)
    and (b) and 244.1(h) and sections 244.5 and 286 shown on the
    principal display panel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ingredients listed in descending order of their
    proportion by weight of the product; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the components of the ingredients,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} listed immediately after the ingredient of which
      they are components, in such a manner as to indicate that they are
      components of that ingredient,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} listed in descending order of their proportion
      by weight of the ingredient determined before they are combined to
      form the ingredient, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} shown in accordance with sections B.01.009 and
      B.01.010 of the Food and Drug Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If one or more components of an ingredient is shown
  in a list of ingredients, the ingredient is not required to be shown
  in the list if all of the components of the ingredient are shown in
  the list by their common names and in accordance with paragraph (1)(b)
  as if they were ingredients.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 42](#1362223-1362989){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362223-1362989.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-283-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 283"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- omission or substitution of
ingredient or component

- **[[284]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-284 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  the case where it is an acceptable manufacturing practice for a
  licence holder to omit, from an edible meat product that is not
  prepackaged, any food that is ordinarily an ingredient of the meat
  product or a component of the ingredient, or substitute in whole or in
  part, in an edible meat product that is not prepackaged, any food for
  a food that is ordinarily an ingredient or a component of the
  ingredient, the list of ingredients on the label of the meat product
  may, for the 12‑month period beginning at the time the label is
  applied or attached to the edible meat product, show as ingredients of
  the meat product or components of the ingredients the food that may be
  omitted or substituted, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all the foods that may be used as ingredients or
    components throughout the 12‑month period are shown in the list of
    ingredients;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is clearly stated as part of the list of
    ingredients that the food shown as an ingredient or component may
    not be present in the meat product or that another food may be
    substituted; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the food that may be omitted or substituted is
    grouped with foods of the same class of foods that are used as
    ingredients or components in the product and the foods within each
    of the groups of foods are listed in descending order of the
    proportion by weight in which they are likely to be used during the
    12‑month period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- varying proportions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case where it is an acceptable manufacturing
  practice for a licence holder to vary the proportions of ingredients
  of an edible meat product that is not prepackaged or components of the
  ingredients, the list of ingredients on the label of the edible meat
  product may, for the 12‑month period beginning at the time the label
  is applied or attached to the meat product, show the ingredients or
  components in the same proportions throughout the 12‑month period, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is clearly stated as part of the list of
    ingredients that the proportions indicated are subject to change;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ingredients or components are listed in
    descending order of the proportion by weight in which they are
    likely to be used during the 12‑month period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 43](#1362228-1362996){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362228-1362996.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-284-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 284"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[285]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-285 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in sections 283 and 284.

[[component]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[component]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual unit of food
    that is combined as an individual unit of food with one or more
    other individual units of food to form an
    ingredient. ([constituant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ingredient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ingredient]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual unit of food
    that is combined as an individual unit of food with one or more
    other individual units of food to form an integral unit of food that
    is an edible meat product. ([ingrédient]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged edible meat products

**[[286]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-286 .sectionLabel}** The label of a
prepackaged edible meat product must bear on the principal display panel
a statement that indicates that the meat product must be kept
refrigerated or kept frozen, as the case may be, unless the meat product

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is in a hermetically sealed package and is
  commercially sterile;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is dried to attain a water activity of 0.85 or less;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a pH of 4.6 or less;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} is packaged in salt or a saturated salt solution; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} is fermented and has a pH of 5.3 or less, and a water
  activity of 0.90 or less, at the end of the fermentation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 44](#1362237-1363005){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362237-1363005.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-286-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 286"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection legend --- prepackaged edible
meat products

- **[[287]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-287 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of a prepackaged edible meat product must also bear

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the inspection legend set out in Figure 1 or 2 of
    Schedule 2, if the prepackaged edible meat product is sent or
    conveyed from one province to another or exported; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the official inspection mark of the foreign state
    of origin, if the prepackaged edible meat product is imported.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Principal display panel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a prepackaged edible meat product,
  other than a consumer prepackaged meat product, the principal display
  panel must bear the inspection legend or the official inspection mark
  of the foreign state of origin.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tamper-resistant seal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), in the case of a prepackaged
  edible meat product, other than a consumer prepackaged meat product,
  the inspection legend or the official inspection mark of the foreign
  state of origin may be shown on the tamper-resistant seal, if such a
  seal is used, unless that seal is applied to the bottom of the
  container.

**[[288]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-288 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 45\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-288-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 288"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Animal species

**[[289]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-289 .sectionLabel}** The label of an edible
meat product may describe the meat product as, or as being derived from,
a carcass or part of a carcass or a cut, organ or tissue of a food
animal only if the label bears the name of the animal species, as it is
commonly known, from which the meat product was derived.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ready-to-eat edible meat products

- **[[290]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-290 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of an edible meat product may bear a word or expression that
  indicates or suggests that it is a ready-to-eat product only if the
  requirements of section 47 are met in respect of the edible meat
  product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cooked or fully cooked edible meat
  product

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The label may also bear the word or expression
  "Cooked" or "Fully Cooked" provided the word or expression is in close
  proximity to the common name and the product has been subjected to
  heat for a time sufficient to produce the characteristics of a cooked
  meat product in respect of friability, colour, texture and flavour.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 46](#1362240-1363013){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362240-1363013.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-290-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 290"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uncooked meat products

**[[291]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-291 .sectionLabel}** The label of a
prepackaged edible meat product that is not a ready-to-eat product but
could be mistaken for one must bear

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the expression "Must Be Cooked" or "[doit être
  cuit]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or "Raw Product" or "[produit
  cru]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or the word "Uncooked" or "[non
  cuit]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", or any equivalent word or expression, on
  the principal display panel, in close proximity to the common name, to
  indicate that the meat product requires cooking before consumption;
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} comprehensive cooking instructions --- such as a
  combination of internal temperature and cooking time --- that, if
  followed, will result in a ready-to-eat meat product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 47](#1362242-1363017){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362242-1363017.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-291-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 291"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged poultry carcass

**[[292]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-292 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
prepackaged poultry carcass, if the carcass is dressed or partially
dressed and has been graded, the common name must be shown on

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the part of the package that lies on or over the
  anterior centre of the breast of the poultry, if the carcass is
  individually packaged; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} on a tag that is attached to the V of the wishbone of
  the poultry carcass, if it is not individually packaged.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Seasoned consumer prepackaged poultry
carcass

**[[293]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-293 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
consumer prepackaged poultry carcass, if the carcass is dressed or
partially dressed, has been graded in accordance with these Regulations
and has been seasoned, the label must bear the words "Seasoned" and
"[assaisonné]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 48](#1362244-1363025){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362244-1363025.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-293-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 293"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Poultry carcass --- not individually
packaged

**[[294]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-294 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
prepackaged poultry carcass, if the carcass is dressed or partially
dressed, has been graded in accordance with these Regulations, and is
not individually packaged, a tag must be attached to the V of the
wishbone of the poultry carcass that bears the name and principal place
of business of the person by or for whom the poultry carcass was
packaged.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 48](#1362244-1363021){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362244-1363021.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-294-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 294"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[295]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-295 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2022-144, s. 48\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-295-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 295"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Label of edible meat products ---
exception

- **[[296]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-296 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  edible meat product whose label does not meet the requirements of
  these Regulations may be sent or conveyed from an establishment that
  is identified in a licence if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is a prepackaged meat product, other than a
    consumer prepackaged meat product, that is sealed with a
    tamper-resistant seal or it is in a conveyance that is sealed with a
    tamper-resistant seal;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is sent or conveyed to another establishment
    where meat products are manufactured, processed, treated, preserved,
    graded, packaged or labelled by a licence holder; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is accompanied by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a document from the licence holder that states
      that the meat product is identified as edible under section 125,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a list of ingredients that meets the
      requirements of the [Food and Drug
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870) that apply in
      respect of a [[prepackaged product]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as
      defined in subsection B.01.001(1) of those Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tamper-resistant seal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The tamper-resistant seal on the prepackaged meat
  product or conveyance must not be broken until after the meat product
  arrives at the other establishment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported edible meat products

- **[[297]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-297 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  label of an imported edible meat product must bear the expression
  "Product of" or "[produit de]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", followed by the
  name of the foreign state of origin, in close proximity to the common
  name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information must, whether or not the edible meat
  product is a consumer prepackaged meat product, be shown in characters
  of the height required by subsections 210(2) and (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsequent packaging or labelling

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section applies whether or not the imported
  edible meat product is subsequently packaged or labelled in Canada
  without being manufactured or prepared in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported consumer prepackaged poultry
carcasses

**[[298]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-298 .sectionLabel}** In the case of an
imported consumer prepackaged poultry carcass, if the carcass is dressed
or partially dressed and has been graded in accordance with these
Regulations, the information required by subsection 297(1) must be shown
in the same colour as the grade name.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Exception]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845890 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consumer prepackaged food

**[[299]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-299 .sectionLabel}** Paragraphs 199(1)(a)
and 199(2)(a), subsections 199(3) to (5) and sections 200, 216, 221 to
224 and 229 to 241 do not apply in respect of a consumer prepackaged
food that is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} manufactured, prepared, produced, packaged or
  labelled for use by commercial or industrial enterprises or
  institutions without being sold by them as a consumer prepackaged
  food;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} manufactured, prepared, produced, packaged or
  labelled only for sale to or by a duty-free shop; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} distributed to one or more persons for no
  consideration.

## [PART 12]{.HLabel1}[Grades and Grade Names]{.HTitleText1} {#h-845917 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845918 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[304]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-304 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[beef carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[beef carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
    Grades Document. ([carcasse de boeuf]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bison carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bison carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
    Grades Document. ([carcasse de bison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[grader]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grader]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person designated as a grader
    under subsection 13(3) of the [Canadian Food Inspection Agency
    Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5) for the purposes of the
    Act. ([classificateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[grade roller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grade roller]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tool that is used to
    apply a roller brand on each side of a livestock carcass. ([rouleau
    à estampiller]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Grades Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Grades Document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the document entitled
    Beef, Bison and Veal Carcass Grade Requirements, prepared by the
    Canadian Beef Grading Agency and published on its website, as
    amended from time to time. ([Document de
    classification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[grade stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grade stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mark that is applied to
    a livestock carcass and that shows the grade name and the grader's
    code. ([cachet de classification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[grade stamp applicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grade stamp applicator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tool that is
    used to apply a grade stamp or a yield stamp to a livestock
    carcass. ([applicateur de cachet de classification]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[grading stand]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[grading stand]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a platform that is used
    for grading livestock carcasses. ([plate-forme de
    classification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[identification code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[identification code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distinct code
    that is applied to a food animal before slaughter and grading to
    enable the food animal to be traced. ([code
    d'identification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[knife-rib]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[knife-rib]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to cut the left side, or the
    left and right sides, of a beef carcass or bison carcass in the
    following locations by severing the vertebrae and cutting 15 cm or
    more beyond the [longissimus]{.otherLang lang="la"} muscles in order
    to expose those muscles for evaluation by a grader:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a beef carcass, between the 12th
      and 13th ribs; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a bison carcass, between the 11th
      and 12th ribs. ([incision transversale]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[lot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[lot]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a group of food animals or a
    quantity of livestock carcasses that, for any reason, is considered
    together for inspection. ([lot]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[marketing agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[marketing agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a board or commission
    that is established under an Act of a province that regulates the
    marketing of bovine or ovine animals. ([office de
    commercialisation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[meat inspection stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[meat inspection stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an inspection legend that is prescribed under
      section 179 in respect of a meat product; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a mark that is authorized under an Act of a
      province to be applied to or used in connection with a livestock
      carcass or poultry carcass after inspection. ([cachet d'inspection
      de viande]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[musculature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[musculature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the size and shape of the
    muscles of a livestock carcass. ([musculature]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[ovine carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ovine carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
    Compendium. ([carcasse d'ovin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[primal cut]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[primal cut]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a beef carcass or bison carcass,
      the round, sirloin, short loin, rib or chuck of the carcass side;
      and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an ovine carcass or veal carcass,
      the leg, loin or foresaddle of the carcass side. ([coupe
      primaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[producer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[producer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person who sells
    livestock for slaughter. ([producteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[provincial establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[provincial establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    establishment

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is registered under an Act of a province
      that regulates the inspection of livestock carcasses or poultry
      carcasses; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where livestock carcasses or poultry carcasses
      are prepared by any person who is authorized to do so under an Act
      of a province that regulates the inspection of those
      carcasses. ([établissement provincial]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[roller brand]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[roller brand]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the mark that is applied
    to a beef carcass and that shows the grade name and the number that
    is assigned to the establishment where the livestock carcass is
    graded. ([marque d'estampillage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sub-primal cut]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sub-primal cut]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cut of meat that is
    greater than 125 cm^3^ and that is derived from a beef carcass or a
    primal cut of a beef carcass. ([coupe
    sous-primaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[trim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[trim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to remove all or part of the
    external fat from a livestock carcass. ([parer]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[veal carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[veal carcass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
    Grades Document. ([carcasse de veau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[weighmaster]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[weighmaster]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an employee of an
    establishment that is identified in a licence or of a provincial
    establishment who is trained to operate a scale that is approved
    under section 3 of the [Weights and Measures
    Act](/eng/acts/W-6). ([peseur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[yield class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[yield class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
    Compendium or the Grades Document. ([catégorie de
    rendement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[yield stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[yield stamp]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
    Compendium or the Grades Document. ([cachet de
    rendement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Grade Names]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845954 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition [[grade
name]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in Act

**[[305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-305 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of the
definition [[grade name]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the Act,
the grade names that are set out in the Compendium and in the Grades
Document are prescribed in respect of foods.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Grading]{.HTitleText2} {#h-845957 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory grading

- **[[306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-306 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  eggs, fish, fresh fruits or vegetables, processed fruit or vegetable
  products, honey, maple syrup or beef carcass in respect of which
  grades are prescribed by these Regulations that are sent or conveyed
  from one province to another or that are imported or exported must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be graded;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements that are set out in the
    Compendium or the Grades Document in respect of the applicable grade
    of that food; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be labelled, in accordance with the Compendium or
    the Grades Document, with the applicable grade name that is set out
    in the Compendium or the Grades Document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- subsection (1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} frozen gutted Pacific salmon;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fresh fruits or vegetables that are exported;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fresh blueberries, fresh cantaloupes, fresh
    crabapples, fresh cranberries, fresh field rhubarb and fresh
    strawberries;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if they are in a hermetically sealed package, mixed
    vegetables (macédoine), stewed tomatoes, tomato puree, tomato pulp,
    tomato paste, tomato ketchup and tomato chili sauce;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a beef carcass, or a carcass side, hind quarter,
    front quarter, primal cut or sub-primal cut of a beef carcass that
    has been imported, in the following cases:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if it is prepackaged, its container is labelled
      with the expression "Ungraded Beef" or "[boeuf non
      classifié]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if it is not prepackaged, it is accompanied by
      documents for presentation to an inspector or grader that show
      that it has not been graded; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a beef carcass, or a carcass side, hind quarter,
    front quarter, primal cut or sub-primal cut of a beef carcass, that
    is sent or conveyed from one province to another or exported, in the
    following cases:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if it is prepackaged, its container is labelled
      with the expression "Ungraded Beef" or "[boeuf non
      classifié]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if it is not prepackaged, it is accompanied by
      documents for presentation to an inspector or grader that show
      that it has not been graded.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- paragraphs (1)(b) and (c)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(b) and (c) do not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any processed fruit or vegetable product that does
    not meet the requirements of the Regulations with respect to grade
    and that is sent or conveyed from one province to another, if it is
    labelled with the words "Substandard" or "[sous-régulier]{.otherLang
    lang="fr"}";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} honey that does not meet the requirements of the
    Regulations with respect to grade and that is sent or conveyed from
    one province to another, if it is labelled with the words
    "Substandard" or "[sous-régulier]{.otherLang lang="fr"}"; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} honey that does not meet the requirements of the
    Regulations with respect to grade and that is exported, if the
    information on the label or the container with respect to the
    quality of the honey is not false, misleading or deceptive or is not
    likely to create an erroneous impression.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- paragraph (1)(c)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 1(c) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a food that is imported, if the Compendium
    indicates that it must be labelled with a grade designation that is
    established by the foreign state of origin and the imported food is
    labelled with that grade designation rather than with a grade name,
    and the grade name must be shown in accordance with sections 205,
    206 and 312 as if it were a grade name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables that are
    imported, sold in their original container and labelled with a grade
    designation that is established by the foreign state of origin, if
    they meet the requirements of the foreign state in respect of that
    grade designation and those requirements are substantially
    equivalent to any requirements that apply under these Regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} imported prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables,
    other than consumer prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables, if they
    are graded and meet the requirements of the Compendium in respect of
    that grade;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a beef carcass, or a carcass side, hind quarter,
    front quarter, primal cut or sub-primal cut of a beef carcass, that
    is not prepackaged and that is imported if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it is graded and labelled in accordance with the
      requirements, in respect of grades of beef carcasses, that are
      established by the foreign state of origin, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it is accompanied by documents for presentation
      to an inspector or grader that show the grade designation that is
      established by the foreign state of origin; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a beef carcass, or a carcass side, hind quarter,
    front quarter, primal cut or sub-primal cut of a beef carcass, that
    is not prepackaged and does not bear a grade name, and that is sent
    or conveyed from one province to another, if it is accompanied by
    documents, for presentation to an inspector or grader, that show its
    grade name.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Optional grading

**[[307]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-307 .sectionLabel}** The following foods for
which grades are prescribed by these Regulations, if they are graded and
sent or conveyed from one province to another, or imported or exported,
must meet the requirements that are set out in the Compendium or the
Grades Document in respect of the applicable grade of that food and must
be labelled, in accordance with the Compendium or Grades Document, with
the applicable grade name that is set out in the Compendium or Grades
Document:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a food referred to in paragraphs 306(2)(a), (c) and
  (d);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a dairy product, except for a dairy product that is
  exported;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a bison carcass, ovine carcass or veal carcass; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a poultry carcass that is dressed or partially
  dressed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized application or use

- **[[308]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-308
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a licence
  holder is authorized to apply a grade name to, and use a grade name in
  connection with, a food that is identified in their licence if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the food meets the requirements of paragraphs
    8(1)(a) to (d);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the food meets the requirements that are set out in
    the Compendium or the Grades Document in respect of the applicable
    grade of that food;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the food complies with any standards that are set
    out in the Standards of Identity Document;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a dairy product, an egg, fish, a
    processed fruit or vegetable product, honey or maple syrup, the food
    has been graded by a licence holder;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a livestock carcass or poultry
    carcass that is dressed or partially dressed, the food has been
    graded by a grader; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the food is packaged and labelled in accordance
    with these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Livestock carcass or poultry carcass

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a livestock carcass or a poultry
  carcass that is dressed or partially dressed, only the persons who are
  referred to in the Compendium or the Grades Document are authorized,
  under the circumstances set out in the Compendium or the Grades
  Document, to apply a grade name to, or use a grade name in connection
  with, a food in accordance with this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Imported foods --- no prescribed grade
name

**[[309]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-309 .sectionLabel}** A food that is imported
and in respect of which no grade name is prescribed by these Regulations
may be labelled with the grade designation that is established by the
foreign state of origin if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the food meets the requirements for the grade
  designation that are established by that foreign state;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the food is labelled in accordance with these
  Regulations; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of that foreign state of origin is clearly
  indicated on the label.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized reproduction

**[[310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-310 .sectionLabel}** The following persons
are authorized to reproduce a grade name:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} printers of labels or manufacturers of packages, if
  the labels or packages that bear the grade name are provided to a
  person who is authorized to apply or use the grade name under section
  308;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} publishers of documents on the subject of a food that
  is graded;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} publishers of documents that advertise a food that is
  graded; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} manufacturers of grade stamp applicators or grade
  rollers, if the grade stamps applicators or grade rollers are provided
  to a grader.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advertising or sale

**[[311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-311 .sectionLabel}** Any person is
authorized to use a grade name in the advertising or sale of a food if
the food is labelled with the grade name in accordance with these
Regulations.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Packaging and Labelling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846020 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846021 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Labelling of grade name --- consumer
prepackaged food

**[[312]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-312 .sectionLabel}** In the case of a
consumer prepackaged food, the grade name must be shown

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the principal display panel or in the manner set
  out in the Compendium; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in characters of the height that is required by
  another provision in this Division or, if there is no such provision,
  in characters of at least the minimum character height that is set out
  in column 2 of Schedule 6 for the area of a principal display surface
  that is set out in column 1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Illustration of grade name

**[[313]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-313 .sectionLabel}** A grade name that is
applied to a beef carcass, bison carcass, ovine carcass, veal carcass,
poultry carcass that is dressed or partially dressed, dairy product or
egg must be shown as illustrated in the Compendium or the Grades
Document.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Eggs]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846028 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grade name --- prepackaged eggs

- **[[314]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-314 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  grade name on prepackaged eggs must be shown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a tray with an overwrap or an egg
    carton, on the top of the tray or egg carton; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a container, other than a tray with
    an overwrap or an egg carton, in a central location on the
    container, except the top or the bottom.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- paragraph (1)(a)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The grade name is not required to be shown on trays
  with an overwrap or on egg cartons that are packaged inside of a
  second container if the grade name is shown on the second container
  and the second container is sent or conveyed to an establishment where
  eggs are graded, packaged and labelled by a licence holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- paragraph (1)(b)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If consumer prepackaged eggs in a container other
  than a tray with an overwrap or an egg carton are packaged inside of a
  second container, the grade name is not required to be shown on the
  second container if the grade name is easily visible and legible
  without opening the second container and the grade name is not
  obscured by the second container.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 52(E)](#1362254-1363041){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362254-1363041.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-314-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 314"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

**[[315]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-315 .sectionLabel}** The grade name on
prepackaged eggs must be shown in characters that are at least the
following height:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a tray with an overwrap or an egg
  carton of eggs graded Canada A or Canada B, 1.5 mm for the word
  "Canada" and 3 mm for "A" or "B";

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a tray with an overwrap or an egg
  carton of eggs graded Canada C or Canada Nest Run, 1.5 mm;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a container, other than a tray with an
  overwrap or an egg carton, that contains eggs graded Canada A or
  Canada B, 6 mm for the word "Canada" and 13 mm for "A" or "B"; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a container, other than a tray with an
  overwrap or an egg carton, that contains eggs graded Canada C or
  Canada Nest Run, 13 mm.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eggs --- Canada A

**[[316]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-316 .sectionLabel}** Eggs that are graded
Canada A must be labelled with any applicable size designation that is
set out in the Compendium. The size designation must be shown on the
container in close proximity to the grade name.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Fish]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846046 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged fish

**[[317]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-317 .sectionLabel}** Prepackaged fish that
is sent or conveyed from one province to another or that is imported or
exported must be labelled with any applicable class and size designation
that is set out in the Compendium. The class name or size designation
must be shown in close proximity to the grade name and in characters
that are at least 3.2 mm in height.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Second container

**[[318]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-318 .sectionLabel}** If consumer prepackaged
fish that is labelled in accordance with these Regulations is placed
inside of a second container and the resulting product is consumer
prepackaged fish, the second container is not required to be labelled
with the grade name.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

**[[319]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-319 .sectionLabel}** The grade name of
prepackaged fish must be shown in characters that are at least 3.2 mm in
height if the declaration of net quantity is 900 g or less.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[Fresh Fruits or Vegetables]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846053 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grade name --- prepackaged fresh fruits
or vegetables

- **[[320]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-320 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  grade name of prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables, other than
  consumer prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables, must be shown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on any surface of the container, except the bottom;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in characters of at least the minimum character
    height that is set out in column 2 of Schedule 6 for the area of a
    principal display surface that is set out in column 1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- type size

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the grade name of prepackaged
  fresh fruits or vegetables, other than consumer prepackaged fresh
  fruits or vegetables, may, if the container is a reusable plastic
  container, be in characters that are at least 1.6 mm in height.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- second container

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If prepackaged fresh fruits or vegetables that are
  labelled in accordance with these Regulations are placed inside of a
  second container and the resulting product is prepackaged fresh fruits
  or vegetables, other than consumer prepackaged fresh fruits or
  vegetables, the second container is not required to be labelled with
  the grade name if the grade name is easily visible and legible without
  having to open the second container and the grade name is not obscured
  by the second container.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 53(E)](#1362256-1363045){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362256-1363045.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-320-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 320"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Size designation

**[[321]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-321 .sectionLabel}** Fresh fruits or
vegetables that are sent or conveyed from one province to another or
imported must be labelled with the applicable size designation that is
set out in the Compendium, if any. The size designation must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be shown in close proximity to the grade name;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of prepackaged fresh fruits or
  vegetables, other than consumer prepackaged fresh fruits or
  vegetables,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if their container is a reusable plastic container,
    be shown in characters that are at least 1.6 mm in height, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if their container is not a reusable plastic
    container, be shown in characters of at least the minimum character
    height that is set out in paragraph 320(1)(b) for the grade name;
    and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of consumer prepackaged fresh fruits or
  vegetables, be shown in characters of at least the minimum character
  height that is set out in column 2 of Schedule 6 for the area of a
  principal display surface that is set out in column 1.

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[Processed Fruit or Vegetable Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846070 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Size designation

- **[[322]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-322 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  processed fruit or vegetable product that is sent or conveyed from one
  province to another or that is imported or exported must be labelled
  with any applicable size designation that is set out in the
  Compendium. The size designation must be shown in close proximity to
  the grade name in characters that are at least 1.6 mm in height.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), green or wax beans, peas,
  lima beans, asparagus tips or spears, whole white potatoes, whole
  carrots or cut carrots-whole style that are in a hermetically sealed
  package and have not been size graded or that are not all of the same
  size may be labelled, as the case may be, with the expression

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} "Ungraded as to Size" or "[non calibré]{.otherLang
    lang="fr"}"; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} "Assorted Sizes" or "[grosseurs
    assorties]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or "Mixed Sizes" or "[grosseurs
    mixtes]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".

#### [SUBDIVISION F]{.HLabel3}[Honey]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846078 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grade name --- prepackaged honey

**[[323]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-323 .sectionLabel}** The grade name of
prepackaged honey, other than consumer prepackaged honey, must be shown

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} on at least one side or one end of the container; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in characters that are at least 9.5 mm in height.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Colour class

**[[324]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-324 .sectionLabel}** Honey that is sent or
conveyed from one province to another or that is imported or exported
must be labelled with any applicable colour class that is set out in the
Compendium. The colour class must be shown on the container, in close
proximity to the grade name,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of consumer prepackaged honey, in
  characters that are of at least the minimum character height that is
  set out in column 2 of Schedule 6 for the area of a principal display
  surface that is set out in column 1; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of prepackaged honey other than consumer
  prepackaged honey, in the manner set out in paragraphs 323(a) and (b).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-144, s. 54(E)](#1362258-1363048){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362258-1363048.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-324-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 324"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [SUBDIVISION G]{.HLabel3}[Maple Syrup]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846087 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Colour class

**[[325]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-325 .sectionLabel}** Maple syrup that is
graded Canada Grade A and is sent or conveyed from one province to
another or exported, or that is graded Grade A and is imported, must be
labelled with the applicable colour class that is set out in the
Compendium. The colour class must be shown on the container in
characters of at least the minimum character height that is set out in
column 2 of Schedule 6 for the area of a principal display surface that
is set out in column 1.

#### [SUBDIVISION H]{.HLabel3}[Livestock Carcasses]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846090 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepackaged cut of beef

**[[326]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-326 .sectionLabel}** A grade name that is
applied to a prepackaged primal cut or sub-primal cut of a beef carcass
must correspond to the grade of the beef carcass from which it is cut.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Beef --- Canada AAA

**[[327]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-327 .sectionLabel}** A cut from a beef
carcass that is graded Canada AAA and that is exported in a container
may be labelled with the expression "Canada Choice" or "[Choix
Canada]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" instead of the grade name.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Livestock carcass --- removal of marking

- **[[328]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-328 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  grade stamp, roller brand or yield stamp must not be removed from a
  livestock carcass or a primal cut of a livestock carcass unless the
  removal is at the direction of and under the supervision of a grader
  or the livestock carcass or primal cut is being trimmed for further
  processing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of marked fat

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If fat that is marked with a grade stamp, roller
  brand or yield stamp is removed from a livestock carcass or a primal
  cut, the fat must be disposed of under a grader's supervision unless
  the fat is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} reapplied to the same livestock carcass or primal
    cut from which it was removed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applied, under a grader's supervision, to another
    livestock carcass or primal cut that bears the same grade stamp,
    roller brand or yield stamp.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Beef carcass --- rib

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the carcass referred to in paragraph (2)(b) is a
  beef carcass that is graded Canada A, Canada AA, Canada AAA or Canada
  Prime, the fat must be applied to the rib of the carcass.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Livestock carcass --- additional marks

**[[329]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-329 .sectionLabel}** A livestock carcass or
primal cut of a livestock carcass that is marked with a grade stamp,
roller brand or yield stamp may bear another mark only if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the mark is shown no more than once on the livestock
  carcass or once on each primal cut;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mark is shown alone or in combination with a
  date;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the size in height and width of the mark or, if a
  date is shown, of the mark and date in combination does not exceed 76
  mm; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the mark and any date do not touch the grade stamp,
  the roller brand or the yield stamp.

#### [SUBDIVISION I]{.HLabel3}[Poultry Carcasses]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846110 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grade name --- poultry carcass

- **[[330]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-330 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  grade name of a poultry carcass must be shown

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a poultry carcass that is
    individually packaged, on the part of the package that lies on or
    over the anterior centre of the breast of the poultry; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a poultry carcass that is not
    individually packaged, on a tag that is attached to the V of the
    wishbone of the carcass.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Type size

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The grade name of a poultry carcass must be shown in
  characters that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of poultry carcass that is not
    individually packaged or is in a container labelled "not for further
    processing" or « aucune transformation ultérieure » and individually
    wrapped, at least 1.5 mm in height;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged poultry
    carcass graded Canada A or Canada C that has a net weight of 1 kg or
    less, at least 3 mm in height;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged poultry
    carcass graded Canada A or Canada C that has a net weight of more
    than 1 kg but not more than 5 kg, at least 6 mm in height;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged poultry
    carcass graded Canada A or Canada C that has a net weight of more
    than 5 kg, at least 9 mm in height;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged poultry
    carcass graded Canada Utility that has a net weight of 5 kg or less,
    at least 3 mm in height;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a consumer prepackaged poultry
    carcass graded Canada Utility that has a net weight of more than 5
    kg, at least 5 mm in height; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a prepackaged poultry carcass, other
    than a consumer prepackaged poultry carcass, at least 6 mm in
    height.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging in same container

**[[331]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-331 .sectionLabel}** Only graded poultry
carcasses that are dressed or partially dressed and that have the same
common name may be packaged in the same container.

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Conditions for Grading of Certain Foods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846127 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Grading of Eggs]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846128 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for grading

- **[[332]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-332 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence holder may grade an egg only if the egg

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is edible;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not emit an abnormal odour;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is not mouldy;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has not been in an incubator;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} does not have any internal defect; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} is of a usual colour.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(e), a licence holder may grade
  and apply the grade name Canada C to an egg that has a particle of the
  oviduct or a blood spot neither of which exceeds 3 mm in diameter.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ungraded eggs

- **[[333]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-333
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Ungraded eggs that are received at
  an establishment where eggs are graded by a licence holder must be
  graded and labelled with the applicable grade name that is set out in
  the Compendium or, if they do not meet the requirements in respect of
  any grade that are set out in these Regulations, they must be
  rejected.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rejected eggs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Eggs that are rejected must be destroyed or be placed
  in a container that is labelled with the words "Rejects" and
  "[rejetés]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Grading of Livestock Carcasses]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846145 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for grading

**[[334]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-334 .sectionLabel}** A grader may grade a
livestock carcass in an establishment that is identified in a licence or
in a provincial establishment if one of the following persons has
requested in writing that the carcass be graded:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any person who is in authority in the establishment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any producer; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person who is in possession of the livestock
  carcass.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for grading

**[[335]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-335 .sectionLabel}** A grader may grade a
livestock carcass if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carcass bears a meat inspection stamp or, in the
  case of an imported beef carcass, the official inspection mark of the
  foreign state of origin;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the grading takes place

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a bison carcass or ovine carcass, in
    the establishment that is identified in a licence, or the provincial
    establishment, where the animal was slaughtered,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a veal carcass, in the
    establishment that is identified in a licence, or the provincial
    establishment, where the animal was slaughtered or where the carcass
    was divided into primal cuts or sub-primal cuts, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a beef carcass, in the
    establishment that is identified in a licence, or the provincial
    establishment, where the animal was slaughtered or in the
    establishment that is identified in a licence where the carcass was
    divided into primal cuts or sub-primal cuts;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the carcass has been weighed by a weighmaster using a
  scale that is approved under section 3 of the [Weights and Measures
  Act](/eng/acts/W-6);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the carcass is presented for grading

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at a grading stand where the lighting intensity,
    measured at the level of the grading stand, is at least 1 000 lx, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in a cooler where the lighting intensity, measured
    at the loin level of the carcass, is at least 200 lx;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} at least 10 minutes before grading,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a beef carcass or bison carcass, an
    employee of the establishment performs, to the satisfaction of the
    grader, a knife-rib on the carcass, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a veal carcass, an employee of the
    establishment performs, to the satisfaction of the grader, a cut on
    the lean of the brisket to enable the grader to determine the colour
    reading of the veal carcass;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the establishment has adequate equipment and
  facilities for weighing and grading livestock carcasses; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the grading equipment is functioning properly and is
  accurate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adequate facilities

- **[[336]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-336 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  more than 400 livestock carcasses are graded per hour in an
  establishment that is identified in a licence or in a provincial
  establishment, more than one grading stand is required for the
  purposes of paragraph 335(f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grading stand --- requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 335(f), a grading stand
  must be easily adjustable for height and must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the rate of grading is 150 carcasses per hour or
    less, be at least 3 m long and 2 m wide;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the rate of grading is more than 150 carcasses
    per hour but not more than 300 carcasses per hour, be at least 4 m
    long and 2 m wide; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the rate of grading is more than 300 carcasses
    per hour, be at least 5 m long and 2 m wide.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weighing before trimming

**[[337]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-337 .sectionLabel}** A livestock carcass
that is graded must be weighed before it is trimmed, unless an inspector
or grader directs that it be trimmed before it is weighed.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Grading of Poultry Carcasses]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846177 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for grading --- dressed
carcass

- **[[338]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-338 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  grader may grade a poultry carcass that has been dressed if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carcass is from poultry slaughtered in an
    establishment that is identified in a licence or in a provincial
    establishment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the carcass has been inspected under the Act or
    under an Act of a province that regulates the inspection of poultry
    carcasses;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a chilled poultry carcass, the flesh
    or skin is not dried out;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the carcass is not discoloured from insufficient
    bleeding;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the carcass has no more than one heart, liver,
    gizzard and neck packed with it or inserted into it;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a poultry carcass that weighs more
    than 900 g, the breast bone is intact; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the carcass has not been basted or stuffed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for grading --- partially
  dressed carcass

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A grader may grade a poultry carcass that has been
  partially dressed if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carcass meets the requirements that are set out
    in paragraphs (1)(a) to (g);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the carcass has been eviscerated;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the epidermis has been removed from the feet and
    shanks;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the claws have been removed;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the head, if present, is wrapped; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the beak, if present, is clean.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grading in an establishment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A poultry carcass must not be graded in any place
  other than in an establishment that is identified in a licence or in a
  provincial establishment.

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Grading Certificates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846198 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for issuance

- **[[339]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-339 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  grader --- or a licence holder, the operator of a provincial
  establishment or a marketing agency under the direction of a grader
  --- may issue a grading certificate in respect of a livestock carcass
  or a lot of livestock carcasses if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time of delivery of the food animal or lot
    of food animals to an establishment that is identified in a licence
    or to a provincial establishment for slaughter, the producer has

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} requested the certificate in writing,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} identified each animal that is intended to be
      slaughtered with an identification code, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} completed and filed with any person who is in
      authority in the establishment a list that associates each
      identification code with the producer; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after slaughter, the identification code of each
    slaughtered animal is retained on or transferred to the livestock
    carcass by any person who is in authority in the establishment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The grading certificate must be signed by the grader
  and include the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the producer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of any person who is acting on behalf of
    the producer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the establishment where the
    livestock carcasses were graded;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the certificate number;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date of slaughter;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} for each livestock carcass,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its identification code,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its warm weight as determined by a weighmaster,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} its grade;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a lot of livestock carcasses,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of livestock carcasses per grade or
      per yield class, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the number of livestock carcasses that have been
      condemned;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a grading certificate that is issued
    in respect of a beef carcass that is graded Canada A, Canada AA,
    Canada AAA or Canada Prime, the yield of the beef carcass;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a grading certificate that is issued
    in respect of a beef carcass or bison carcass, an indication, for
    each carcass, of its age, meat colour, firmness, and, if any,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} its musculature,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} its marbling, specifically, the amount, size and
      distribution of intramuscular fat deposits in the
      [longissimus]{.otherLang lang="la"} muscles,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} its fat colour or texture,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} its fat measurement, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} its pronounced masculinity;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a grading certificate that is issued
    in respect of a lamb carcass, as defined in the Compendium, for each
    carcass,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the fat measurement,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the score for musculature of each primal cut and
      the average score for musculature,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a carcass that is graded Canada
      AAA, the yield, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} an indication of any musculature demerits, meat
      colour demerits or fat colour demerits that were assigned to the
      carcass; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a grading certificate that is issued
    in respect of a mutton carcass, as defined in the Compendium, the
    fat measurement of the carcass.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recording of information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The information referred to in subsection (2) may be
  recorded on the grading certificate by the licence holder, the
  operator or the marketing agency referred to in subsection (1).

## [PART 13]{.HLabel1}[Organic Products]{.HTitleText1} {#h-846236 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846237 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[340]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-340 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[aquaculture product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aquaculture product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in CAN/CGSB-32.312. ([produit aquacole]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[CAN/CGSB-32.310]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CAN/CGSB-32.310]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian General
    Standards Board standard CAN/CGSB-32.310, entitled Organic
    production systems --- General principles and management standards,
    as amended from time to time. ([norme
    CAN/CGSB-32.310]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[CAN/CGSB-32.311]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CAN/CGSB-32.311]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian General
    Standards Board standard CAN/CGSB-32.311, entitled Organic
    production systems --- Permitted substances lists, as amended from
    time to time. ([norme CAN/CGSB-32.311]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[CAN/CGSB-32.312]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[CAN/CGSB-32.312]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian General
    Standards Board standard CAN/CGSB-32.312, entitled Organic
    production systems --- Aquaculture --- General principles,
    management standards and permitted substances lists, as amended from
    time to time. ([norme CAN/CGSB-32.312]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[certification body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[certification body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person who is
    accredited as a certification body under section 361 or 363 and who
    is responsible for the organic certification of food commodities and
    for the certification of packaging or labelling of organic
    products. ([organisme de certification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[conformity verification body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[conformity verification body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
    person who, having complied with the requirements that are set out
    in ISO/IEC 17011, has entered into an agreement with the Agency
    under subsection 14(1) of the [Canadian Food Inspection Agency
    Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5) to assess, recommend for accreditation and
    monitor certification bodies. ([organisme de vérification de la
    conformité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ISO/IEC 17011]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ISO/IEC 17011]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International
    Organization for Standardization standard ISO/IEC 17011, entitled
    Conformity assessment --- General requirements for accreditation
    bodies accrediting conformity assessment bodies, as amended from
    time to time. ([norme ISO/IEC 17011]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ISO/IEC 17065]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ISO/IEC 17065]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International
    Organization for Standardization standard ISO/IEC 17065, entitled
    Conformity assessment --- Requirements for bodies certifying
    products, processes and services, as amended from time to
    time. ([norme ISO/IEC 17065]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition [[food
commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in Act

- **[[341]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-341 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph (c) of the definition [[food
  commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the Act, the
  following are prescribed food commodities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [[feed]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
    section 2 of the [Feeds Act](/eng/acts/F-9); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [[seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
    section 2 of the [Seeds Act](/eng/acts/S-8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Feed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(a), a reference to
  "livestock" in the definition [[feed]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
  section 2 of the [Feeds Act](/eng/acts/F-9) must be read to include
  livestock that is an aquaculture product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A food commodity that is not included in
  paragraph (a) or (b) of the definition [[food
  commodity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the Act is exempted
  from the application of any provision of the Act and of these
  Regulations that is not necessary to give effect to this Part. For
  greater certainty, the exemption does not include section 6 of the
  Act.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Packaging and Labelling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846257 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Packaging and labelling

**[[342]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-342 .sectionLabel}** The packaging and
labelling of an organic product that is to be sent or conveyed from one
province to another may only be conducted by a person who holds a
certificate that is granted under section 345 or 348.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Percentage of Organic Products]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846260 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of percentage of organic
products

**[[343]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-343 .sectionLabel}** The percentage of the
contents of a multi-ingredient food commodity that are organic products
must be determined in accordance with CAN/CGSB-32.310.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Certification]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846263 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Organic Certification of Food Commodities]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846264 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for organic certification

- **[[344]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-344 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who wishes to obtain the organic certification of a food
  commodity must apply in writing to a certification body.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the food commodity;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement that sets out the substances and
    materials that are used to conduct any activities with respect to
    the food commodity and that describes the manner in which those
    substances and materials are used;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a document that sets out in detail the methods that
    are used by the applicant, or by a person acting on behalf of the
    applicant, to conduct any activities with respect to the food
    commodity and the control mechanisms that are in place to ensure
    that those methods meet the requirements that are set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310;
