  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case where a person packages and labels the
    food commodity on behalf of the applicant, the name of the person
    and a copy of the certificate referred to in subsection 348(2) that
    they hold; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity, a
    statement that sets out its composition and the percentage of its
    contents that are organic products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an application for the organic
  certification of a food commodity, the application must be filed
  within 12 months before the day on which the food commodity is
  expected to be sold or, in the case of an application for the organic
  certification of any of the following food commodities, at least 15
  months before that day:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} maple products;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} field crops or crops that are grown in greenhouses
    with an in-ground permanent soil system;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} wild crops within the meaning of CAN/CGSB-32.312;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} aquaculture products with a production cycle of
    more than 12 months.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certification

- **[[345]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-345 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  certification body must conduct an on-site verification and certify a
  food commodity as organic if it determines that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the substances and materials that are used to
    conduct activities in respect of the food commodity are set out and
    used in the manner described

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310 or CAN/CGSB-32.311;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the methods that are used by the applicant, or by a
    person acting on behalf of the applicant, to conduct activities in
    respect of the food commodity and the control mechanisms that are in
    place meet the requirements and comply with the general principles
    respecting organic production that are set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case where a person packages and labels the
    food commodity on behalf of the applicant, the person holds a
    certificate referred to in subsection 348(2);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity,
    at least 70% of its contents are organic products and its
    composition meets the requirements that are set out in
    CAN/CGSB-32.310; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the information submitted in the application is
    complete, truthful and not misleading.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The certification body must provide the applicant
  with a certificate that confirms the organic certification of the food
  commodity and that indicates the name of the food commodity, whether
  CAN/CGSB‑32.310 or CAN/CGSB‑32.312 is applicable, and, in the case of
  a multi-ingredient food commodity, whether at least 70% of its
  contents are organic products or whether at least 95% of its contents
  are organic products.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to provide information

- **[[346]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-346 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  holder of the certificate referred to in subsection 345(2) must
  provide to the certification body the information that is set out in
  subsection 344(2) once every 12‑month period beginning on the day on
  which the certificate is issued and no later than the day that is six
  months before the end of that period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}On-site verification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} After receiving the information from the certificate
  holder under subsection (1), the certification body must, no later
  than the end of the 12‑month period referred to in that subsection,
  conduct an on-site verification to determine whether the requirements
  that are set out in subsection 345(1) are met.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Certification of Packaging and Labelling]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846302 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for certification

- **[[347]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-347 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who wishes to package or label an organic product, other than a
  product in respect of which they hold a certificate granted under
  section 345, must apply in writing to a certification body for
  certification of the activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the type of organic product;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement that sets out the substances and
    materials that the applicant will use to package or label the
    organic product and that describes the manner in which those
    substances and materials will be used; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a document that sets out in detail the methods that
    the applicant will use to package or label the organic product and
    the control mechanisms that the applicant will put in place to
    ensure that those methods meet the requirements that are set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certification

- **[[348]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-348 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  certification body must conduct an on-site verification and certify
  the activity in respect of the packaging or labelling of an organic
  product if it determines that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the substances and materials that are used by the
    applicant for packaging or labelling are set out and used in the
    manner described

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310 or CAN/CGSB-32.311;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the methods that are used by the applicant for
    packaging or labelling and the control mechanisms that are in place
    meet the requirements and comply with the general principles
    respecting organic production that are set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The certification body must provide the applicant
  with a certificate that confirms the certification of the packaging or
  labelling of the organic product and that indicates the type of
  organic product to which it applies and the period of validity
  referred to in subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The certification of the packaging or labelling of an
  organic product is valid for 12 months beginning on the day on which
  it is granted under subsection (1).

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Suspension and Cancellation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846326 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

- **[[349]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-349
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  certification body must suspend a certification that is granted under
  section 345 or 348 if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the certificate does not comply with
    any provision of the Act or this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the substances or materials that are used are other
    than those that are set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310 or CAN/CGSB-32.311;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the food commodity comes into contact with a
    substance or material other than one that is set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310 or CAN/CGSB-32.311;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the substances or materials that are used are set
    out but are not used in the manner described,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310 or CAN/CGSB-32.311;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the methods that are used do not meet the
    requirements or do not comply with the general principles respecting
    organic production that are set out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product, in
      CAN/CGSB-32.312, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
      aquaculture product, in CAN/CGSB-32.310; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity,
    less than 70% of its contents are organic products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The certification body must not suspend a
  certification unless the holder of the certificate

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was provided with a written report that sets out
    the grounds for the suspension and the period within which
    corrective action must be taken in order to avoid the suspension;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} failed to take corrective action within that period
    or, if the certification body granted an extension at the written
    request of the holder, within any later period specified by the
    certification body.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The certification body may grant an extension of the
  period in which corrective action must be taken only once.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The certification body must notify the holder of the
  certificate in writing of the suspension and the date on which it
  takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of suspension

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The suspension of a certification must be lifted if
  the certification body determines that corrective action has been
  taken.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

- **[[350]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-350 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  certification body must cancel a certification if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the certificate fails to take
    corrective action within 30 days after the day on which the
    certification was suspended;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holder of the certificate was not in compliance
    with section 15 of the Act in respect of the application made under
    section 344 or 347 or at any time during the period of validity of
    the certification; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} while the certification is suspended,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a certification that was granted
      under section 345, the holder of the certificate

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} sends or conveys from one province to another a
        food commodity that is labelled with an expression that is
        referred to in subsection 353(1) or (2),

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} sends or conveys from one province to another a
        food commodity that has on it the product legend that is set out
        in Schedule 9 or a food commodity in connection with which that
        product legend is used,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} applies or attaches to a food commodity a label
        that bears an expression that is referred to in subsection
        353(1) or (2) or uses such an expression in the advertisement of
        a food commodity,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} applies the product legend that is set out in
        Schedule 9 to, or uses it in connection with, a food commodity,
        or

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} conducts any activity with respect to a food
        commodity that is identified in the certificate, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a certification that was granted
      under section 348, the holder of the certificate packages or
      labels an organic product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The certification body must not cancel a
  certification unless the holder of the certificate was notified in
  writing of the grounds for the cancellation and was provided with an
  opportunity to be heard in respect of the cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The certification body must notify the holder of the
  certificate in writing of the cancellation and the date on which it
  takes effect.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[General]{.HTitleText3} {#h-846371 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents

**[[351]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-351 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a
certificate must prepare, keep and maintain the documents that are set
out in the following standards, in accordance with those standards:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an aquaculture product,
  CAN/CGSB-32.312; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity other than an
  aquaculture product, CAN/CGSB-32.310.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Changes affecting certification

**[[352]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-352 .sectionLabel}** The holder of a
certificate must immediately notify the certification body in writing of
any change that could affect the certification and of any complaint that
they receive in relation to the organic integrity of the organic product
referred to in the certification.

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Labelling and Advertising]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846378 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expressions

- **[[353]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-353 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  expressions "organic" or "[biologique]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or
  "[organique]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", "organically grown" or "[cultivé
  biologiquement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", "organically raised" or
  "[élevé biologiquement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" and "organically
  produced" or "[produit biologiquement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" and any
  similar expressions, including abbreviations of, symbols for and
  phonetic renderings of those expressions, may be shown on the label or
  used in the advertisement of a food commodity that is sent or conveyed
  from one province to another if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the food commodity is an organic product; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity,
    at least 95% of its contents are organic products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expression "Organic ingredients"

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if a multi-ingredient food
  commodity is an organic product but less than 95% of its contents are
  organic products, it may be labelled with or advertised using the
  expression "organic ingredients" or "[d'ingrédients
  biologiques]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" if that expression is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} immediately preceded by the percentage of its
    contents that are organic products, rounded down to the nearest
    whole number; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in characters of the same height and prominence as
    the words, numbers, signs or symbols that indicate that percentage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multi-ingredient food commodities

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the list of ingredients that
  is shown on the label of a multi-ingredient food commodity that is not
  an organic product may indicate which of the ingredients are organic
  products.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

**[[354]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-354 .sectionLabel}** If an expression that
is referred to in subsection 353(1) or (2) is shown on the label of a
food commodity, the label must also bear

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity that is sent or
  conveyed from one province to another, the name of the certification
  body that certified the food commodity as organic;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity that is imported, the
  name of the certification body or the name of the entity accredited by
  a foreign state referred to in subparagraph 357(1)(a)(ii) or (iii)
  that certified the food commodity as organic;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity that
  is sent or conveyed from one province to another or that is imported,
  the organic contents that are identified as organic in its list of
  ingredients; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a food commodity that is imported and
  on whose label the product legend that is set out in Schedule 9 is
  applied, the expression "Product of" or "[produit de]{.otherLang
  lang="fr"}" immediately preceding the name of the foreign state of
  origin or the word "Imported" or "[importé]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" in
  close proximity to that product legend.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Official languages

- **[[355]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-355
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  expressions that are referred to in subsections 353(1) and (2) and
  paragraph 354(d) and the information that is referred to in paragraph
  354(c) must be shown on the label of a food commodity in both official
  languages.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Those expressions and that information may be shown
  on the label of a food commodity in only one official language if the
  food commodity is any of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a [[feed]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
    section 2 of the [Feeds Act](/eng/acts/F-9);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a [[seed]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
    section 2 of the [Seeds Act](/eng/acts/S-8); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a food, if subsection B.01.012(3), (7) or (11) of
    the [Food and Drug Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._870)
    allows the required information to be shown in only one official
    language.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Feed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(a), a reference to
  "livestock" in the definition [[feed]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
  section 2 of the [Feeds Act](/eng/acts/F-9) must be read to include
  livestock that is an aquaculture product.

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Interprovincial Trade and Import]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846406 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interprovincial trade

- **[[356]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-356 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  food commodity that is sent or conveyed from one province to another
  and that is labelled with or advertised using an expression that is
  referred to in subsection 353(1) or (2) must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be an organic product;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements for the use of that
    expression that are set out in subsection 353(1) or (2), as the case
    may be; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements of sections 354 and 355.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multi-ingredient food commodities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity that
  is not an organic product and that is sent or conveyed from one
  province to another, the list of ingredients that is shown on the
  label of the food commodity may indicate which of the ingredients are
  organic products.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Import

- **[[357]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-357 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  food commodity that is imported and that is labelled with or
  advertised using an expression that is referred to in subsection
  353(1) or (2) must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} meet one of the following requirements:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} be certified as organic under subsection 345(1),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} be imported from a foreign state with which the
      Agency has entered into an agreement or arrangement regarding the
      import and export of organic products and be certified as organic,
      in accordance with the agreement or the arrangement, by an entity
      that is accredited by that foreign state, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} be imported from a foreign state with which the
      Agency has not entered into such an agreement or arrangement, but
      be certified as organic by an entity that is accredited by a
      foreign state that is referred to in subparagraph (ii) with the
      certification being in accordance with the agreement or
      arrangement referred to in that subparagraph;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements for the use of that
    expression under subsection 353(1) or (2), as the case may be; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements of sections 354 and 355.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multi-ingredient food commodities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity that
  is not an organic product and that is imported, the list of
  ingredients that is shown on the label of the food commodity may
  indicate which of the ingredients are organic products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Demonstration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The person who imports the organic product must be
  able to demonstrate that the product meets one of the requirements of
  paragraph (1)(a) by providing, at the request of the Minister or an
  inspector, a certificate that confirms the organic certification of
  the product.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention period of certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The certificate referred to in subsection (3) must be
  kept for five years after the day on which the organic product is
  imported.

### [DIVISION 7]{.HLabel2}[Product Legend]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846430 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition [[inspection
mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in Act

**[[358]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-358 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of the
definition [[inspection mark]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the
Act, the product legend that is set out in Schedule 9 is prescribed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application or use of product legend

- **[[359]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-359 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person is authorized to apply the product legend that is set out in
  Schedule 9 to and use it in connection with a food commodity if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the food commodity is an organic product; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity,
    at least 95% of its contents are organic products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advertisement and sale

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person is authorized to advertise and sell a food
  commodity that has on it the product legend that is set out in
  Schedule 9, or a food commodity in connection with which that product
  legend is used, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the food commodity is an organic product; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a multi-ingredient food commodity,
    at least 95% of its contents are organic products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application or use --- other than food
  commodity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person is authorized, for advertisement or
  information purposes, to apply the product legend that is set out in
  Schedule 9 to, and use it in connection with, any item to which the
  Act applies other than a food commodity.

### [DIVISION 8]{.HLabel2}[Conformity Verification Bodies and Certification Bodies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-846444 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for accreditation

**[[360]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-360 .sectionLabel}** Any person who wishes
to be accredited as a certification body must apply for the
accreditation, in writing, to a conformity verification body and must
undergo an assessment, in accordance with ISO/IEC 17011, to verify

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} their compliance with ISO/IEC 17065;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} their knowledge with respect to organic certification
  and that of their employees and of any persons acting on their behalf;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the validity of their certification methodology and
  the validity of the results of that methodology.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accreditation

- **[[361]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-361 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the recommendation of a conformity verification body, accompanied by
  supporting documents, the Minister must accredit the applicant,
  provide them with an accreditation number and notify them in writing
  of the period of validity referred to in subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The accreditation of a certification body is valid
  for five years beginning on the day on which the Minister accredits
  the applicant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refusal

**[[362]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-362 .sectionLabel}** If the conformity
verification body refuses to recommend the applicant's accreditation, it
must send them a written notice by registered mail that states the
reasons for the decision and notifies the applicant of their right to
make a request, within 30 days after the day on which they receive the
notice, to the Minister for a review of the decision. The conformity
verification body must also send a copy of the notice to the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

**[[363]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-363 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must, at
the written request of the applicant, review the decision referred to in
section 362 and, if the Minister decides to confirm it, must provide a
copy of his or her decision with reasons to the applicant. If the
Minister does not confirm the decision, the Minister must accredit the
applicant, provide them with an accreditation number and notify them in
writing of the period of validity referred to in subsection 361(2).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

- **[[364]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-364
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), on the
  recommendation of a conformity verification body, the Minister must
  suspend the accreditation of a certification body if it does not
  comply with any provision of the Act, this Part or ISO/IEC 17065.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must not suspend an accreditation unless
  the certification body

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was provided with a written report that sets out
    the grounds for the suspension and the period within which
    corrective action must be taken in order to avoid the suspension;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} failed to take corrective action within that period
    or, if the conformity verification body granted an extension at the
    written request of the certification body, within any later period
    specified by the conformity verification body.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The conformity verification body may grant an
  extension of the period in which corrective action must be taken only
  once.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the certification body in
  writing of the suspension and the date on which it takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of lists

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The certification body must provide the Minister,
  within 15 days after the day on which the suspension takes effect,
  with a list of the holders of the certificates that it has granted and
  a list of pending applications for certification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of suspension

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The suspension of an accreditation must be lifted if
  the Minister determines, on the recommendation of the conformity
  verification body, that corrective action has been taken.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation

- **[[365]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-365 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  the recommendation of a conformity verification body, the Minister
  must cancel an accreditation if the certification body

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fails to take corrective action within 30 days
    after the day on which the accreditation was suspended;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} was not in compliance with section 15 of the Act in
    respect of the application made under section 360 or at any time
    during the period of validity of the accreditation; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} continues, while their accreditation is suspended,
    to accept applications for certification, to make determinations
    under subsection 345(1) or 348(1), to suspend certifications under
    subsection 349(1) or to cancel certifications under subsection
    350(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must not cancel an accreditation unless
  the certification body was notified in writing of the grounds for the
  cancellation and was provided with an opportunity to be heard in
  respect of the cancellation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must notify the certification body in
  writing of the cancellation and the date on which it takes effect.

## [PART 14]{.HLabel1}[Seizure and Detention]{.HTitleText1} {#h-846484 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Detention tag

**[[366]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-366 .sectionLabel}** An inspector who seizes
and detains a thing under section 25 of the Act must apply or attach a
detention tag to it which bears the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the expression "UNDER DETENTION" and the word
  "[RETENU]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", in capital letters;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the detention tag number;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the thing;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reason for the seizure and detention;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date of the seizure and detention; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the inspector's name and signature.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- removal of detention tag

**[[367]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-367 .sectionLabel}** It is prohibited for a
person to remove a detention tag from a thing that has been seized and
detained unless authorized to do so by an inspector.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of detention

- **[[368]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-368 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} As
  soon as feasible after a thing has been seized and detained, an
  inspector must provide a notice of detention to its owner or to the
  person having possession, care or control of it at the time of its
  seizure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of notice of detention

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice of detention must indicate that the thing
  was seized and detained under section 25 of the Act and set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the detention tag number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the thing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the reason for the seizure and detention;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date of the seizure and detention;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the place of the seizure and detention;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the inspector's name and signature; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a telephone number to call for further information
    about the seizure and detention.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Storage conditions

**[[369]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-369 .sectionLabel}** Anything that is seized
must be stored by the person to whom the notice of detention is
provided, under storage conditions that are appropriate for its
preservation and at the person's expense.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of release

**[[370]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-370 .sectionLabel}** If a thing is released
under section 30 of the Act, an inspector must provide a notice of
release to the person to whom the notice of detention was provided.

## [PART 15]{.HLabel1}[Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-846511 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}18‑month delay

- **[[371]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-371
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 5(2) and 7(2), section
  11, paragraphs 15(1)(a) and (b), subsections 18(3) and 19(2) and Parts
  4 and 5 do not apply, for the 18‑month period that begins on the day
  on which these Regulations come into force, in respect of foods other
  than dairy products, eggs, processed egg products, fish, fresh fruits
  or vegetables, processed fruit or vegetable products, honey, maple
  products and meat products.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional delay --- four employees or
  less

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 11 and Part 4 do not apply to any person who
  did not have more than four employees at any one time during the last
  12 months of the period referred to in subsection (1) in respect of
  foods other than dairy products, eggs, processed egg products, fish,
  fresh fruits or vegetables, processed fruit or vegetable products,
  honey, maple products and meat products for 12 months after the last
  day of the period referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional delay --- \$100,000 or less

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 11 and 45 to 85 do not apply to any person
  whose gross sales derived from food were \$100,000 or less for the
  last 12 months of the period referred to in subsection (1) in respect
  of foods other than dairy products, eggs, processed egg products,
  fish, fresh fruits or vegetables, processed fruit or vegetable
  products, honey, maple products and meat products for 12 months after
  the last day of the period referred to in that subsection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fresh fruits or vegetables --- 12-month
delay

**[[372]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-372 .sectionLabel}** For the 12-month period
that begins on the day on which these Regulations come into force,
section 11 and Part 4 do not apply in respect of fresh fruits or
vegetables and Part 5 does not apply in respect of fresh fruits or
vegetables to any person who grows or harvests fresh fruits or
vegetables unless the person is the holder of a licence to conduct an
activity in respect of those fresh fruits or vegetables.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Aquaculture products --- 24-month delay

- **[[373]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-373 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Part
  13 does not apply in respect of aquaculture products --- other than
  seaweed in respect of which a certification has been issued under
  section 13 of the [Organic Products Regulations,
  2009](/eng/regulations/SOR-2009-176) --- for the 24-month period that
  begins on the day on which these Regulations come into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} However, during that period, an application referred
  to in section 344 or 347 may be made in respect of any aquaculture
  product, including seaweed, and a certification in respect of the
  aquaculture product may be granted under section 345 or 348. If such a
  certification is granted, Part 13 applies in respect of any
  aquaculture product referred to in the certification.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Food commodities deemed to meet
applicable requirements

- **[[374]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-374 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  food commodity that, immediately before the day on which these
  Regulations come into force, meets the requirements that apply in
  respect of that food commodity under the [Consumer Packaging and
  Labelling Act](/eng/acts/C-38), the [Fish Inspection
  Act](/eng/acts/F-12), the [Food and Drugs Act](/eng/acts/F-27), the
  [Meat Inspection Act](/eng/acts/M-3.2) and the [Canada Agricultural
  Products Act](/eng/acts/C-0.4) is deemed, as of the day on which these
  Regulations come into force, to meet the requirements that apply under
  these Regulations with respect to the manufacturing, preparing,
  storing, packaging and labelling of that food commodity if those
  activities are conducted before that day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In paragraph 29(1)(a), subparagraph 30(a)(ii) and
  paragraph 39(c), a reference to "the Act" must be read to include a
  reference to any provisions of the [Consumer Packaging and Labelling
  Act](/eng/acts/C-38) that apply in respect of a food, the [Fish
  Inspection Act](/eng/acts/F-12), the [Meat Inspection
  Act](/eng/acts/M-3.2), and the [Canada Agricultural Products
  Act](/eng/acts/C-0.4), as they read immediately before the coming into
  force of these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspection legends

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of sections 179 to 183, subsection
  184(1), section 185, paragraphs 258(a), 282(1)(a) and 287(1)(a), the
  following are deemed to be an inspection legend that is set out in
  Figure 1 or 2 of Schedule 2, until December 13, 2022:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a meat product, an inspection legend
    set out in Figure 1, 2 or 3 of Schedule III to the [Meat Inspection
    Regulations, 1990](/eng/regulations/SOR-90-288), as they read
    immediately before the coming into force of force of these
    Regulations, or such an inspection legend without the registration
    number if it meets the conditions under subsection 93(3) of those
    Regulations, as they read immediately before the coming into force
    of force of these Regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a prepackaged processed egg product,
    the inspection legend set out in Schedule II to the [Processed Egg
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._290), as they read
    immediately before the coming into force of these Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of prepackaged fish, a mark or label
    with a designation that is referred to in section 28 of the [Fish
    Inspection Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._802), as they
    read immediately before the coming into force of these Regulations.

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1362262-1363055.html"
  role="button"}

Version](section-374-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 374"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificates, authorizations, exemptions,
certifications and accreditations

- **[[375]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-375 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  certificate, authorization, exemption, certification or accreditation
  that is set out in column 1 of the table to this section and that is
  valid immediately before the day on which these Regulations come into
  force is deemed to have been issued or obtained under the provision of
  the Act or these Regulations that is set out in column 2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), unless it is suspended or
  cancelled under these Regulations, the certificate, authorization,
  exemption, certification or accreditation remains valid until the end
  of the period for which it was issued or obtained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity --- seaweed

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An organic certification of seaweed granted under
  section 13 of the [Organic Products Regulations,
  2009](/eng/regulations/SOR-2009-176) ceases to be valid on the expiry
  of the 24-month period that begins on the day on which these
  Regulations come into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspensions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A certificate, authorization, exemption,
  certification or accreditation that was suspended before the day on
  which these Regulations come into force and that continues to be
  suspended on that day is deemed to be suspended under these
  Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applications for certificates,
  authorizations, exemptions, certifications or accreditations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An application for a certificate, authorization,
  exemption, certification or accreditation that is set out in column 1
  of the table to this section that was made before the day on which
  these Regulations come into force and in respect of which no decision
  has been made is deemed to be an application made under these
  Regulations for the certificate, authorization, exemption,
  certification or accreditation referred to in the provision of the Act
  or these Regulations that is set out in column 2.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 36(3) of Consumer Packaging
and Labelling Regulations

- **[[376]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-376
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every exemption referred to in
  subsection 36(3) of the Consumer Packaging and Labelling Regulations
  in respect of a test market product that is valid immediately before
  the day on which these Regulations come into force is deemed to be an
  exemption granted under subsection 174(2) of these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless cancelled under these Regulations, the
  exemption remains valid until the product ceases to be a test market
  product under subsection 6(6) of the [Consumer Packaging and Labelling
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._417), as they read
  immediately before the day on which these Regulations come into force.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for exemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A notice of intention that is filed under subsection
  36(3) of the Consumer Packaging and Labelling Regulations before the
  day on which these Regulations come into force and in respect of which
  no decision has been made before that day is deemed to be a request
  for an exemption under subsection 174(1) of these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Foreign systems deemed to be recognized

- **[[377]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-377 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following systems are deemed to be recognized under Part 7:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any inspection system of a foreign state for meat
    products that was, immediately before the day on which these
    Regulations come into force, approved for the purposes of the [Meat
    Inspection Act](/eng/acts/M-3.2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any inspection system of a foreign state for live
    or raw shellfish if that foreign state was, immediately before the
    day on which these Regulations come into force, authorized in
    respect of the import of those shellfish into Canada for the
    purposes of the [Fish Inspection Act](/eng/acts/F-12); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any systems of manufacturing, processing, treating,
    preserving, handling, testing, grading, coding, slaughtering,
    storing, packaging and labelling that are used in an establishment
    in a foreign state in relation to meat products if, immediately
    before the day on which these Regulations come into force, both the
    establishment and the inspection system of the foreign state for
    those meat products were approved for the purposes of the [Meat
    Inspection Act](/eng/acts/M-3.2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension and cancellation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the recognition of any system
  referred to in subsection (1) may be suspended or cancelled in
  accordance with Part 7.

## [SCHEDULE 2]{.scheduleLabel}[(Sections 179, subsections 180(1), (2) and (4), sections 181 to 183, subsection 184(1), section 185, paragraphs 258(a), 282(1)(a) and 287(1)(a) and subsection 374(3))]{.OriginatingRef}[Inspection Legends]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-846678 .scheduleLabel}
:::

### [Figure 1]{.HTitleText1} {#figure-1 .SchedHeadL1}

<figure>
<div class="imageGroupcenter">
<img src="/images/SOR-2018-108img/SOR-2018-108_ef001.jpg"
alt="Figure 1 is a circle, in the middle of which is a black maple leaf that has the word “Canada” written within it and the numbers “00” written below it." />
</div>
</figure>

### [Figure 2]{.HTitleText1} {#figure-2 .SchedHeadL1}

<figure>
<div class="imageGroupcenter">
<img src="/images/SOR-2018-108img/SOR-2018-108_ef002.jpg"
alt="Figure 2 is a circle, in the middle of which is a black maple leaf that has the word “Canada” written within it." />
</div>
</figure>

Figure 1 or Figure 2 is to appear with the circle outline, the maple
leaf excluding the word "Canada" and, in the case of Figure 1, the
number identifying the licence holder's establishment that replaces the
numbers "00", in the same, single colour that contrasts with the colour
of the background and the word "Canada" such that the Figure is easily
visible.

### [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Formula for Determining the Weighted Average Quantity of the Units in a Sample]{.HTitleText1} {#part-2formula-for-determining-the-weighted-average-quantity-of-the-units-in-a-sample .SchedHeadL1}

For the purposes of paragraph 200(4)(a) of these Regulations, the
formula for adjusting the sample mean to determine the weighted average
quantity of the units in the sample is as follows:

::: {#846795 .Schedule}
[X]{.Overbar}~a~ = [x]{.Overbar} + s(t ÷ √n)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_SOR-2018-108_e_hq_22139){#fn_SOR-2018-108_e_hq_22139-ID0EAAGFA}

where

[[X]{.Overbar}~a~]{.dfn}
:   is the weighted average quantity of the units in the sample;

[[x]{.Overbar}]{.dfn}
:   is the sample mean calculated as follows:
    ::: {#846799 .NestedFormula}
    [x]{.Overbar} = ∑x ÷ n

    where

    [∑x]{.dfn}
    :   is the sum of the net quantities of all units in the sample;
    :::

[s]{.dfn}
:   is the standard deviation of the sample, calculated as follows:
    ::: {#846803 .NestedFormula}
    s = √(∑(x -- [x]{.Overbar})^2^ ÷ (n -- 1))

    where

    [∑(x -- [x]{.Overbar})^2^]{.dfn}
    :   is the sum of the squared differences between the sample mean
        and the net quantity of each unit in the sample;
    :::

[t]{.dfn}
:   is the value determined in accordance with Part 3 for the selected
    sample size; and

[n]{.dfn}
:   is the number of units in the sample.
:::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#fn_SOR-2018-108_e_hq_22139-ID0EAAGFA){#fn_SOR-2018-108_e_hq_22139}The
  value of (t ÷ √n) may, instead of being calculated in accordance with
  this Part, be determined using the applicable value set out in Column
  3 of the table to Part 3.
  :::

<td class="noBorderRight">Value of t to be determined by linear
interpolation<a href="#fn_SOR-2018-108_e_hq_22119"
id="fn_SOR-2018-108_e_hq_22119-ID0EABAAACCA"><span
class="wb-invisible">Footnote for </span><sup>2</sup></a></td>

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^1^](#tablefoot_1-ID0EABABACCA){#tablefoot_1}If all
  units in a lot are selected to constitute a sample, zero must be used
  as the value of t and (t ÷ √n).
  :::

- :::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^2^](#fn_SOR-2018-108_e_hq_22119-ID0EABAAACCA){#fn_SOR-2018-108_e_hq_22119}The
  value of t will be determined by linear interpolation as follows:

  ::: Footnote
  t = a -- ((a -- b) × (c -- e) ÷ (c -- d))

  where

  [a]{.dfn}
  :   is the value of t for the closest sample size below the selected
      sample size;

  [b]{.dfn}
  :   is the value of t for the closest sample size above the selected
      sample size;

  [c]{.dfn}
  :   is the result of 120 divided by the closest sample size below the
      selected sample size;

  [d]{.dfn}
  :   is the result of 120 divided by the closest sample size above the
      selected sample size; and

  [e]{.dfn}
  :   is the result of 120 divided by the selected sample size.
  :::
  ::::

## [SCHEDULE 9]{.scheduleLabel}[(Clauses 350(1)(c)(i)(B) and (D), paragraph 354(d) and sections 358 and 359)]{.OriginatingRef}[Product Legend]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-846842 .scheduleLabel}
:::

<figure>
<div class="imageGroupcenter">
<img src="/images/SOR-2018-108img/SOR-2018-108_ef004.JPG"
alt="CFIA_ACIA-8737870-v2-Canada organic logo Black and White" />
</div>
</figure>

The product legend is to appear in black with a white background (as
illustrated), in black with a transparent background or in colour. If it
appears in colour, the background is white or transparent, the outer and
inner borders as well as the hills are green (Pantone no. 368), the
maple leaf is red (Pantone no. 186) and the lettering is black.

::: HistoricalNote
